link = "https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=d0f2d10e"
content = "OpenAI 的 Decisions API 已开放公测，可通过 Responses API 调用，官方称其决策速度最高可达 GPT-6 Luna 的 10 倍。接口接收文本或图像，并支持真假判定、从预设选项中选择并返回置信度，以及在指定数值范围内评分三类输出。开发者可将其用于请求路由，把任务分配给合适的模型、工具或智能体，也可用于图像分析与风险标记。该接口将通用模型中的快速判断能力独立出来，面向需要低延迟分类和分流的实时系统。"
purpose = "- 为多模型或多智能体系统提供低延迟的请求分类与路由组件。\n- 利用置信度分数设置转交、复核或拒绝阈值，降低错误决策风险。\n- 将简单判断交给专用接口，有望减少调用大型模型带来的延迟与成本。\n- 适合风险识别、内容分类、工具选择和实时图像判断等场景。"
notes = "标题：速度快达GPT-6 Luna的10倍，OpenAI开放决策API公测\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=d0f2d10e\n摘要：（信源：X/OpenAIDevs）OpenAI 的 Decisions API 开放公测了。官方说，通过 Responses API 调用，它做决定的速度最高能到 GPT-6 Luna 的 10 倍。…"


[[items]]
id = "eazo-personal-agent"
title = "Eazo：以智能界面为中心的 Personal Agent"
subtopic = "人工智能辅助编程"
topics = [
    "人工智能辅助编程",
    "智能体",
    "用户界面原生应用",
    "人工智能原生应用",
    "智能体执行框架",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "人工智能辅助编程",
    "智能体框架",
    "软件开发流程",
    "应用生成",
]
score = 2
source = "新智元 ASI爆点"
summary = "Eazo 提出“对话用于创建、界面用于使用”的 Personal Agent 路线，可从模糊自然语言需求生成并发布具备前后端、数据库和多模态内容的持久化定制 App。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = "https://eazo.ai/benchmark"
link = "https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=tg1a55c6"
content = "Eazo 认为纯对话框不适合高频、重复和视觉化任务，Personal Agent 应将一次性的自然语言需求转化为稳定、可复用和可分享的图形界面。平台支持一句话生成前端、后端、数据库、登录、支付及部署，并可在同一创作流程中生成图像、3D 场景、动画和音频。其底层 EazoTack 采用四层智能体架构，以 15 个基础工具和一个按需调用技能的元工具组织执行、协作与浏览器验证，并通过前缀稳定和上下文复用提高缓存命中率。官方公开的 Eazo Benchmark v3.0 包含 147 个创作任务、8 个能力维度；文中称 Eazo 得分 88.0，高于 Codex CLI 的 84.4，但这些结果仍需独立复核。文章还介绍了面向 Personal Agent 的托管基础设施 Qoni，用于提供身份、行动与记忆能力。"
purpose = "- 为个人化、短生命周期或应用商店无法覆盖的需求提供低成本 App 生成路径。\n- 启发智能体产品将自然语言用于需求表达和修改，将持久化 GUI 用于日常重复交互。\n- EazoTack 的少工具、技能按需加载和缓存优先设计，可作为降低智能体执行成本与延迟的工程参考。\n- 公开创作型智能体基准有助于从代码生成扩展到可运行、可发布、可复用的完整产品评估，但应关注题目分布及厂商自评偏差。"
notes = "标题：GPT-6凌晨上线智能界面！对话式Agent没戏了\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=tg1a55c6\n摘要：Eazo：以智能化 UI 为中心的 Personal Agent"

id = "langchain-deep-agents"

score = 2

summary = "LangChain 为 Deep Agents 引入技能绑定工具、固定技能和动态刷新机制，使企业智能体能够按需加载数千项技能并降低上下文与 Token 开销。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=c1752534"
content = "- Deep Agents 将工具绑定到具体技能，只有智能体读取相应技能时，关联工具才会进入上下文。\n- 用户通过 `/meeting-prep` 等显式指令可预先固定并加载技能，避免模型自行判断所带来的一次额外往返。\n- 长线程可将 `skills_metadata` 设为 `None`，在后续运行中重新发现新增或更新的技能，无需重启会话。\n- 新机制已进入最新版 `deepagents`，核心思路是以按需加载替代将整个企业技能注册表塞入上下文。"
purpose = "- 支持企业构建包含数千项能力的智能体技能库，同时控制上下文长度和 Token 成本。\n- 通过隐藏无关工具减少候选空间，有望降低工具误选并提高任务执行稳定性。\n- 固定技能适合意图明确、流程标准化的业务入口，可减少调用延迟。\n- 动态刷新机制使长期运行的智能体能够及时获取团队新增或更新的技能。"
notes = "标题：LangChain给Agent装「外挂」！几千个技能不炸内存\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=c1752534\n摘要：（信源：LangChain：Blog（RSS））LangChain 改了 Deep Agents 的技能机制，对准的是企业技能库越堆越大的问题。官方博客说，现在企业注册表里的技能动辄几千个，全塞进上下文，模型容易挑花眼…"

summary = "OpenAI的Mark Chen称其AI工具在一周内完成了相当于十年进展的Navier–Stokes相关数学研究，并计划将这类能力用于生命科学、下一代模型研发和对齐。"
paper = ""
code = ""

link = "https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=bcb09613"
content = "OpenAI的Mark Chen在X上转发Navier–Stokes相关成果，并称此次运行在一周内完成了相当于十年的数学进展。Navier–Stokes方程用于描述流体运动，是数学与物理研究中的核心方程组。Chen更关注这套AI研究工具的迁移价值，表示将进一步用于生命科学、下一代模型研发和模型对齐。该消息主要引自个人推文，原文未提供论文、技术报告或可复现实验细节来支撑量化说法。"
purpose = "- 展示AI可能显著压缩数学与科学研究周期，推动自动化科学研究工作流发展。\n- 表明数学研究能力可作为生命科学、基础模型研发与安全对齐的通用技术底座。\n- 体现“用AI研发更强AI”的闭环趋势，但其实际进展仍需论文、同行评审和复现实验验证。\n- 对相关宣称应重点审查基线定义、成果新颖性以及“十年进展”的量化依据。"
notes = "标题：1周跑完10年的数学进展，OpenAI下一步要拿它搞生命科学\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=bcb09613\n摘要：（信源：X/markchen90）人类十年的数学进展，AI 一周跑完了。说这话的是 OpenAI 的 Mark Chen。 他在 X 上转发了 OpenAI 分享 Navier-Stokes 相关突破的帖子…"


[[items]]
id = "gemini-3-flash-gpt-5-2"
title = "低价模型未必省钱：Gemini 3 Flash部分任务总成本反超GPT-5.2"
subtopic = "推理效率"
topics = [

]

]
score = 2
source = "新智元 ASI爆点"
summary = "斯坦福、微软等机构对6800余项任务的研究发现，低价模型可能因消耗更多思考Token而产生更高的任务总成本。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=4eb9ce41"
content = "研究覆盖6800余项任务，发现32%的场景中低价模型完成任务的实际成本反而更高，最大成本反转达到28倍。尽管Gemini 3 Flash的标价低约78%，其完成部分任务的总花费仍比GPT-5.2高22%。主要原因是复杂任务会让较便宜的模型消耗更多内部思考Token，而这些Token同样计费；剔除这部分后，成本反转现象减少约70%。因此，企业选择模型时不能只比较单位Token价格，还应衡量完成任务所需的总推理量和成功成本。"
purpose = "- 模型采购应采用端到端任务成本，而非输入、输出Token标价作为核心指标。\n- 可按任务复杂度动态路由模型，避免低价模型在困难任务上进行冗长且昂贵的推理。\n- 成本评测应同时记录思考Token、任务成功率、重试次数和完成时延。\n- 企业需要建立真实工作负载下的成本基准与预算监控机制，提高AI费用的可预测性。"
notes = "标题：32%的场景下便宜AI更费钱，买Gemini 3 Flash比GPT-5.2还贵\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=4eb9ce41\n摘要：（信源：www.implicator.ai）标价便宜 78% 的 Gemini 3 Flash，真把任务跑完，花的钱比 GPT-5.2 还多 22%。 这笔账来自斯坦福、微软等机构的一项研究。他们测了 6800…"


[[items]]
id = "claude-lean"
title = "Claude被曝以Lean证明渗流理论连续相变猜想"
subtopic = "形式化证明"

    "模型可靠性",
]
orgs = [
    "佐治亚理工学院",
    "Weizmann Institute of Science",
]
score = 2
source = "新智元 ASI爆点"
summary = "报道称Anthropic内部Claude模型借助Lean完成了渗流理论连续相变猜想关键不等式的形式化证明，但成果仍有待独立核验及人类可读版本解释。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12397"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=tg8728dd"
content = "报道称，Anthropic工程师提交了一份由Claude生成并经Lean检查的形式化证明，目标是解决渗流理论中临界点不存在无限连通簇的连续相变猜想。证明沿用了Gady Kozma与Shahaf Nitzan在2024年论文中建立的归约：只要证明一个特定代数不等式，即可覆盖此前困难的3至10维情形。相关代码据称包含数千行形式化推理，并已被其他数学家快速修改和泛化。由于文中未提供代码仓库地址，且原论文作者仍在等待人类可读证明，该突破及其“菲尔兹奖级”定位尚需独立审查。"
purpose = "- 展示大语言模型与Lean结合后处理高难度数学证明和构建可机检推理闭环的潜力。\n- 提示评估此类重大成果时，应同时核查代码仓库、依赖假设、Lean内核验收结果及同行复现情况。\n- 说明AI可能更适合承接繁复推导，而人类研究者可转向问题选择、证明解释与新工具提炼。\n- 可作为自动定理证明系统中“自然语言研究线索—关键引理归约—形式化验证”工作流的案例。"
notes = "标题：突发！Claude拿下概率论「圣杯」，AI跨过菲尔兹奖终点线\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=tg8728dd\n摘要：如果被验证，这或许是AI真正取得菲尔茨奖级别数学成果。"

content = "据报道，Anthropic 的一款未发布 Claude 模型用约一天半时间，将一项停滞 37 年的黎曼猜想相关下界从 41.6% 推进至 67.2%。相关结果能够进行形式化验证，使机器可以逐步核查推导过程。与此同时，GPT-5.2 Pro 被用于解决 1980 年遗留的 Erdős #281 问题，陶哲轩称其为 AI 解决开放问题的明确案例之一。下界取得突破并不意味着黎曼猜想已被证明，但显示前沿模型正开始参与可验证的开放数学研究。"

notes = "标题：AI要干翻黎曼猜想了？未发布Claude仅用一天半打破37年数学记录\n来源：新智元 ASI爆点（aiera.com.cn）\n链接：https://aiera.com.cn/asi-preview/asi-item.html?id=1a9e4745\n摘要：（信源：www.quantamagazine.org）黎曼猜想上一个卡了人类 37 年的下界，被 AI 用一天半打破了。 据福布斯报道，Anthropic 披露，出手的是一个还没发布的 Claude 模型。它把这个下界从…"


[[items]]
id = "agent-lightning-v1-0-harness-rl"
title = "微软开源 Agent Lightning v1.0：基于真实 Harness 的智能体 RL 框架"
subtopic = "智能体训练"
topics = [
    "智能体",
    "强化学习",
    "智能体执行框架",
    "系统优化",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "智能体训练",
    "强化学习",
    "智能体框架",
    "系统优化",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
]
orgs = [
    "微软亚洲研究院",
]
score = 3
source = "Microsoft Research 博客"
summary = "Agent Lightning v1.0 让部署使用的真实智能体 harness 直接参与强化学习，并以约 3,500 行代码实现可扩展、可复现的轻量训练控制平面。"
paper = ""
code = "https://github.com/microsoft/agent-lightning"
dataset = ""
link = "https://aihot.news/items/t9wypjd9cbb42ttuq7vrpp07l"
content = "Agent Lightning v1.0 提出 Harnessed Agentic RL 范式，通过兼容 OpenAI API 的 LLM 代理记录真实 harness 的模型调用，无需在训练框架中重写智能体及其环境循环。系统由 API Gateway、Rollout Controller 和基于 verl 的 Customized Trainer 组成，并原生支持将智能体作为 Kubernetes 作业运行。框架针对样本重新分词与合并、rollout 级优势计算、损失归一化及可变工作负载调度等问题提供专门设计，共置异步 RL 相比同步训练获得约 2 倍端到端加速。基于约 6,000 个 SWE-smith 样本进行训练后，Qwen3.5-9B 搭配 mini-SWE-agent 在 SWE-bench Verified 上的 Pass@1 从 41.8% 提升至 56.4%。"
purpose = "- 允许现有智能体框架直接接入强化学习，降低训练版本与部署版本行为不一致的风险。\n- 为编码智能体和通用工具型智能体提供轻量、开源且可复现的 RL 训练基础设施。\n- rollout 级优势计算与损失归一化表明，训练统计应以完整执行轨迹为单位，避免 harness 拆分样本造成权重偏差。\n- Kubernetes 与共置异步 RL 可复用既有基础设施，减少商业沙箱费用并提高 GPU 利用率。"
notes = "标题：Microsoft Research Asia 开源 Agent Lightning v1.0：3,500 行代码的真实 harness 智能体 RL 训练框架\n来源：AIHOT 每日精选（aihot.virxact.com）\n链接：https://aihot.news/items/t9wypjd9cbb42ttuq7vrpp07l\n摘要：（信源：microsoft.com）Microsoft Research Asia 提出 Harnessed Agentic RL 训练范式并开源重建的 Agent Lightning v1.0，让部署时使用的同一 agent harness 直接参与强化学习，无需在训练框架内重写 agent。"

id = "liquid-ai-d1-3b-d1-omni-600m"

summary = "Liquid AI 发布两款开放权重 d1 决策模型，以单次前向传播完成文本、视觉和音频决策，并面向低延迟端侧部署。"
paper = ""

link = "https://aihot.news/items/qawfhyw1v10hv75bld8ogbjgj"
content = "Liquid AI 发布开放权重模型 d1-3B 和实验性检查点 d1-omni-600M，二者不生成 token，而是通过单次前向传播直接输出决策。d1-3B 支持文本和图像，在 Decision Index v0.2.1 上取得 48.57 分，并以 82.9 的均分领先所比较的文本决策模型。d1-omni-600M 基于双向编码器并接入视觉、音频编码器，可处理文本与图像或文本与音频输入。d1-3B 在 RTX 4090 上单问题延迟为 8 毫秒，在 Jetson 系列设备上为 16至50 毫秒，并原生支持 llama.cpp。两款模型已上线 Hugging Face，可供下载、微调和部署。"
purpose = "- 为摄像头交互、内容审核、意图分类和具身导航等实时任务提供低延迟决策模型。\n- 单次前向传播替代自回归生成，可显著降低结构化决策任务的推理延迟与计算成本。\n- 公开的架构、训练方法及跨硬件延迟数据，可用于评估端侧部署中的精度、模型体积和响应速度取舍。\n- d1-omni-600M 展示了通过适配器、LoRA 和权重平均为小型编码器扩展视觉与音频能力的路径。"
notes = "标题：Liquid AI 发布开源决策模型 d1-3B 和 d1-omni-600M\n来源：AIHOT 每日精选（aihot.virxact.com）\n链接：https://aihot.news/items/qawfhyw1v10hv75bld8ogbjgj\n摘要：（信源：liquid.ai）Liquid AI 发布开源权重决策模型 d1-3B 和 d1-omni-600M，已在 Hugging Face 上提供。"


[[items]]
id = "nemotron-3-ioi-imo-2026"
title = "Nemotron 3 微调系统达 IOI 与 IMO 2026 金牌水平"
subtopic = "模型微调"
topics = [
    "模型训练",
    "推理",
    "强化学习",
    "数学人工智能",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
]
research = [
    "EvolveLRM",
    "LogicEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "模型微调",
    "强化学习",
    "数学推理",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
    "生成—验证—精炼",
]
orgs = [
    "NVIDIA",
]
score = 2
source = "Hugging Face 社区博客"
summary = "NVIDIA 通过领域数据微调、强化学习与生成—验证—精炼推理循环，使 Nemotron 3 系统在 IOI 2026 和 IMO 2026 测试中均超过金牌门槛。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aihot.news/items/r96kyuxfie0w9mup4206gzn29"
content = "Nemotron 团队以 Nemotron 3 为基座，结合监督微调、强化学习和反馈驱动推理，分别构建竞技编程与奥林匹克数学专家系统。IOI 系统基于约 2.2 万道题及合成推理轨迹训练，并通过 GenCorrect 迭代生成、评估和修正代码，最终取得 535.4/600 分；该结果来自非官方但采用真实竞赛约束的前瞻性测试。IMO 系统使用覆盖 15,818 道证明题的 414,890 个质量过滤样本进行 SFT，并在 9,597 道前沿难度题目上开展 RL，再组合通用、SFT 和 RL 检查点生成、批评与精炼证明，获得 30/42 分。结果表明，领域专业化模型与测试时搜索、验证和改进循环的协同设计，比单纯增加采样量更有效。"
purpose = "- 展示了用统一基座模型配合领域数据和标准后训练方法构建高水平专家模型的可复用路径。\n- 说明 SFT 与 RL 的收益取决于模型规模和任务类型，应针对不同检查点设计差异化训练方案。\n- 证明生成、批评、验证和精炼等测试时计算环节能够显著放大微调模型的能力。\n- 为竞技编程和自然语言数学证明系统提供模型、数据、基准及推理流水线的开放复现思路。"
notes = "标题：Nemotron 系列微调后达到 IOI 2026 与 IMO 2026 金牌水平\n来源：AIHOT 每日精选（aihot.virxact.com）\n链接：https://aihot.news/items/r96kyuxfie0w9mup4206gzn29\n摘要：（信源：huggingface.co）NVIDIA Nemotron 团队基于 Nemotron 3，用 SFT、RL 和反馈驱动推理分别构建了在 IOI 2026 与 IMO 2026 达到金牌水平的系统。"


[[items]]
id = "sierra-meta-personal-agent-protocol"
title = "Sierra 与 Meta 联合发布 Personal Agent Protocol 开放协议"
subtopic = "智能体框架"
topics = [
    "智能体",
    "智能体兼容性",
    "访问控制",
    "工具调用",
    "业务集成",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "智能体框架",
    "工具调用",
    "智能体兼容性",
    "访问控制",
    "业务集成",
]
orgs = [
    "Sierra",
    "Meta",
    "Genesys",
    "Shopify",
    "Stripe",
    "Walmart",
]
score = 2
source = "Sierra Blog"
summary = "Sierra、Meta 及多家企业联合开发 Personal Agent Protocol，以统一个人 AI 智能体通过网站、API 或企业智能体与企业安全交互的方式。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aihot.news/items/ktjti7hbm1gor33jpoc6ghili"
content = "Personal Agent Protocol 是由 Sierra、Meta、Genesys、Shopify、Stripe、Walmart 等共同开发的开放标准，旨在规范个人 AI 智能体与企业之间的交互。协议基于 OAuth 建立跨渠道会话，由消费者授予只读或写入权限，企业则控制智能体可执行的操作范围。个人智能体可通过企业网站、基于 MCP 或 OpenAPI 的 API，以及企业自有智能体三种路径完成任务。项目计划发布 v0.1 规范与参考实现，并进一步支持细粒度权限、推送通知和隐私友好的支付扩展。"
purpose = "- 为个人智能体接入企业服务提供统一接口，减少网页自动化、客服热线等低效交互。\n- 通过 OAuth、权限控制和企业侧操作参数增强身份验证、访问控制与行为可见性。\n- 有望促进 MCP、OpenAPI 与企业智能体之间的互操作，推动跨企业智能体生态标准化。\n- 可用于评估面向消费场景的智能体在账户操作、交易和通知任务中的安全边界。"
notes = "标题：Sierra 与 Meta 联合多家企业发布 Personal Agent Protocol 开放协议\n来源：AIHOT 每日精选（aihot.virxact.com）\n链接：https://aihot.news/items/ktjti7hbm1gor33jpoc6ghili\n摘要：（信源：sierra.ai）Sierra 与 Meta 联合 Genesys、Instinct、Rocket、Shopify、Stripe、Walmart 等伙伴宣布开发 Personal Agent Protocol，一个定义个人 AI 智能体如何与企业交互的开放标准，任何人都可实现。"


[[items]]
id = "promptarmor-webmcp"
title = "PromptArmor 解析 WebMCP 机制与主要安全风险"
subtopic = "工具调用"
topics = [
    "工具链安全",
    "智能体安全",
    "工具调用",
    "提示注入攻击",
    "访问控制",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "工具调用",
    "工具链安全",
    "提示注入攻击",
    "访问控制",
    "脚本劫持",
]
orgs = [
    "PromptArmor",
]
score = 2
source = "PromptArmor"
summary = "WebMCP 允许网站直接向浏览智能体暴露可执行工具，在提升网页操作效率的同时引入治理绕过、提示注入、脚本劫持、权限误标和操作漂移等安全风险。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aihot.news/items/aj31qyu01hx95ra5sq2f0k5a9"
content = "WebMCP 将名称、描述、输入参数及读写能力等工具定义嵌入网站，使智能体能够在用户已登录的会话中直接调用站点功能，而不必操作传统图形界面。截至 2026 年 10 月 6 日，相关注册表已记录 2,959 个采用 WebMCP 工具的站点，Chrome、Edge、Brave、ChatGPT、Cloudflare 和 Shopify 等均已提供不同程度的支持。其治理风险在于厂商可通过前端代码增改工具，绕过传统 OAuth 应用、MCP 服务器或集成审批流程；恶意站点还可借助工具描述和返回结果实施提示注入。由于工具由页面 JavaScript 支撑，跨站脚本、第三方脚本失陷和恶意扩展都可能注册或篡改工具，同时读写权限误标会破坏人工审批机制。WebMCP 工具与可见 UI 的运营漂移还可能让智能体执行偏离用户真实意图的操作。"
purpose = "- 将 WebMCP 工具清单、能力变更和供应商采用情况纳入持续资产发现与安全审计。\n- 不应仅信任工具声明的“只读”属性，应结合实际网络请求、状态变化和副作用进行验证。\n- 对写操作、敏感数据提交和高权限动作设置独立授权、人工确认及最小权限控制。\n- 将工具描述、调用结果及页面脚本视为不可信输入，部署提示注入检测、脚本完整性校验和执行隔离。\n- 持续比对人类 UI 与智能体工具的语义和行为，及时发现接口漂移及用户意图错配。"
notes = "标题：PromptArmor 解析 WebMCP 的机制与主要安全风险\n来源：AIHOT 每日精选（aihot.virxact.com）\n链接：https://aihot.news/items/aj31qyu01hx95ra5sq2f0k5a9\n摘要：（信源：promptarmor.com）PromptArmor 发布 WebMCP 威胁情报解读，介绍 WebMCP 如何让网站向浏览中的智能体提供工具，并指出治理、提示词注入、Web 安全、误配置和操作漂移等新增风险。"


[[items]]
id = "anthropic-cowork-vm"
title = "Anthropic Cowork 将模型推理与 VM 全面迁至云端"
subtopic = "系统优化"
topics = [
    "云端智能体",
    "智能体运行时",
    "执行隔离",
    "安全沙箱",
    "系统优化",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "系统优化",
    "云端智能体",
    "智能体运行时",
    "执行隔离",
    "安全沙箱",
]
orgs = [
    "Anthropic",
]
score = 2
source = "AIHOT 每日精选"
summary = "Anthropic 将 Cowork 的虚拟机由本地迁至云端，并以会话级独立沙盒和本地文件工具调用兼顾持续运行、设备资源开销与数据隔离。"
paper = ""
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = "https://aihot.news/items/oj7q14paghhkh66541sg0xdgy"
content = "- 旧版 Cowork 在云端执行模型推理，但在用户电脑上运行 Anthropic 提供的本地虚拟机，只有明确加入会话的数据会被映射至 VM。\n- 本地 VM 会带来磁盘占用、电池消耗和性能开销，用户合上笔记本后，正在执行的任务也会随之中断。\n- 新版将模型推理和虚拟机统一迁移到云端，每个会话使用独立沙盒，且不同会话之间不共享状态。\n- 当云端 VM 需要访问用户设备中的文件等资源时，由桌面应用代理相应工具调用，从而支持手机使用和后台持续运行。"
purpose = "- 展示云端智能体将推理与执行环境共置的架构，可降低端侧资源消耗并提升长程任务的持续性。\n- 会话级独立沙盒有助于限制状态串扰和数据泄露，可作为智能体执行隔离的设计参考。\n- 本地应用仅代理必须依赖设备资源的工具调用，体现了云端运行时与端侧权限边界分离的混合架构。\n- 云端迁移也意味着服务方需进一步处理运行成本、访问控制、隐私保护及故障恢复问题。"
notes = "标题：Anthropic Cowork 改为云端运行模型推理与 VM\n来源：AIHOT 每日精选（aihot.virxact.com）\n链接：https://aihot.news/items/oj7q14paghhkh66541sg0xdgy\n摘要：（信源：simonwillison.net）Anthropic 工程师 Felix Rieseberg 说明 Cowork 的新旧架构差异：旧版在云端推理、在用户电脑上运行本地 VM，磁盘、电池和性能开销大，合上笔记本工作就停止。新版把模型推理和 VM 都移到云端，每个会话有独立沙盒，桌面应用只负责文件访问等需要本机设备的工具调用，官方认为这解决了手机使用、保持工作运行和电池消耗等问题。"


[[items]]
id = "expdis-rlvr"
title = "ExpDis：解耦 RLVR 中的探索与优化"
subtopic = "策略优化"
topics = [
    "强化学习",
    "策略优化",
    "探索与利用",
    "模型蒸馏",
    "数学人工智能",
]
research = [
    "EvolveLRM",
]
keywords = [
    "策略优化",
    "强化学习",
    "探索与利用",
    "模型蒸馏",
    "轨迹筛选",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ExpDis 将带新颖性奖励的探索策略与学生策略优化解耦，通过筛选并蒸馏高质量正确轨迹，在不损害模型质量的前提下扩大推理策略探索。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出 Exploration-Distillation（ExpDis）框架，以解决 RLVR 中强化新颖性奖励可能导致模型质量下降的问题。框架训练一个或多个带新颖性奖励的探索策略，随后按照正确性与质量筛选其轨迹，并将有效探索结果蒸馏至独立的学生策略。学生策略不使用新颖性奖励进行训练，系统通过多轮交替执行探索与优化，实现更激进且风险可控的策略搜索。在七个数学推理基准和两个模型家族上，ExpDis 在相同墙钟时间预算下优于 DAPO，并展现出更好的 pass@$k$ 扩展趋势和正确解法多样性。"
purpose = "- 将高风险的新颖性探索与稳定的策略优化隔离，可避免稀疏可验证奖励造成难以恢复的能力退化。\n- 通过正确性和质量过滤后再蒸馏探索轨迹，为发现并内化新推理策略提供了可复用流程。\n- 多探索器设计有望进一步增加正确解法的多样性，并改善测试时多次采样的收益。\n- 评估强化学习方法时，除单次准确率外，还应关注 pass@$k$、解法多样性及同等墙钟预算下的效率。"
notes = "标题：Decoupling Exploration from Optimization in RLVR\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536\n摘要：Modern language models undergo reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) on top of already-trained checkpoints. A key promise of RLVR is the discovery of new reasoning strategies. In principle, a model can sample novel ideas absent from its prior training data. In practice, however, augmenting RLVR with strong novelty incentives has seen limited success and can degrade model quality. Because verifiable rewards supervise only a narrow slice of the model's knowledge and behavior, such degradations are difficult to recover from. Instead, we decouple exploration from optimization in a framework we call Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis). We train one or more explorer policies with a novelty bonus in the reward, filter their trajectories for correctness and quality, and distill them "


[[items]]
id = "sciexam-for-enso-ai"
title = "SciExam for ENSO：AI 智能体能否构建气候模型？"
subtopic = "科研评测"
topics = [
    "智能体评估",
    "自动化科学研究",
    "科学研究基准",
    "科学发现",
    "环境科学",
]
research = [
    "EvalEvolve",
    "ResearchEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "科研评测",
    "自动化科学研究",
    "评测基准",
    "气候建模",
    "智能体评测",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SciExam for ENSO 通过真实观测、冻结诊断与隐藏评分，在无已知标准答案的条件下评估语言模型智能体自主构建 ENSO 随机气候模型的能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10513"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SciExam for ENSO 要求智能体在六小时内根据真实观测数据，构建描述厄尔尼诺—南方涛动（ENSO）的低阶随机模型。智能体需自行编写诊断程序，诊断被冻结后只能以其反馈迭代模型，隐藏评分器则检验统计复现、未观测变量恢复和留出年份预测能力。在测试的 12 个智能体系统中，有 6 个构建的模型得分超过已发表模型，优势主要来自更好的重建与预测表现。较强模型的简化结构分别支持 ENSO 暖冷不对称性的两种竞争性解释，表明智能体生成的模型可能触及尚存争议的科学问题。"
purpose = "- 为没有标准答案的开放式科学建模任务提供基于真实预测与隐藏检验的评测范式。\n- 将诊断工具冻结并隔离隐藏测试，可降低模型评判偏差、答案记忆和针对评分器过拟合的影响。\n- 结果表明科研智能体已能构建具有竞争力且具科学解释意义的气候模型，可用于研究自动化科学发现能力。\n- 可借鉴该框架设计其他领域的“科学考试”，以留出观测、潜变量恢复和外推预测验证新模型的有效性。"
notes = "标题：SciExam for ENSO: Can AI Agents Build Climate Models?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10513\n摘要：Language-model agents are increasingly asked to carry out open-ended scientific research, yet their results are usually graded against a known answer, a rubric, or a language-model reviewer, none of which can tell whether a new scientific model is valid. The AI Science Exam for El Nino-Southern Oscillation (SciExam for ENSO) is a benchmark in which agents build low-order stochastic models of ENSO, the dominant mode of interannual climate variability, from real observations. Within a six-hour budget, agents process the observations, write their own diagnostics, which are then frozen, and develop a model using only these diagnostics as feedback. Hidden graders then test whether the model reproduces ENSO's statistics, recovers unobserved variables, and forecasts held-out years, and score a pu"


[[items]]
id = "jolt"
title = "JOLT：联合在策略学习与自教师训练"
subtopic = "在策略蒸馏"
topics = [
    "在策略蒸馏",
    "模型蒸馏",
    "模型训练",
    "强化学习",
    "长程任务",
]
research = [
    "EvolveLLM",
    "EvolveLRM",
]
keywords = [
    "在策略蒸馏",
    "自教师训练",
    "强化学习",
    "工具使用训练",
    "长程任务执行",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "JOLT 通过联合训练具备特权信息的自教师与无特权学生，使教师指导贴合学生当前能力，从而提升数学、编程和工具使用等任务的训练效率与表现。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，仅为自教师提供特权信息并不能保证蒸馏有效，因为教师可能采用学生无法复现的捷径，甚至导致学生性能下降。作者推导出教师局部蒸馏更新与学生奖励梯度同向的充要条件，强调教师既要有较强表现，也要适配学生当前能力。基于该分析，JOLT 使用结果奖励与朝向学生策略的 token 级 KL 正则来训练特权教师，并对无特权学生执行密集的在策略蒸馏。实验覆盖数学推理、代码、工具调用和终端操作，结果显示 JOLT 能提高训练效率与最终性能，叠加学生奖励后还可进一步增益。"
purpose = "- 为稀疏结果奖励下的困难长程任务提供更密集的 token 级训练信号。\n- 表明自教师的能力上限并非唯一关键，教师策略与学生当前行为的匹配程度同样决定蒸馏效果。\n- 可将 KL 正则化教师目标用于降低特权信息引发的捷径学习和师生分布错配。\n- 为自蒸馏、强化学习与工具型智能体训练的联合优化提供理论条件和实践方案。"
notes = "标题：A Good Self-Teacher Meets the Student Where They Are: Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447\n摘要：Reinforcement Learning (RL) from outcome rewards suffers from sparse supervision, particularly on difficult, long-horizon tasks where successful trajectories are rare and costly to generate. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers an attractive alternative by providing dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher along the student's own generations. Self-distillation methods further remove the need for a separate teacher model by conditioning the same policy on privileged information to serve as its own teacher. However, privileged conditioning alone does not guarantee that the resulting distillation update improves the student. Indeed, privileged information can lead the teacher to solve tasks through shortcuts unavailable to the student, producing supervision poorly matched to the s"


[[items]]
id = "cotrace"
title = "CoTrace：面向终端智能体的脚手架感知数据配方"
subtopic = "协同演化"
topics = [
    "智能体脚手架演化",
    "模型与框架协同优化",
    "数据策展",
    "模型训练",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
    "DataEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "协同演化",
    "脚手架演化",
    "数据策展",
    "课程学习",
    "能力迁移",
]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "CoTrace通过轨迹路由、来源匹配和课程刷新实现运行时脚手架与模型策略的协同演化，显著提升终端智能体性能与跨基准迁移稳定性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10426"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "CoTrace建立交替协同演化框架，通过组件级晋升决策解耦脚手架搜索与模型策略训练。该方法不再将搜索阶段产生的轨迹混入统一回放池，而是依据脚手架来源、运行时兼容性和验证结果进行轨迹路由与课程刷新。监督微调仅使用与采用运行时匹配的已验证轨迹，强化学习则使用新鲜在线交互，反复出现的执行失败可进一步指导脚手架合成。在 Tmax 晋升划分上，CoTrace将 Qwen3.5-9B 的已解决任务数由78项提升至88项，在线强化学习版本达到90项，并在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 SWE-bench Lite 上验证了运行时一致性对分布外迁移的重要性。"
purpose = "- 说明终端智能体的训练数据价值取决于生成轨迹时采用的脚手架，不能简单跨运行时混池复用。\n- 为脚手架搜索、轨迹筛选、监督微调和在线强化学习提供可解耦的协同演化流程。\n- 启示智能体训练应保留轨迹来源并验证训练与评测运行时的兼容性，以减少程序化执行失效。\n- 表明小规模、严格匹配脚手架的数据集可能比跨脚手架汇集的大型语料更高效。"
notes = "标题：CoTrace: Data Recipes for Training Terminal Agents with Harness-Model Co-Evolution\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10426\n摘要：Terminal-agent capability depends jointly on model weights and the runtime harness that formats prompts, binds tools, and handles error recovery. Existing harness-model co-evolution approaches improve both components, yet often treat trajectories produced during harness search as an undifferentiated replay buffer. This practice overlooks that a trajectory's value for model training depends on the harness under which it was generated. To systematically analyze this interface, we establish an alternating co-evolution framework that decouples harness search and policy training through component-wise promotion decisions. Within this framework, we introduce CoTrace, a harness-aware data recipe that explicitly governs trajectory routing, provenance matching, and curriculum refresh. Under CoTrace"

score = 3

summary = "Kernaut 让编程智能体在构造契约约束下开放式生成有效核函数，并结合质量多样性归档与新颖性筛选，发现可泛化、可解释且可由人类继续改进的归纳偏置。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10394"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "固定核语法虽能保证有效性，却将搜索空间限制在预设基础核与算子的组合中；不受约束的程序生成则可能产生在尺度或维度变化后失效的核函数，实验中的失效率达到 22%–58%。Kernaut 将核设计视为开放式模型发现，由编程智能体直接编写核程序，并通过构造契约确保所有被接受的候选核均满足有效性要求。系统利用质量多样性归档保留行为不同的高性能核，并通过新颖性筛选推动智能体探索功能上全新的候选方案。发现的核函数可泛化至未见过的黑盒优化与酶动力学任务，且作为可解释程序还能由研究人员手工改进，进一步将预测误差降低 5.7%、优化遗憾降低 7.8%。"
purpose = "- 为自动模型设计提供兼顾开放搜索空间与结构有效性保证的新范式。\n- 表明构造契约可以替代固定语法，在不压缩程序表达能力的前提下过滤无效生成结果。\n- 质量多样性归档与新颖性筛选可用于避免搜索收敛到单一结构，并积累具有不同行为的可复用归纳偏置。\n- 可解释的程序化模型便于人类研究者审查和精修，为人机协同科学发现提供实践路径。"
notes = "标题：Kernel Autoresearch for Open-Ended Model Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10394\n摘要：Kernels encode the inductive bias of a wide range of machine learning models, yet automated kernel design faces a fundamental dilemma. A fixed grammar of base kernels and operators guarantees validity but limits the search to structures expressible by those building blocks. Conversely, unrestricted programs remove this limitation but no longer guarantee validity. In our stress tests, 22-58% of LLM-generated kernels that pass numerical checks on random inputs fail when evaluated at different scales or dimensions. We propose Kernel Autoresearch (Kernaut), which treats kernel design as open-ended model discovery. Coding agents write kernels as programs, while construction contracts ensure that every accepted kernel is valid. A quality-diversity archive retains high-performing kernels with dis"

content = "研究覆盖2017—2025年得州的5,601,890起交通事故，联合分析警方记录中的结构化字段与事故叙述文本。Kumo Tabular读取全部事故的编码字段，Jev读取两个概率样本中的叙述，并通过人工判断重新校准预测概率。多波次“先预测、后去偏”估计器将三层信息整合为总体估计，第二层按已知概率抽取的人工样本则用于设计性验证。结果显示，叙述文本揭示的涉伤事故因素明显多于编码字段；Kumo Tabular生成的复核列表发现真实字段冲突的效率是随机阅读的7至58倍，且表格读取速度约为TabPFN 3.5的15倍。"
purpose = "- 为大规模行政记录中结构化字段遗漏的信息提供可校准、可验证的总体估计方案。\n- 展示表格基础模型、文本模型、概率抽样与人工复核结合后，可同时兼顾处理规模、统计有效性和审查成本。\n- 可用于道路安全部门定位高价值复核样本、绘制编码冲突分布，并据此规划人工标注预算。\n- “预测后去偏”与分层人工校准方法可迁移到医疗、保险和公共治理等含结构化记录与自由文本的场景。"
notes = "标题：Estimating Uncoded Crash Factors with Tabular Foundation and System One Models: Kumo Tabular and Jev\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10321\n摘要：Road safety programs count the coded fields of police crash records, while the officer's narrative, which often records factors the fields omit, is rarely read. A safety office thus cannot tell how much its counts miss or where to review. This study develops and evaluates a system that joins both views of the 5,601,890 Texas crashes from 2017 to 2025 into population estimates with stated validity. An in-context tabular foundation model, Kumo Tabular, reads the coded record of every crash, a calibrated System One model, Jev, reads the narratives of two probability samples, and human judgments recalibrate its probabilities. A multiwave predict-then-debias estimator joins the three tiers, and a second human tier drawn with recorded probabilities checks the estimates by design. For hydroplanin"

This design enables agents to investigate individual interventions and jointly optimize data, training settings, and execution harnesses within the same environment. We define RSI-Index as the "

id = "item-99"
title = "个性化记忆生成前失效：陈旧、错归因与延迟"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文以Personal Fact Memory为参照，直接评测语言智能体在生成前出现的记忆陈旧、身份错归因、更新合并错误及服务延迟问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10265"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，仅以最终答案是否正确评估个性化记忆，会掩盖生成前的陈旧值暴露、人物错配和超时等故障。受控修订实验中，只提供正确键槽的当前有效值可消除观测到的陈旧暴露，而不执行更新解析时，70.3%的提示会包含已被取代的值。真正困难的是将修订内容分配到正确槽位：漏合并会保留旧值，误合并则会静默删除当前值；四种LLM键分配器虽有更高召回率，却导致更低的干净检索率，且LongMemEval上的开放域合并召回率最高仅为0.062。实体后验可减少受控数据中的同名错归因，但无法区分LoCoMo中姓名完全相同的说话者；此外，生成模型会复现提示中的记忆错误，而端到端延迟主要由提示预填充而非检索排序主导。"
purpose = "- 将智能体记忆评测前移至生成之前，分别测量存储状态有效性、身份解析、拒答能力和服务延迟。\n- 记忆更新系统应显式维护键槽与唯一身份，并区分漏合并和误合并两类风险，避免仅优化检索召回率。\n- 评测检索器时应确保其共享相同的有效记忆库和参与者信息，以免把记忆构建缺陷误判为排序问题。\n- 系统优化不应只关注检索耗时，还需重点降低长记忆块引起的提示预填充成本。"
notes = "标题：Stale, Misattributed, or Late: Where Personal Memory Fails Before Generation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10265\n摘要：Personal memory for language agents is usually judged by whether the final an- swer is correct. That score hides errors that arise before generation: the memory block may contain an obsolete value, a fact about the wrong person, or no use- ful fact before the serving deadline. We measure these failures directly. Using Personal Fact Memory (PFM) as a reference layer, we find that temporal validity is primarily a property of memory construction in our setting. On a controlled revision benchmark, serving only the active value of each correctly keyed slot eliminates observed stale exposure; without update resolution, 70.3% of prompts expose a superseded value. Once retrievers share the same active store and par- ticipant information, participant-aware BM25 is equivalent to the reference ranker"

summary = "TestGRAD通过差异化损失、完整CRUD测试更新与失败模式动量，自动演化可区分SWE-Agent候选补丁的仓库测试套件，在SWE-bench Verified上将四智能体集成的Pass@1提升至84.2%。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10242"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "TestGRAD将SWE-Agent集成中的补丁选择建模为测试空间优化，持续演化可执行测试套件，直至候选补丁呈现可区分的行为。其差异化损失以候选补丁之间的执行差异作为明确优化目标，避免生成大量无判别力的测试。完整CRUD梯度支持读取既有测试设施、创建新测试、更新过时断言及删除失效测试，而非仅进行简单增删。失败模式动量从历史记忆中提炼高频失败序列，减少重复探索并将失败历史上下文压缩超过100倍；在SWE-bench Verified上，四智能体集成取得84.2% Pass@1，较最强基线提升3.6个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为多编程智能体生成的候选补丁提供以实际执行行为为依据的自动选择机制。\n- 表明测试套件可被视为可迭代优化的评估脚手架，而不是一次性生成的静态资产。\n- 可将失败轨迹压缩为搜索动量，在降低上下文成本的同时规避反复生成无判别力测试。\n- 完整CRUD更新机制为仓库级测试维护、断言修复和测试基础设施演化提供了通用思路。"
notes = "标题：TestGRAD: Evolving Test Suites via Failure Pattern Momentum for SWE-Agent Ensemble\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10242\n摘要：SWE-agent ensembles improve issue resolution by combining candidate patches from different agents with complementary strengths. The central problem is therefore test-based selection: generate tests, execute candidate patches, and identify the best patch. We formulate this process as test-space optimization: evolving an executable repository test suite until it distinguishes competing patches. Existing test-generation methods are limited optimizers. They usually lack an explicit loss for ensemble selection, optimize through incomplete directions that mostly create new tests or delete old ones, and perform one-off generation without feedback from repeated failures. Inspired by gradient descent with momentum, we introduce TestGRAD, a framework for automatic test optimization. TestGRAD centers"

code = ""

content = "VideoEvolve面向长视频理解中“检索动态适配、记忆内容固定”的失配问题，从低帧率视频概览出发，按需补充关键视觉记忆。框架由选择性增强记忆的 Memory Evolver 和在演化记忆上自适应搜索的 Retrieval Evolver 组成，并通过交替式智能体强化学习更新一方、冻结另一方。瓶颈感知演化反馈（BEF）判断当前限制来自记忆还是检索，将优化资源导向较弱环节。能力感知演化反馈（CEF）则引导训练关注尚未成熟但可学习的视频能力，避免记忆模块过度适配固定训练问题。多项长视频理解基准实验验证了该协同演化机制的有效性。"
purpose = "- 为长视频系统提供记忆写入与信息检索联合优化的方法，减少关键细节遗漏后难以恢复的问题。\n- 展示如何利用下游推理经验持续更新多模态系统能力，为经验驱动的自改进框架提供参考。\n- BEF 的瓶颈定位机制可用于动态分配训练资源，避免只优化检索或只扩充记忆造成收益受限。\n- CEF 可缓解针对固定问题集的过拟合，为长期记忆的能力泛化与课程设计提供启示。"
notes = "标题：VideoEvolve: Co-Evolving Memory and Retrieval for Long Video Understanding\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10183\n摘要：Long video understanding increasingly relies on external memory to organize massive visual streams into compact representations. However, most memory-based methods dynamically adapt how information is retrieved for different questions, while largely fixing what is remembered. This mismatch makes missing details costly to recover, whereas stored information is valuable only when it can be reliably retrieved. To address this issue, we propose VideoEvolve, a novel self-evolving framework that jointly evolves memory and retrieval for long video understanding. Specifically, starting from a coarse low-frame-rate overview, VideoEvolve couples a Memory Evolver for selective memory augmentation with a Retrieval Evolver for adaptive retrieval over the evolving memory. We then co-evolve the two Evolv"

id = "uniskill-actor"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "UniSkill通过对比式动作反馈，在无需为每个技能提案执行额外 rollout 的情况下，实现与持续演化 Actor 对齐的技能库编辑与稳定联合训练。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10164"
code = "https://github.com/LimOkii/UniSKill"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "UniSkill使用共享策略与环境交互，并根据轨迹提出 Add、Update 或 No Edit 三类技能库操作，使策略与技能库协同演化。Actor依据环境奖励学习，而技能提案通过对比式动作反馈进行优化，该反馈衡量替换检索技能后，当前 Actor 在同任务成功与失败轨迹上的动作对数似然差变化。该方法复用已有轨迹评估技能与 Actor 的匹配程度，避免为每个候选技能执行昂贵的额外 rollout。为防止低质量提案内容抑制本应合理的编辑操作，作者加入技能编辑支持正则化以维持探索；最终在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上分别达到 98.4% 与 84.7% 的成功率。"
purpose = "- 为持续学习智能体提供低成本的技能提案评估机制，减少逐提案环境交互带来的训练开销。\n- 将技能价值与 Actor 自身能力提升解耦，有助于避免把策略进步错误归因于技能库编辑。\n- 对比式动作反馈可作为技能库与当前策略对齐的训练信号，适用于策略持续变化的联合训练场景。\n- 技能编辑支持正则化表明，技能内容质量与编辑操作选择应分别约束，以兼顾训练稳定性和探索能力。"
notes = "标题：UniSkill: Learning Actor-Aligned Skill Proposals for an Evolving Policy\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10164\n摘要：Large language model agents can improve across tasks by retaining reusable skills distilled from prior interactions. Recent work jointly optimizes task execution and skill extraction, enabling the policy and skillbank to co-evolve. However, as the actor continues learning, rewarding skill proposals through their reuse in subsequent training steps may conflate skill benefits with actor improvement, while directly testing each proposed skill requires costly additional actor rollouts. In this paper, we introduce UniSkill, which uses a shared policy to interact with the environment and propose skillbank edits (Add, Update, or No Edit) from the resulting trajectories. Specifically, the actor learns from environment rewards, while contrastive action feedback guides skill proposal learning. This "

content = "- 作者组织一名教师和七名学生开展八次智能体算法探索试验，部分参与者缺乏论文发表经验，研究主题也超出团队原有专长。\n- 在人工较少干预算法的情况下，智能体能够构造数学方案、分析现有工具并实现改进，但部分项目未达到实际应用目标。\n- 团队还利用 AI 收集、分类和分析了 2022—2026 年四个 EDA 会议及两种期刊的 8,420 篇论文；在 2,380 篇核心论文中，97.7% 被判定为可在计算环境中闭环执行。\n- 结果表明，EDA 为普及化的智能体算法研究提供了良好的可执行环境，但成果生成速度超过审查速度后，验证标准、贡献认定和发表机制需要重新设计。"
purpose = "- 展示科研智能体如何在具备明确目标、工具链和可执行反馈的 EDA 领域降低算法探索成本。\n- 启发工具开发者利用智能体试验过去因时间或人力不足而搁置的研究设想。\n- 提醒研究社区将投入重点从成果生成转向严格验证、复现检查和真实效用评估。\n- 为研究成果廉价化背景下的同行评审、论文贡献认定及会议期刊评价机制改革提供案例依据。"
notes = "标题：When Algorithmic Exploration Becomes Cheap: A Case Study of Agentic Research in EDA\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10129\n摘要：As EDA researchers, we conducted eight deliberate trials of agentic algorithm exploration, selecting several topics outside our areas of depth. One faculty member and seven students participated, including students without publication experience. With limited intervention in the algorithms, agents developed mathematical constructions, analyzed existing tools, and implemented improvements; some efforts fell short of their practical goals. We also used AI to collect, classify, and analyze 8,420 papers from four EDA conferences and two journals over 2022-2026. Among 2,380 primary-core papers, we classified 97.7% from titles and abstracts as computationally closed, including work on new formulations. Together, these observations suggest that much of EDA offers an executable environment for inc"

summary = "RewardWeaver通过基于失败归因的动态过程奖励分配与受控能力扩展，改善长程语言智能体训练中的稀疏反馈和信用分配问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10120"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "RewardWeaver维护一个经过验证且语义稳定的能力空间，并将策略优化、任务评估、失败归因和奖励适配连接为闭环。每个训练阶段结束后，框架会对低回报轨迹进行基于任务结果的反向归因，聚合反复出现且可由策略控制的能力瓶颈。系统据此为下一阶段动态选择相应的过程奖励；若既有能力空间无法覆盖反复出现的失败，则通过独立、受控的流程扩展能力及其 Rubric。该方法在 SOTOPIA、Amazon HistoryPrice 和新构建的销售基准上覆盖社交互动、双边议价及领域销售任务，并取得新的 SOTA 结果。"
purpose = "- 为长程交互训练提供比固定过程奖励更具针对性的动态奖励分配机制。\n- 利用结果驱动的反向失败归因识别当前真正限制任务成功的能力瓶颈，缓解信用分配困难。\n- 通过固定已接纳 Rubric 的语义并受控扩展能力空间，降低奖励定义漂移及训练目标失稳风险。\n- 可用于构建随策略能力演进而同步调整监督重点的语言智能体强化学习闭环。"
notes = "标题：RewardWeaver: Long-Horizon Interactive Learning for Language Agents via Self-Evolving Reward Adaptation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10120\n摘要：Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has driven substantial progress in domains where task outcomes can be reliably evaluated, but long-horizon interaction remains challenging due to sparse terminal feedback and difficult credit assignment. Process rewards provide denser supervision, yet the capabilities most relevant for training can change as the policy evolves: a behavior that is easy to evaluate or frequently deficient need not be the bottleneck currently limiting task success. We introduce RewardWeaver, a self-evolving reward adaptation framework for language agents in long-horizon interaction. RewardWeaver maintains a validated capability space in which the semantics of admitted Rubrics remain fixed, and closes the loop between policy optimization, task evaluation, f"

summary = "SkillSandbox为每项智能体技能动态合成相关但新颖的可执行场景，通过对比使用与不使用技能的执行结果，验证其可复用性并决定是否纳入技能库。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10088"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "SkillSandbox旨在识别自进化智能体从经验中提炼出的错误流程或不可迁移技能。框架由Proposer、Builder和Verifier组成，分别负责定义保留与变化条件、构建可执行场景，以及对比启用和禁用目标技能时的执行表现。Verifier从可执行性、效用和效率三个维度给出Keep或Reject结论，以控制技能进入技能库。该方法在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用三种模型进行测试，持续提升了下游任务表现和执行效率。"
purpose = "- 为自进化智能体的技能库提供准入验证机制，防止错误或过拟合经验被长期复用。\n- 通过按技能动态生成测试任务与环境，解决既有基准难以触发目标技能的问题。\n- 可将有无技能的对照执行作为技能效用与迁移性的直接因果证据。\n- 为技能库治理、持续演化和执行效率优化提供可扩展的评测框架。"
notes = "标题：SkillSandbox: Skill Verification via Dynamic Scenario Synthesis\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10088\n摘要：Self-evolving agents distill task-solving experience into skills for future reuse, but these skills can encode incorrect procedures or non-transferable knowledge. It is therefore critical to verify each skill's reusability: whether its guidance remains useful beyond the experience from which it was distilled. Such verification requires observing how a skill affects execution in new tasks, yet existing tasks may not expose the situations where the target skill can actually be exercised. To construct such situations, we propose SkillSandbox, a framework that dynamically synthesizes a task and its environment for each skill that are skill-relevant yet novel. A Proposer specifies the conditions to preserve and the source-specific details to vary, a Builder constructs an executable scenario, an"

id = "knowledge-weaver"

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Knowledge Weaver利用词元级互信息反馈与边际成功奖励，从智能体轨迹中提炼低冗余、可复用且能增量提升任务表现的知识。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10042"
code = "https://github.com/LaoKuiZe/Knowledge-Weaver"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Knowledge Weaver是一个强化学习框架，训练语言模型将智能体交互轨迹整理为可复用的知识库条目。其将词元级互信息启发的反馈与边际成功奖励结合，鼓励新条目保留经验中的独特信息，并在加入现有知识库后产生额外任务收益。独立成功奖励则确保单个知识条目本身也具备实用性，避免知识积累只追求差异而损害可用性。在ALFWorld和WebShop上，检索10条知识时平均成功率分别达到54.0%和42.0%，较GRPO高16.9和18.7个百分点，并在冻结执行模型时超过多种提示式、既有及人工编写的知识库。"
purpose = "- 为持续扩展的智能体知识库提供兼顾去冗余、信息增量与任务效用的训练目标。\n- 表明仅用独立任务成功率训练知识策展器，容易重复强化已有的通用经验；应显式衡量新知识相对于现有知识库的边际贡献。\n- 可用于长期运行智能体的经验沉淀、轨迹蒸馏和知识库自动维护，并支持在不更新执行模型的情况下持续改善任务表现。\n- 词元级互信息反馈可作为知识条目差异性和新增信息量的代理信号，为记忆演化与经验复用研究提供新思路。"
notes = "标题：Learning to Accumulate Knowledge with Mutual Information\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10042\n摘要：Large language model (LLM) agents can improve their performance by reusing knowledge distilled from past interactions. However, curating new experiences into a knowledge bank that becomes more useful as it grows remains challenging. Effective knowledge accumulation should limit redundant overlap among entries and ensure that new knowledge contributes beyond what the bank already provides. Yet training a curator with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on standalone task success can reinforce general guidance even when it duplicates existing knowledge. Therefore, we propose Knowledge Weaver, a reinforcement learning framework that trains a language model to curate reusable knowledge from agent trajectories. We couple feedback inspired by token-wise mutual information (MI) with margina"

id = "anchorloop"
title = "AnchorLoop：历史锚点驱动的工具智能体自进化训练"

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "AnchorLoop复用上一轮冻结的Executor作为历史锚点，为执行器和课程智能体提供跨版本参照，在无外部任务与答案监督下持续提升工具智能体的推理能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09856"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "AnchorLoop在自进化训练循环中引入上一迭代Executor的冻结副本，以缓解当前模型完全共识时组相对优势消失的问题。对于Executor，历史锚点通过比较当前与历史多数答案，构造跨参考优势信号；对于Curriculum Agent，则根据采样结果的多数一致性差异辅助选择仍具学习价值的任务。由于课程训练期间Executor与锚点参数相同，该比较主要是任务选择的代理信号，不能直接证明跨版本进步或答案正确性。在13个推理基准上，该方法相较Agent0将数学推理和通用推理分别提升2.5%与2.8%，并在后期迭代中维持更高的有效优势方差。"
purpose = "- 将历史模型快照作为轻量参照，可缓解自训练中因输出完全一致而导致奖励信号退化的问题。\n- 同一历史锚点可同时服务于策略优化和课程任务筛选，形成更稳定的闭环自进化机制。\n- 该方法不依赖外部任务或答案监督，适合用于降低自主智能体持续训练的数据与标注成本。\n- 一致性差异并不等同于正确性或真实能力提升，应用时仍需结合独立验证集或外部验证器。"
notes = "标题：Self-Evolve With a Reference:Anchored Training of Tool-Integrated Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09856\n摘要：Self-evolving tool-integrated agents learn from tasks and feedback generated within their own training loop. A Curriculum Agent generates tasks, while an Executor Agent learns from self-consistency signals through reinforcement learning. However, relying solely on the current Executor for feedback has two limitations: group-relative advantages vanish under full consensus, while uncertainty-based curriculum rewards favor disagreement without showing whether the generated tasks support further learning. These limitations motivate an additional reference beyond the current Executor. We propose \\textit{AnchorLoop}, which introduces a frozen copy of the previous iteration's Executor as a historical reference and reuses it on both sides of the training loop. For the Executor, the anchor provides"


[[items]]
id = "item-108"
title = "灾难性遗忘集中于低频词元的输出嵌入"
subtopic = "持续学习"
topics = [
    "持续学习",
    "灾难性遗忘",

]
score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，持续预训练与微调中的灾难性遗忘主要源于新语料未覆盖词元的输出嵌入漂移，并可通过仅增大输出投影层的 Adam epsilon 显著缓解。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09835"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究通过最高 1.4B 参数、五种设置下的系统性参数冻结实验，将灾难性遗忘定位到新语料中罕见或缺失词元的输出嵌入，而模型主体中相同 `sqrt(v̂)` 区间的参数基本不参与遗忘。其机制是缺失词元持续接收单向 softmax 梯度，并被 Adam 的二阶矩归一化放大为显著更新，因此仅凭固定基础模型上的词元计数即可在重训练前评估遗忘风险。作者提出只提高输出投影层的 Adam epsilon，在覆盖 160M 至 12B 参数、四个模型家族的稳定实验中消除了 39.4% 至 67.9% 的遗忘，且未损害目标任务学习。该方法还能与数据回放叠加，并缓解释放输出头的 LoRA 所造成的遗忘激增；训练后再编辑漂移嵌入行仅能恢复不足 5%，说明干预必须发生在训练期间。"
purpose = "- 为缺少原始回放数据的持续预训练和领域微调提供低成本、单行优化器级遗忘防御方案。\n- 可在训练前依据新语料的词表覆盖率和词元频次，对基础模型的遗忘风险进行排序与诊断。\n- 提示模型开发者应对输出投影层采用独立优化器配置，而非统一冻结模型主体或事后修补权重。\n- 可与数据回放、LoRA 等方法组合，在保持新领域学习能力的同时进一步降低灾难性遗忘。"
notes = "标题：A Deafening Silence: Catastrophic Forgetting Lives in the Output Embeddings of Tokens the Data Never Speaks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09835\n摘要：Continual pre-training and fine-tuning in Large Language Models (LLMs) inevitably induce catastrophic forgetting, typically mitigated by replay using often-inaccessible original data. In this data-free regime, we analyze where forgetting occurs and why. Systematic parameter freezing across five settings up to 1.4B reveals that forgetting concentrates selectively in the output embeddings of tokens rarely seen in the new corpus, whereas the same sqrt(v-hat) band of the body is inert and new learning resides elsewhere. This localization is governed by the vocabulary deficiency of the corpus rather than the training mode, allowing pre-retraining risk ranking from token counts alone within a fixed base model. Mechanistically, absent tokens receive persistent one-sided softmax gradients that Ada"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09832"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SkillForge将技能划分为试用、活跃、稳定和退役四种状态，根据适应度持续执行筛选、稳定化、淘汰与变异。强化学习前，系统利用基础模型自身的执行轨迹预先淘汰低适应度技能，并以过滤后的技能库构造监督微调起点。进入强化学习后，技能生命周期管理与策略优化同步进行，从而避免过时或有害技能持续误导智能体。该方法在多个交互式智能体基准上取得最高综合成功率，相对最强基线最高提升7.8%，并发布包含5000余条标注记录的SkillFurnace数据集。"
purpose = "- 为智能体技能库引入显式生命周期和适应度机制，控制技能积累造成的污染与冗余。\n- 展示技能记忆演化与模型策略优化可以组成闭环，而非将技能库视为静态外部资源。\n- SkillFurnace中的技能适应度、退役事件和人工失败分类可用于技能质量评估及治理研究。\n- 适合应用于需要持续学习、经验复用和长程任务执行的智能体训练流程。"
notes = "标题：SkillForge: Co-Evolving Skills and Agents via Dynamic Skill Lifecycles\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09832\n摘要：Memory-augmented reinforcement learning strengthens LLM agents' ability to solve complex long-horizon tasks. Skills are one such form of memory, pairing instructions with an applicability condition over task types. However, retaining every skill indiscriminately as the policy improves lets obsolete or harmful entries accumulate and mislead the agent. We propose SkillForge, an agentic RL method that compiles and evolves the skill library through a fitness-driven skill lifecycle of trial, active, stable, and retired states, so that the skills and the model co-evolve throughout training. A pre-RL evaluation phase first uses the base model's own rollouts to pre-retire low-fitness skills, yielding a filtered library that then seeds supervised fine-tuning. Reinforcement learning takes over from "

summary = "BoT-GRPO通过长度无关的词元级奖励聚合，在无需价值网络的情况下提升推理强化学习的收敛速度与最终效果。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09804"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "BoT-GRPO将GRPO扩展至词元级奖励模型，汇集多条轨迹中的全部词元奖励，并按来源序列长度的倒数进行加权。算法依据加权组统计量计算逐词元优势，既保持长度不变性，又无需评论家或价值网络，可直接替换使用词元级奖励的GRPO。在React前端代码生成任务中，该方法达到80%编译率的速度最高提升1.9倍，并优于GSPO、DAPO和PURE等变体的最终编译率及VLM评判胜率。在AIME数学推理任务上，BoT-GRPO以一半训练步数取得相对GRPO最高8.1%的Pass@$k$绝对增益；实验还表明，干净、有界且稳定的细粒度奖励比信息更丰富但噪声较大的奖励更有效。"
purpose = "- 为推理模型提供无需价值网络的高效过程监督强化学习方案，降低训练复杂度与计算开销。\n- 说明按序列长度逆加权的词元聚合可缓解不同长度轨迹对优势估计的偏置。\n- 可作为GRPO的即插即用替代方案，用于数学推理和代码生成等具有细粒度过程奖励的任务。\n- 奖励模型设计应优先保证信号干净、有界和稳定，而非单纯追求反馈维度与信息丰富度。"
notes = "标题：BoT-GRPO: Efficient Process-Reward RL for Reasoning via Bag-of-Token Aggregation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09804\n摘要：Reinforcement learning is now central to eliciting reasoning in large language models, while in the popular algorithm Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) every token in a rollout receives the same advantage. We ask how to make process supervision efficient: accelerating convergence and improving final quality without the cost of value networks. We propose Bag-of-Tokens Group Relative Policy Optimization (BoT-GRPO), which extends GRPO to token-level reward models through a length-invariant \"bag of tokens\" aggregation: it collects all token-level rewards across rollouts, weights each by the inverse of its source sequence length, and computes per-token advantages relative to weighted group statistics. BoT-GRPO is critic-free, and is a drop-in replacement wherever GRPO is used when token"

content = "Bolzano 是面向数学开放问题求解的开源多智能体系统，由多个并行证明智能体和一个验证智能体组成，并持续维护人类可读的研究状态。系统早期在专家选定问题上得到 8 项结果，其证明均由领域专家检查。随后，研究者在没有问题特定人工指导的情况下，将其用于从四组论文中提取的约 3800 个开放问题，成功解决约 200 个。针对 STOC 2026 录用论文的实验还回答了论文提出的 4 个问题，并得到原作者确认。"

notes = "标题：From Expert-Guided Proof Search to Automated Open-Problem Solving\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09769\n摘要：Large language models are increasingly contributing to mathematical research, where progress often depends on efficient proof search, incremental improvements and careful verification. Initial manual use on expert-selected problems yielded 8 results whose proofs were checked by domain experts. Motivated by these case studies, we ran Bolzano without problem-specific human guidance on about 3,800 open problems extracted from four sets of papers, solving about 200 open problems. One experiment used papers accepted to STOC 2026, a top conference in theoretical computer science. There, we answered four questions raised in the papers, as conf"

id = "cero-rollout"
title = "CERO：强化学习后训练的 Rollout 时空预算分配"
subtopic = "策略优化"
topics = [

score = 3
source = "arXiv"
summary = "CERO 通过在线原始—对偶调度，在整个强化学习后训练周期内动态决定提示选择、重访频率与每轮 Rollout 组数，从而提升有限生成预算的利用效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09679"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "CERO 将有限 Rollout 预算在完整训练周期中的分配建模为基于累计提示曝光量的凹代理效用优化问题。该方法采用在线原始—对偶调度器，根据奖励变化反馈和预算偏差，动态调整提示准入、跨轮次重访频率以及每轮生成的响应组数量。其利用紧凑的 Fenchel 表示线性化累计曝光依赖，并通过投影在线梯度下降更新提示特定的支撑斜率和共享预算价格。理论上，CERO 针对固定速率及同路径时变基准给出了代理分配目标的逐路径保证；实验中，它在相同训练响应预算下，于三个骨干模型和五个数学推理基准上均取得最高的 avg@16 宏平均成绩。"
purpose = "- 将 Rollout 优化从单次更新内的固定预算分配扩展到整个训练周期的全局预算协调。\n- 可通过奖励组内差异识别更具训练价值的提示，并自适应安排其重访频率。\n- 在线预算价格与节奏控制机制可用于减少均匀采样或预设支出计划造成的计算浪费。\n- 为受限计算预算下的强化学习后训练提供了兼具理论保证和实证收益的样本调度方案。"
notes = "标题：CERO: Where and When to Allocate Rollouts for RL Post-Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09679\n摘要：Adaptive rollout methods for group-relative reinforcement learning typically allocate a fixed per-update budget across prompts. We instead study how to coordinate a finite rollout budget over the entire training horizon. We formulate this problem using a concave surrogate utility of cumulative prompt exposure and introduce CERO, an online primal dual scheduler for prompt admission and budget pacing. In our experiments, each admitted prompt receives a fixed-size response group. CERO instead adapts which prompts are selected, how often they are revisited across rounds, and how many groups are generated in each round. A compact Fenchel representation linearizes the dependence on cumulative exposure, while projected online gradient descent updates prompt-specific supporting slopes and a shared"

title = "在策略蒸馏传递新技能而非新知识"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，反向 KL 在策略蒸馏能够显著传递跨结构的组合推理技能，却几乎无法扩展学生模型的参数化事实知识。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09639"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究通过可控合成框架，将教师额外提供的事实知识与多步组合推理技能独立分离，并测量学生模型的初始能力。覆盖三个模型家族的四个模型实验表明，基于反向 KL 的在策略蒸馏可将组合技能迁移到未见过的推理结构，但事实知识迁移非常有限。消融实验发现，将反向 KL 替换为正向 KL 可以恢复事实传递，而学生自身轨迹主要改善多步推理的执行能力。近期事实问答与竞赛数学实验呈现相同的不对称性，说明该方法主要教会模型组织和组合已有知识，而非写入新的参数化知识。"
purpose = "- 澄清在策略蒸馏的主要收益来自推理技能重组，而不是事实记忆扩容。\n- 在需要注入新知识时，应考虑正向 KL、专门的知识微调或检索增强，而不能仅依赖反向 KL 蒸馏。\n- 学生模型自身生成的轨迹适合训练多步推理执行，可作为提升组合泛化能力的重要机制。\n- 评估蒸馏方法时应分别测量知识获取与技能迁移，避免将推理性能提升误判为知识增长。"
notes = "标题：On-Policy Distillation Teaches New Skills but Not New Knowledge\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09639\n摘要：On-policy distillation (OPD) strengthens language-model reasoning, yet whether students acquire new factual knowledge or compositional skill for multi-step reasoning remains unknown. We separate these capabilities using a controlled synthetic framework that measures the student's initial capabilities and independently controls the teacher's additional facts, compositional skill, or both. Across four models from three families, reverse-KL OPD reliably transfers compositional skill across unseen reasoning structures, but transfers minimal factual knowledge. Decoupling the distillation recipe reveals the source of this asymmetry: replacing reverse KL with forward KL restores factual transfer, whereas student rollouts specifically improve the execution of multi-step reasoning. Experiments on r"

score = 3

summary = "研究基于 JetBrains IDE 中真实软件工程师的交互会话提出 SWE-TaskFlow，主张编码智能体基准应针对具体使用场景校准任务类型、会话长度与任务转换分布。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09633"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究收集了 4,782 个真实软件工程师使用 JetBrains IDE 编码智能体的生产会话，并重点分析至少包含三条用户消息的长会话。真实会话不仅覆盖代码问答、规划、审查、重构和执行等多种任务，而且用户会在一次会话中频繁切换任务类型，与基于 issue 构造的单一任务基准明显不同。作者提出 SWE-TaskFlow，通过提示拆分和可验证的代码仓库问答改造现有 issue 派生基准，并使用任务流对齐分数 TFAS 选择更符合目标场景的交互轨迹。在 700 个 SWE-Bench Pro 任务上的试验显示，分步顺序执行会使智能体成本约翻倍，却未带来稳定的解决率变化，说明交互协议本身也是关键评测维度。"
purpose = "- 构建编码智能体基准时，应先明确目标用户与使用场景，再依据真实会话测量校准任务类型、会话长度和任务转换。\n- 除最终解决率外，还应报告交互轮次、任务流对齐程度和执行成本，避免单轮 issue 修复任务掩盖真实使用负担。\n- SWE-TaskFlow 可在保留原有任务及测试可验证性的同时，将现有软件工程基准扩展为更贴近真实用户流程的多阶段交互评测。"
notes = "标题：Coding-Agent Benchmarks Should Match Their Users' Task Flows\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09633\n摘要：The evaluation of coding agents generally strives to be as realistic as possible. In our study, we collect 4,782 agent sessions of real software engineers in JetBrains IDEs, which we call Production Sessions. Since our subject is interactive agents, we study the sessions with at least three user messages (33% of the sample). These long sessions differ from issue-derived benchmark tasks in two ways: (i) user requests span a far wider mix of task types - questions about the project's code, planning, review, refactoring, execution - and (ii) users switch between types throughout a session. Long-session samples from three public interaction corpora exhibit markedly different Task Flows (the distributions of session lengths, task types, and type-to-type transitions), so no single interaction di"

summary = "论文将分散的历史推理经验内化为轻量级情境条件化策略，使长程智能体能按当前时空情境激活合适的思考类型，而无需无限扩张显式记忆与上下文。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09590"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出情境条件化思考记忆框架，将历史推理经验转化为预测“当前应思考什么”的轻量级策略，具体推理仍由大语言模型完成。框架中的情境不仅表示当前状态，还可描述时间或时空演化，并通过跨多个独立轨迹定期分析临时经验，发现重复出现的长程规律。实验中，该策略在时间规则泛化任务上达到 1.000 F1，并将 DeepSeek 的推理 F1 从 0.789 提升至 0.868。在包含 3 万个历史情境时，每次查询的在线处理时间由 0.3636 ms 降至 0.0382 ms；积累充分的跨经验重复证据后，关系发现 F1 与未来思考准确率均达到 1.000。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体提供一种固定规模的经验内化方式，缓解显式记忆库和上下文持续膨胀的问题。\n- 将记忆功能从内容检索提升为思考策略激活，使智能体能够根据时序或时空情境选择应关注的推理方向。\n- 通过跨轨迹规律发现，把分散经验中的长期模式固化为新的思考知识，适用于持续运行和重复任务环境。\n- 轻量级策略显著降低在线检索与处理开销，可作为大模型推理前的低成本认知路由模块。"
notes = "标题：Learning Situation-Conditioned Thinking Policies for Long-Term LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09590\n摘要：Long-running autonomous agents must reuse accumulated reasoning experience without allowing explicit historical memory and LLM context to grow indefinitely. This paper proposes a situation-conditioned thinking memory framework that transforms historical reasoning experience into a lightweight policy for predicting what should be thought about in the current situation, while leaving detailed reasoning to a large language model. Situations may represent temporal or spatiotemporal evolution rather than only current states. Temporary experiences are also "

content = "DrugTargetWorld 以程序化方式生成模拟生物样本库，每个“世界”包含 5.4 万名参与者的基因型、蛋白质、健康记录、临床结局及合成 MRI 数据。因果结构对评测者已知、对智能体隐藏，智能体需要构建疾病表型、识别因果驱动蛋白，并判断具有治疗价值的调控方向，还可选择开展虚拟湿实验。研究在 20 个心血管疾病世界和三档实验预算下完成了 540 次评测，其中 Opus 5 与 GPT-5.6 Sol 的平均综合得分最高，分别为 39.98 和 35.38，平均均能找回 64% 的因果驱动因素。现有智能体仍难以稳定排除具有误导性的非因果蛋白，瓶颈主要在于整合表型构建、因果证据与干预决策。"
purpose = "- 为药物靶点发现智能体提供具有隐藏真值和可验证奖励的可扩展训练环境。\n- 可用于评估科研智能体区分因果关系与统计关联的能力，避免真实生物样本库缺乏因果真值的问题。\n- 多模态患者级合成数据有助于研究表型构建、证据整合和干预决策之间的端到端误差传播。\n- 结果表明，仅提高因果驱动因素召回率并不足够，未来还需重点训练智能体识别和排除误导性非因果候选。"
notes = "标题：DrugTargetWorld: A Synthetic Biobank for Training and Benchmarking AI Scientists\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09558\n摘要：Drug target discovery requires distinguishing molecules that causally drive disease from those that are merely associated with it. Training and evaluating AI agents to perform this workflow end-to-end is difficult because real world biobanks lack known causal ground truth and participant-level data is access controlled. We introduce DrugTargetWorld, a framework that procedurally generates simulated biobanks, or \"worlds,\" with known but concealed causal structure. Each world contains genotypes, proteins, health records, outcomes, and synthetic magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) for 54,000 participants. Agents must construct a disease phenotype, identify causal driver proteins, infer the beneficial direction of modulation, and optionally conduct virtual 'wet lab' experiments. We evaluated nine"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09520"
code = ""

content = "SIGMA面向车端快速模块与云端慢速VLM协作，解决何时应让云端推理介入时效敏感驾驶决策的问题。该框架将规划器嵌入不确定性评估，通过仿真语义和几何不确定性下的合理场景，分析其对可行轨迹与规划成本的影响。论文提出预期规划收益（EPG），用于决定云端调用、云端指导融合以及期限和资源约束下的请求优先级。CARLA实验显示，相比固定周期协作，SIGMA可减少50%的非必要云端交互，在动态场景中将导航成功率提高超过6%，并将完成时间最多缩短26.2%。"
purpose = "- 将不确定性评估从感知层提升至决策层，优先处理真正影响规划结果的不确定性。\n- 可为端云协同自动驾驶系统提供按需调用大模型的路由指标，兼顾实时性、算力成本与决策质量。\n- EPG思路可推广至机器人等具身系统，用任务收益而非单纯置信度决定是否启用昂贵推理。"
notes = "标题：Not All Uncertainty Matters: Simulation-in-the-Loop Fast-Slow Reasoning for Decision-Critical Autonomous Driving System\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09520\n摘要：Large vision-language models (VLMs) provide powerful open-world perception and reasoning for autonomous driving, but their high computational cost and inference latency make continuous cloud-side use impractical. This motivates fast--slow collaboration, where efficient onboard modules handle real-time perception and control while cloud models provide high-level reasoning only when needed. The key challenge is deciding when cloud reasoning should influence time-critical driving decisions. Existing methods often rely on perception uncertainty, heuristic triggers, or resource-driven policies, without assessing whether resolving an uncertainty will improve planning. We propose \\textbf{SIGMA}, a simulation-in-the-loop framework for task-oriented fast--slow collaboration. SIGMA embeds the planne"

summary = "RSI-Forge利用多智能体流水线将研究论文转化为带自动评估器和复现基线的可执行环境，以规模化训练与评估递归自我改进智能体。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09426"
code = ""

content = "RSI-Forge由构建、复现和审查三个智能体协作，将已发表论文转化为可执行任务，并为任务配备自动评估器。系统独立复现每篇论文的方法以建立基线，共构建覆盖18个领域的210个环境，其中90个经过独立领域专家审查。在120个环境的三轮连续尝试中，模型继承先前代码与笔记但保持权重不变，84%的环境至少有一个模型在首轮后继续提升。模型在68个环境中超过论文复现基线，且95%的成功轨迹包含超越参数调优的实质性工作；分析也发现低分模型探索不足、更易接受低于标准误差的增益，并更依赖开发集调参。"
purpose = "- 将论文中的研究问题、方法和指标自动转化为可执行环境，降低递归自我改进研究对领域专家的依赖。\n- 通过独立复现建立可比较的起始基线，并用自动评估器支持多轮迭代和进步测量。\n- 可用于研究固定权重智能体如何依靠代码、笔记与经验积累实现持续改进。\n- 专家指出的捷径抵抗、论文忠实度和任务可穷尽性问题，可作为后续环境审计与质量控制的重点。"
notes = "标题：RSI-Forge: From Research Papers to Environments for Recursive Self-Improvement\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09426\n摘要：Environments are the foundation of recursive self-improvement: they provide the problems agents work on and the feedback used to evaluate progress. Yet constructing challenging research environments with reliable evaluation still depends on domain experts, limiting their scale and disciplinary coverage. We introduce RSI-Forge, a multi-agent pipeline that turns published papers into executable environments for self-improvement. Three agents coordinate construction, reproduction, and review to produce tasks with automated evaluators; each paper's method is independently reimplemented to establish a baseline score. We present 210 environments across 18 fields, including 90 reviewed by independent human domain experts. Both experts and agent judges give high ratings to the potential for improv"

summary = "研究揭示自消耗训练中模型分布与人类偏好的协同演化会放大初始偏差，而注入足量参考数据可稳定并引导系统的长期均衡。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09415"
code = ""

content = "论文研究用户从模型生成候选中筛选偏好样本、再用这些合成数据迭代训练模型时，模型分布与用户偏好相互影响的长期动力学。完全依赖用户筛选的合成数据会持续放大初始偏差，使系统收敛到多个单点均衡之一，并由初期占优的样本最终主导。以足够高的比例注入参考数据后，系统会转而形成唯一且全局吸引的均衡。作者进一步提出联合选择参考分布及其混合权重的高效算法，在保留目标属性的同时降低参考数据采集成本。"
purpose = "- 说明合成数据闭环不仅会改变模型分布，也会反向塑造用户偏好，因此固定偏好假设可能低估长期偏差放大风险。\n- 可将参考数据注入作为防止分布单一化和偏好坍缩的稳定机制。\n- 为迭代式生成模型训练提供参考数据分布与混合比例的联合优化方法。\n- 启示实际平台持续监测模型输出、用户选择行为及其耦合变化，而非仅评估单轮模型质量。"
notes = "标题：Self-Consuming Generative Models with Co-Evolving Human Preferences\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09415\n摘要：Generative models are increasingly trained in self-consuming iterative loops, where users curate preferred samples from model-generated candidates and the curated samples are used to train future generations of the model. Prior work has largely assumed fixed user preferences, but in practice exposure to model outputs gradually reshapes what users perceive as desirable, creating a feedback loop in which model distributions and user preferences co-evolve. We take a first step toward understanding the long-term behavior of such coupled dynamics. We show that when training relies entirely on user-curated synthetic data, iterative curation amplifies initial biases and drives the system toward one of multiple singleton equilibria in which the instance holding an initial advantage eventually domi"

notes = "标题：Stability and Diversity of Networked Self-Consuming Generative Ecosystems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09409\n摘要：The widespread deployment of generative AI has made it increasingly difficult to distinguish synthetic content from real data. Consequently, synthetic data is inevitably incorporated into the training pipelines of future model generations, forming a self-consuming training loop. Prior work has studied the effects of such recursive self-consuming training, but analyses have largely been limited to isolated models, where a model consumes only its own synthetic data, or to simplified interactions between two models. This paper takes a first step toward understanding networked self-consuming generative models, in which multiple models consume synthetic data generated by one another through complex interaction pathways. We introduce a theoretical framework representing models as nodes in a dire"

code = ""

content = "论文指出，词法或嵌入相关性无法保证检索结果整体具备回答问题所需的充分证据，尤其容易遗漏多会话及时间问题中的互补事实。作者引入盲化 LLM 判断，并以 Gold Hit 和 Turn Hit 作为证据覆盖代理指标。提出的预算约束平坦重构方法 BFR 先通过 FCA-MS 分解问题的信息需求并选出联合覆盖需求的紧凑候选集，再从深层文本搜索及实体、会话视图中持续补充未见记录。LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 实验显示，BFR 优于使用相同记忆存储的近期智能体记忆方法；在 LongMemEval-S 上，评判准确率由 72.4% 提升至 82.2%，Turn Hit 达到 91.4%。"
purpose = "- 长期记忆检索应优化证据集合的联合充分性，而不只是单条记录的相关性。\n- 可先将问题分解为多个信息需求，再通过互补检索补齐跨会话、跨时间的证据缺口。\n- Gold Hit、Turn Hit 与盲化 LLM 判断可用于同时评估答案质量和证据覆盖情况。\n- BFR 展示了在固定存储与检索预算下改进智能体记忆系统的可行路径。"
notes = "标题：Relevance Is Not Sufficiency: What Actually Closes the Evidence Gap in Long-Term Memory QA\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09348\n摘要：LLM agents that interact with a user across many sessions accumulate histories that exceed their context window, so they store past interactions in an external memory and answer each question from a small set of retrieved records. Existing memory systems rank records by lexical or embedding relevance, yet the top-ranked memories can each be relevant while jointly omitting a complementary fact that the answer requires, especially for multi-session and temporal questions. Drawing on the distinction between relevance and sufficiency in legal evidence scholarship, we recast memory retrieval as constructing a sufficient memory set. To operationalize this view, we introduce a blinded LLM judgment over the retrieved set, together with Gold Hit and Turn Hit as evidence-coverage proxies. We then pr"

code = ""

content = "Visual Jev 面向多主体图像生成，要求只有在目标条件成立且相关主体与给定参考身份一致时，验证问题才获得正标签。研究离线构造固定问题，以二元监督训练 Qwen3.5-4B 验证器，并直接读取语言模型头输出的 Yes 概率。各问题概率的均值被用作 GRPO 训练奖励，同时保留单项判断以支持检查和故障分析。使用 200 个 MICo-150K 任务训练 30 次更新后，方法在人工筛选的 897 个 MICo-Bench 任务上将综合分数由 41.78 提升至 52.50，但离线人工评估尚未证明其相对直接打分具有统计显著优势。"

We construct fixed questions offline, train a Qwen3.5-4B verifier with binary supervision, and directly read Yes probabilities from its language-model head. Their mean supplies a GRPO reward while retaining individual judgments for inspection. Using 200 MICo-150K training tasks and"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究揭示 LLM 自我改进循环反复使用小型评估集会放大选择噪声并造成赢家诅咒，使表面提升显著高估真实的留出集收益。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09239"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文将自我改进系统中的“保留更优候选”建模为存在相关测量误差的选择过程，并分析评估集复用引发的锁定效应。Qwen 自主改写指令的实验显示，首轮之后多数提案实际上有害，而循环仍会因选择噪声将最佳候选判为进步。预注册实验中，仅含 16 个样本的选择集会令最终得分相对留出集准确率虚高 13～20 个百分点，扩大至 256 个样本后偏差仍有 1～5 个百分点。使用从未参与选择的 64 个样本评估初始与当前指令，可消除平均报告偏差，但单次估计仍可能偏离约 6 个百分点；所测试的接受规则在完整循环上也未优于贪心接受。"
purpose = "- 说明自我改进循环中的局部择优不等于真实能力提升，反复复用评估集会系统性放大偶然高分。\n- 建议将候选选择集与最终报告用留出集严格分离，并报告留出集收益及其置信区间。\n- 设计接受规则时应显式建模候选分数的相关误差、赢家诅咒与错误提交后的路径锁定。\n- 小规模评估集尤其容易制造虚假进步，扩大样本量虽可降低偏差，但不能完全替代独立验证。"
notes = "标题：The Winner's Curse in LLM Self-Improvement Loops: Selection Noise, Lock-in, and Acceptance Rules\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09239\n摘要：Self-improving LLM systems propose changes to themselves and keep those that score better on a small evaluation set. We treat this keep-if-better step as selection under measurement noise, model the correlated errors of the candidates in a single decision, and study empirically what happens when the evaluation set is reused. In runs where Qwen models rewrite their own instructions and every candidate is also scored on 600 held-out items, most proposals after the first are harmful, and the model gives the size of the winner's curse of a generation's best candidate. With a prior from a separate pilot, it matches the average overstatement of first-generation commits in native loops, though not setting by setting. In a pre-registered study, the final selection-set score of greedy loops exceede"

summary = "RLDiscover 通过渐进式协同进化与概率评估，让 LLM 自动搜索可解释、可迁移且性能显著提升的无模型深度强化学习算法。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09218"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "RLDiscover 面向无模型深度强化学习算法的自演化，解决耦合算法组件难以联合搜索以及候选算法训练评估成本高的问题。框架先对特定组件进行定向修改，再逐步推进至联合进化，以降低同时改动多个组件导致训练失效的风险。其渐进式概率评估结合分阶段训练和重复实验，在搜索广度、评估可信度与计算成本之间取得平衡。实验覆盖 SAC、PPO、DQN 和四组基准，算法家族的回报中位增益达到 32%～84%，部分结果相对近零基线提升约 363 倍；发现的自适应鲁棒损失、进度相关价值目标和运行统计组合还能迁移至未见任务。"
purpose = "- 展示 LLM 驱动的程序进化可从超参数优化进一步扩展到强化学习算法结构与更新规则的自动发现。\n- 渐进式协同进化适合搜索存在强依赖关系的算法组件，可避免一次性联合修改造成的训练崩溃。\n- 分阶段训练与重复评估可缓解随机种子带来的适应度不确定性，并显著降低候选算法筛选成本。\n- 多次独立搜索得到相似且可解释的算法机制，说明自动演化结果具备一定稳定性、可分析性和跨任务迁移潜力。"
notes = "标题：RLDISCOVER: LLM-driven co-evolution of reinforcement learning algorithms\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09218\n摘要：LLM-guided program evolution has enabled discoveries in mathematics and computational optimization, raising the prospect of reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms that self-evolve to improve how agents learn. However, realizing this prospect faces two obstacles. Joint search over coupled algorithmic components is difficult to scale: simultaneous changes can disrupt learning, while isolated changes overlook their dependencies. Evaluating candidate algorithms also requires costly training, with fitness remaining uncertain across random seeds. We introduce RLDiscover, a framework for the self-evolution of model-free deep RL algorithms. Progressive Co-Evolution advances from targeted component edits to joint evolution, while Progressive Probabilistic Evaluation balances search breadth and eval"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09215"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "AGAR（Algorithm Generation As RL）把程序进化建模为马尔可夫决策过程，其中动作不是模型生成的程序，而是用于条件化模型的模块化前缀。该框架将父代选择、变异强度、多样性维持、经验记忆和局部信用分配分别连接到价值估计、自适应探索等强化学习组件。各类估计器可以独立替换或关闭，无须改变控制器，也不需要对后端语言模型进行梯度训练，从而支持逐机制审计。统一实验覆盖 19 项任务、两个模型后端和三个随机种子，结果显示其在多数任务上超过两个已发表基线中的较强者，优势主要集中于竞争性编程任务。"
purpose = "- 为 LLM 程序搜索提供统一、模块化且可审计的强化学习形式化框架。\n- 将单一标量适应度扩展为细粒度信用分配，有助于识别程序改写中真正产生收益的部分。\n- 支持按组件替换价值估计、探索和记忆机制，便于开展消融实验与算法迭代。\n- 无须训练后端模型，适合利用现有闭源或开放权重模型进行低成本算法发现。"
notes = "标题：AGAR: a reinforcement learning substrate for LLM program evolution\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09215\n摘要：Given a task and an evaluator, a language model can rewrite a candidate program while a search loop decides which rewrites survive, offering a practical route to algorithm discovery. But that loop is governed by five constants set by hand: which parent to select, how hard to mutate, how to keep diversity, what to remember, and a scalar score that never says which part of the program earned it. Reinforcement learning already has an estimator for each. The obstacle is that program evolution is not usually written down as a decision process. We formalize it as a Markov decision process whose action is the modular prefix the model is conditioned on, rather than the program it emits. Credit assignment, value estimation, adaptive exploration, and experience memory can then attach to distinct com"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09197"
code = ""

content = "FreeEvolve将智能体外围提示、技能和工作流的优化过程本身也纳入自动学习，不再依赖预先设计的固定搜索循环。环境仅规定目标智能体、评估器、数据与资源约束，演化器则自主决定测试内容、证据收集规模、候选推进策略及停止时机。系统以可编辑的“进化技能”控制优化过程，并通过元进化在新生成的目标智能体上评价和改进这些技能。在 tau3-bench、ARC-AGI-2、ARC-AGI-3 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，其主要留出指标平均提升13.6分，元进化技能在新目标智能体上较种子技能再提升6.9分。"

notes = "标题：FreeEvolve: Learning to Evolve Beyond Fixed Loops\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09197\n摘要：Agent evolvers automate the design of the prompts, skills and workflows around language model agents, yet the optimization process they follow is still designed by hand: a fixed search loop decides how candidates are evaluated, which are kept and when the search stops. We propose FREEEVOLVE, which automates this process as well. An environment specifies the goal, target agent, evaluator, data and resource limits; within these limits, the evolver itself decides what to test, how much evidence to collect, which candidates to pursue and when to stop. These decisions follow an editable evolution skill, which we improve through meta-evolution by scoring each candidate skill on the fresh target agent it produces. The optimization process thus becomes a capability learned from experience rather t"

summary = "研究将无文本输出的概率模型 Jev 嵌入搜索并蒸馏为轻量评估器，验证其在国际象棋和段落重排中可低成本提供高质量决策监督。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09188"
code = ""

content = "Jev 是一种仅输出校准概率的 System One 模型，研究将其判断能力用于时间约束严格的国际象棋和段落重排任务。由于实时调用无法满足搜索速度，作者把成对判断蒸馏为可在每个棋局状态运行的紧凑评估器，并结合 Stockfish 搜索、开局库和残局表库，使机器人在 Lichess 快棋对局中达到 2200 分以上。固定标注预算下，融合 Jev 与 Qwen3-32B 标签比全部使用 Qwen 标签高出 9.6 Elo，而重复调用同一教师无法带来同等收益。在段落重排中，Jev 仅用 21 分钟 API 调用生成的标签即可训练出媲美 Qwen 标签效果的重排器，显著降低监督数据生产成本。"
purpose = "- 展示“搜索提供前瞻、蒸馏降低单步判断成本”的高效决策系统设计范式。\n- 表明异构教师之间的互补性可能比增加同一教师的重复标注更有价值。\n- 为固定标注预算下的教师组合、标签融合和成本分配提供实证依据。\n- 说明仅输出概率的轻量判断模型可作为搜索评估器与数据标注器，而不必承担文本生成开销。"
notes = "标题：From Probabilities to Decisions: Search and Multi-Teacher Distillation with Jev\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09188\n摘要：Probability-only models, which TypeSafe calls System One models, return calibrated probabilities for fixed choices in milliseconds and generate no text. We study one such model, Jev, through two tasks that require decisions under tight constraints. In bullet chess, a bot that places Jev's judgment inside Stockfish search alongside an opening book and endgame tablebases climbs above a 2200 Lichess bullet rating against other bots. Live model calls are too slow for search, so we distill pairwise judgments into a compact evaluator that runs at every position. We then ask how best to spend a fixed labeling budget when an LLM, Qwen3-32B, is available as a second teacher. In chess, averaging both judges' labels beats spending the whole budget on Qwen alone by 9.6 Elo (95% interval 4.3 to 14.9), "

summary = "论文提出轨迹级监控器 VoS，学习估计各步骤干预智能体的价值，并在伤害预算约束下选择是否及何时纠正智能体。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09115"
code = ""
dataset = ""

content = "研究针对智能体引导中的三个决策：是否纠正、在哪一步纠正，以及采用何种纠正机制。作者在三个基准和两个智能体的每个非终止步骤上分别应用四种干预机制，构建包含约 8.2 万条反事实续写、覆盖 1,864 条轨迹的逐步结果表（SOT）。实验发现，不确定性信号能够识别可能失败的轨迹，但没有任何单一信号能稳定定位最适合干预的步骤。为此，作者提出可离线或在线运行的轨迹级监控器 VoS（Value of Steering），从 SOT 学习每一步的干预价值，并通过伤害预算触发器限制对原本成功轨迹的扰动。VoS 在全部 12 种设置中平均提升 7.8 个百分点，并在 11 种设置中超过最强不确定性触发方法，平均领先 2.9 个百分点。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体提供数据驱动的运行时纠错策略，避免仅凭不确定性阈值决定干预时机。\n- SOT 展示了通过逐步反事实续写测量干预因果效果的方法，可用于训练轨迹监控器和价值模型。\n- 伤害预算可约束纠错机制对成功轨迹的负面影响，在性能提升与干预风险之间建立可控权衡。\n- 消融结果提示，基于真实执行结果训练以及采用严格的伤害预算，是实现可靠智能体引导的关键。"
notes = "标题：From Uncertainty to Action: Learning to Steer LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09115\n摘要：Steering an LLM agent means deciding whether to correct it, at which step, and with which mechanism. Uncertainty is often used to decide when to correct an agent, but whether it can guide these decisions remains unclear. We steer agent trajectories separately at every non-terminal step with each of four mechanisms and run each continuation to completion. The resulting stepwise outcome table (SOT) holds about 82,000 counterfactual continuations of 1,864 trajectories from three benchmarks and two agents. It shows that uncertainty can identify failing trajectories, but that no single signal reliably locates the step at which steering helps. We therefore propose VoS (Value of Steering), a trajectory-level monitor, offline or online, that learns from SOT the value of steering at each step and d"

summary = "研究在真实家庭自动化智能体的五类调用点上评测九种大小模型，表明按调用点路由本地小模型可在接近前沿模型效果的同时显著降低成本，但执行控制存在突出安全风险。"

code = "https://github.com/waldiez/slm-callsite-eval"

link = ""
content = "研究使用 Wactorz 的原始生产提示和两个真实 Home Assistant 环境，在意图路由、动作分类、设备落地、多智能体流水线规划及 Python 代码生成五类调用点上测试了 9 个模型，共包含 280 个案例和 2520 次评分调用。结果显示，模型能力排序会随调用点变化，规模更大也不保证更优；最佳本地模型在五类调用点中的四类上与托管模型没有统计显著差异，主要差距集中于代码生成。为每个调用点选择最佳本地模型可取得 91.8% 的准确率，而托管方案为 95.4%；在线部署中仅托管两个生成类调用点，即以 28% 的成本获得与全托管相同的用户评分（39/43）。评测还揭示动作执行场景中的安全失效：部分小模型会对系统并不拥有的设备大量执行操作，另一些模型则退化为拒绝所有请求。"
purpose = "- 为智能体系统采用按调用点分配模型的路由策略提供实证依据，避免所有任务统一使用昂贵的前沿模型。\n- 部署时可将意图分类、动作分类等较简单调用交给本地小模型，仅为代码生成等高难度调用使用托管模型。\n- 模型选型不能只看聚合准确率，应针对各调用点分别评估，并报告执行准确率、错误执行率与拒绝率。\n- 家庭自动化等具备现实动作能力的系统需为设备落地与执行控制增加权限校验、拒绝校准和安全验证层。"
notes = "标题：Not Every Call Needs a Frontier Model: Per-Call-Site Evaluation of Small Language Models in a Deployed Agentic Home-Automation System\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09021\n摘要：An agentic system issues several structurally different kinds of LLM calls. It routes intent, classifies actions, grounds language in a device registry, plans multi-agent pipelines and writes the Python code those pipelines run. The difficulty of these call sites varies by an order of magnitude, yet in practice a single model, chosen for the hardest site, serves all of them. In this work, we evaluate 9 models from 0.8B to a frontier hosted model across the five call sites of a deployed open-source home-automation framework (Wactorz), using its unmodified production prompts and two real Home Assistant installations (280 cases, 2520 scored calls). We find that capability is not ordered the same way at every site, and that larger models are not uniformly better: one 4B model is worse than its"

content = "论文指出，智能体在规划、工具模拟或转述他人观点时产生的临时命题，一旦脱离原始语境写入长期记忆，便可能在后续对话中被错误地当作事实。作者将这一缺失的上下文定义为“话语所有权”，即授权某个命题成立的世界、推理分支或说话者。研究发现，语言模型内部已经存在具有因果作用的所有权信号，但传统记忆接口在将推理转换为记录时会丢弃该信号。为此，作者提出 CASK（Causally Anchored Scoping Keys），保存表达所有权的稳定关系，而非依赖可能随等价表征变化的内部坐标。长对话冲突与工具智能体轨迹实验表明，CASK 能改善记忆准入，避免临时内容污染后续回答，并与运行时溯源机制形成互补。"
purpose = "- 为长期记忆系统提供作用域感知的写入边界，区分共享世界事实与推理过程中的临时内容。\n- 提示记忆可靠性问题不仅源于虚假信息，也可能源于真实命题脱离其原始世界、分支或说话者。\n- 相比直接存储模型内部坐标，保存稳定的关系结构更有利于跨表征变化维持所有权信息。\n- 可用于增强长程智能体、工具调用智能体及多轮对话系统的记忆准入与污染防护机制。"
notes = "标题：Whose Memory Is It? Scope-Aware Commit Rules for Long-Term LLM Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09008\n摘要：Persistent memory allows an LLM agent to carry experience across conversations, but it also turns a local reasoning mistake into a durable one. During deliberation, an agent may consider a plan, simulate a tool result, report another speaker's belief, and then reject all of them. If memory retains only the resulting sentences, those once-useful possibilities can later return as facts. The record is neither fabricated nor irrelevant; it has simply been detached from the context in which it was valid. We identify this missing context as \\emph{discourse ownership}: the world, branch, or speaker that licenses a proposition. Our first finding is counterintuitive. Language models already carry a causally active signal for ownership, yet conventional memory interfaces discard it when they convert"


[[items]]
id = "ledger-3d"
title = "Ledger：从第一人称视频构建持久化 3D 对象记忆"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",
    "具身智能",
    "状态持久化",
    "第一人称视觉",
    "状态追踪",
]
research = [

]
keywords = [
    "长期记忆",
    "三维对象记忆",
    "第一人称视觉",
    "状态追踪",
    "具身智能",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Ledger 将对象位置、历史与上下文描述整合为持久化 3D 记忆，使具身助手无需回看原始视频即可回答物体空间问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10538"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Ledger 从第一人称日常活动视频中关联跨时间观测，为物体保存位置、移动历史及简短上下文描述，即使物体离开视野或从未被触碰也会继续保留。系统按照物体的静置位置聚类观测，并仅在获得重复证据后记录移动，以降低定位噪声造成的错误更新。该记忆可保留容器内容、支撑表面等细节，并在不访问原始图像或视频的情况下回答后续空间问题。实验将 HD-EPIC 准确率从 29.7% 提升至 42.6%、UCS-Bench 从 33.8% 提升至 38.5%，在 Ego4D 上返回物体位置的中位误差为 0.99 米；多场景拼接实验则揭示了检索和记忆构建在场景切换时的不足。"
purpose = "- 为具身助手建立跨时间、离视野仍可访问的对象记忆，支持寻物、空间问答和日常活动辅助。\n- 通过重复证据确认状态变化，为存在定位噪声的长期记忆更新提供稳健机制。\n- 表明时间持久性、上下文描述和检索机制具有互补作用，应在记忆系统中联合设计。\n- 多场景下的性能下降提示系统需要场景感知的记忆分区、检索路由与跨场景状态管理。"
notes = "标题：Never Look Back: Understanding Persistence in 3D Object Memory from Egocentric Videos\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10538\n摘要：As we move through the world and carry out everyday tasks, we encounter objects that may become relevant only later. We are capable of recalling where we left something or what was inside a container, even without knowing we would need it later. Here, we study how an embodied assistant can build a similar memory from egocentric videos, by observing a person's day-to-day activities. We present Ledger, a persistent 3D object memory that combines object locations, their histories, and contextual descriptions. It associates observations across the recording and retains objects after they leave the view, including those the person never touches. It clusters each object's observations by resting locations and records a move only after repeated evidence, reducing the effect of localization noise."

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10533"

link = ""
content = "EngramEdit将条件记忆转化为可编辑的知识接口，使事实存储与通用计算相解耦，并保持Transformer主干参数固定。方法首先为同一事实的多种表达计算目标记忆表征，再联合更新共享的n-gram嵌入，使修改后的事实能够跨表达生效。训练时对高频复用嵌入施加更强的更新惩罚，以降低知识编辑对无关事实和通用能力的影响。实验显示该方法接近完美的编辑成功率，并能将新知识泛化到未见表达和多跳推理；在思维链提示下，其准确率接近最强基线的三倍。"
purpose = "- 为冻结模型主干条件下的低成本事实更新提供新路径，减少完整微调或重新训练的需求。\n- 表明条件记忆不仅能扩展模型容量，也可作为独立、可控的外部知识编辑层。\n- 多表达联合优化有助于解决同一事实因措辞变化而激活不同记忆项的问题。\n- 按嵌入复用频率约束更新，可用于缓解连续知识编辑中的旁路影响与能力退化。\n- 对构建可持续维护、支持多跳推理且知识可热更新的大模型具有参考价值。"
notes = "标题：EngramEdit: Decoupled Knowledge Updates in LLMs through Conditional Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10533\n摘要：Conditional memory architectures such as DeepSeek Engram use input n-grams to look up learned embeddings, expanding the capacity of large language models (LLMs) with limited additional computation. Beyond model scaling, this architecture has demonstrated the potential to decouple factual knowledge storage from general-purpose computation, offering a promising route to updating factual knowledge while keeping the Transformer backbone fixed. Realizing this potential is challenging because different expressions of a fact may activate different n-gram embeddings, while updating shared embeddings can unintentionally change the model's predictions about other facts. We propose EngramEdit for decoupled knowledge updates through conditional memory. EngramEdit first computes target memory represent"

score = 2

summary = "RECAST将跨异构信息源的证据构建建模为检索与计算操作上的序列决策，通过训练轻量级路由模型主动生成回答所需证据。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "RECAST不再局限于检索现成文本，而是通过过滤、聚合和计算等操作，从多个异构来源中主动构造证据。轻量级RouterLM迭代选择基础操作或描述定制操作，再由冻结的CompilerLM将其转换为可执行代码；证据充分后，冻结的AnswerLM生成最终答案。RouterLM先接受监督微调，再使用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练。在六类异构基准上，RECAST平均成功率达到75.6%，领先最强大模型基线15.9个百分点，并在三个留出基准上平均提升15.0个百分点。"
purpose = "- 将RAG从“相似内容检索”拓展为“检索、计算与证据合成”的联合决策过程。\n- 表明轻量级、经过训练的路由模型可以超过更强但无需训练的路由模型，为降低智能体系统成本提供思路。\n- 可用于需要跨文档过滤、聚合、计算和动态工具生成的复杂问答与长上下文任务。\n- 冻结编译模型和回答模型、仅训练路由器的设计，有利于模块化迭代及跨任务零样本泛化。"
notes = "标题：RECAST: Learning to Compute the Right Context through Adaptive Evidence Routing\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507\n摘要：Large language models are increasingly applied to tasks grounded in long, heterogeneous information sources. Conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on fixed similarity-based retrieval, while agentic variants adapt queries and tool use but remain largely retrieval-centric. However, in many tasks, the evidence required for a solution is not explicitly present in any single source item. Instead, it must be derived through filtering, aggregation, or computation across multiple source items. In this work, we introduce RECAST (Routing Evidence through Computation, Access, and Synthesized Tools), a learned framework that formulates evidence construction as a sequential decision process over heterogeneous retrieval and computation operations, allowing evidence to be actively deri"

summary = "论文利用成功后训练智能体轨迹中的决定性动作，在无需基础模型完整驱动工具链的情况下，预测其经过智能体后训练后的编程性能。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10478"
code = ""

content = "论文关注如何在投入昂贵的智能体后训练前，判断哪个基础模型检查点最具潜力。方法回放成功轨迹并在每次代码修改后运行测试，定位首次使代码仓库由失败转为通过的“决定性动作”。围绕该动作构建三项筛选指标：Decisive-Action BPB、Patch MCQ，以及前缀条件化 pass@$K$，从动作概率、候选补丁选择和功能正确续写三个角度评估基础模型。对十组公开基础模型与后训练模型的实验表明，三项指标对模型的排序均与后训练后的 SWE-bench Verified pass@$1$ 高度一致。"
purpose = "- 在执行昂贵的智能体后训练前筛选更有潜力的基础模型检查点，降低训练试错成本。\n- 将成功执行轨迹与测试验证器转化为评测信号，规避基础模型工具调用格式不稳定带来的测量偏差。\n- 为编程智能体基准提供一种从端到端结果回溯到关键动作能力的过程评估方法。\n- 该思路可推广到其他具备成功轨迹和自动验证器的长程智能体任务。"
notes = "标题：Before They Can Solve: Predicting Post-Training Coding-Agent Performance from Base Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10478\n摘要：How can we predict which base checkpoint is worth an expensive round of agentic post-training? End-to-end pass@$K$ tests whether successful behavior already appears in a base model's distribution, but it is a poor fit for agentic coding: many base checkpoints cannot reliably produce the well-formed tool invocation required to complete a task end-to-end. Single-shot or short-horizon tasks avoid these tool-calling failures by collapsing a multi-step interaction into a fixed prompt and a single patch, but they sidestep the core capability we care about: maintaining coherent state over many tool-using steps as the repository evolves. To bridge this gap, we treat successful post-trained agent trajectories as a lookahead signal of base-model potential. Replaying each trajectory and rerunning tes"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10455"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "PHRBench是一个面向幻觉后推理（PHR）的受控评测基准，覆盖4个领域、18个大语言模型和4820个受控实例。该基准不只考察最终答案，还将推理轨迹划分为幻觉服从、幻觉规避和启发式纠正，并把成功纠错且最终得到正确答案的轨迹定义为“洞察型轨迹”。实验发现，模型从错误上下文中成功恢复的情况仍较少，但成功恢复通常伴随着推理过程里更频繁的信念更新。幻觉提示本身的属性对恢复成功与否具有较强预测信号，轻量预测器的AUROC达到0.847。"
purpose = "- 为多阶段大模型系统评估错误信息沿上下文传播后的持续影响提供细粒度基准。\n- 提示评测不应只关注最终答案正确率，还应审计模型是否服从、规避或主动纠正错误前提。\n- 可利用提示属性和推理过程中的信念更新信号，预测幻觉恢复概率并触发验证、重试或人工介入机制。\n- 有助于设计具备前提检查、自我验证和错误上下文恢复能力的可靠推理系统。"
notes = "标题：PHRBench: A Behavioral Evaluation of Post-Hallucination Reasoning in LLMs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10455\n摘要：Hallucinated information can propagate through multi-stage LLM systems and become part of the context for subsequent reasoning. Existing studies of post-hallucination reasoning (PHR) mainly characterize changes in final outcomes and aggregate reasoning dynamics, leaving how models resolve hallucinated premises at the response level insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce PHRBench, a controlled benchmark for behaviorally structured PHR across four domains and 18 large language models. PHRBench characterizes each reasoning trajectory independently of final-answer correctness through Hallucination Compliance, Hallucination Avoidance, and Heuristic Correction, and defines an insightful trajectory as successful correction that ultimately reaches the correct answer. Across 4820 con"
