summary = "RunningTab通过环境侧任务标签持续记录需求、文件证据及未打开候选文件，减少智能体直接处理工作区文件时因上下文遗失造成的交付遗漏。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10444"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "RunningTab面向直接工作区交互（DWI），即智能体无需预先建立索引，直接通过终端搜索和读取工作区文件并生成交付物。框架在环境侧维护每项任务尚未满足的需求，自动将已读文件保存为带来源的摘录，并记录已列出但尚未打开的候选文件。智能体可以将需求与匹配证据及候选文件对照，完成需求、说明搁置原因，或在结束任务时通过检查重新发现未完成事项。在三个基准和三种大语言模型上的实验表明，该方法持续优于普通DWI及由模型自身维护记录的基线。"
purpose = "- 为长程知识工作智能体提供独立于上下文窗口的任务状态与需求追踪机制。\n- 将文件摘录与来源绑定，有助于提升交付内容的证据完整性和可追溯性。\n- 通过记录未打开文件和结束检查，降低智能体过早终止及遗漏关键材料的风险。\n- 表明关键执行状态由环境侧持久化，可能比依赖模型内部记忆或上下文记录更可靠。"
notes = "标题：RunningTab: Direct Workspace Interaction with Environment-Side Tabs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10444\n摘要：Much knowledge work produces new deliverables from files a workspace already holds, and LLM agents are beginning to take such work over. Through direct corpus interaction, an agent can search and read any of those files from a terminal with no indexing, and producing a deliverable from many of them in this way is what we call direct workspace interaction (DWI). Reaching the files, however, is only half the task: nothing keeps track of what the task asks for, what has been read, and what was listed but never opened, all of which slip through the context window without leaving a trace, so an agent may extract a figure and still deliver a report without it. To address this, we present RunningTab, a framework that equips direct workspace interaction with an environment-side tab: a per-task rec"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "BehaviorTrace 通过植入已知成因的行为与多重混淆控制，发现在线 GRPO 训练中的 rollout 归因信号常被梯度大小、流畅度及随机性所主导。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10422"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出开放评测框架 BehaviorTrace，在 Qwen2.5-1.5B 的在线 GRPO 微调中植入具有已知原因的行为，以检验训练 rollout 归因方法是否真实有效。实验覆盖三个随机种子，并控制梯度幅度、语言流畅度、性能提升空间、生成采样差异等混淆因素。仅按梯度大小排序的无目标基线即可达到随机水平的 4.2 至 4.5 倍，并在两个种子上追平或超过最佳目标归因方法；饱和检查点上的流畅度也不弱于所比较的梯度方法。控制流畅度后，逐 rollout 归因结果会随随机种子和生成采样显著变化，但触发词梯度与行为实际发生位置构造的目标之间的对齐信号在三个种子上均保持稳定。作者据此给出强化学习归因评测清单，并审计 GAS、TracInCP 与 TRAK 风格估计器，而非提出新的归因算法。"
purpose = "- 为在线强化学习中的训练数据归因提供带已知因果真值的评测框架。\n- 提醒研究者将梯度大小、模型流畅度和检查点饱和度纳入必要对照，避免把混淆因素误判为归因能力。\n- 归因结论应跨随机种子和多次生成采样复现，单次运行不足以支持可靠判断。\n- 可将触发词梯度与行为发生位置的对齐作为较稳健的候选诊断信号。"
notes = "标题：Which Rollout Taught It That? BehaviorTrace and the Limits of Training-Data Attribution in Online RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10422\n摘要：When reinforcement learning teaches a language model a new behavior, can we find the training rollouts that taught it? And when an attribution method says it can, how do we know the answer is real? We study both questions on online RL fine-tuning with GRPO, using a planted behavior with a known cause. We release BehaviorTrace, an open evaluation harness that combines full-gradient sketching, the planted-behavior setup, and controls for gradient magnitude, fluency, headroom, and variation across seeds and generation draws. Across three seeds on Qwen2.5-1.5B, much of the apparent attribution signal comes from confounds. A control that ranks training steps by gradient size alone, with no behavior target, reaches 4.2 to 4.5 times chance and matches or beats the best targeted estimator on two o"

content = "RobotWorld 是面向通用多模态智能体的机器人仿真测试平台，要求智能体根据指令和观测，通过机器人接口完成物理任务。基准包含84项任务，覆盖操作、移动操作、运动控制、驾驶和空中控制，并设置明确的交互预算与可执行成功检查。实验表明，当前智能体能够构建图像分割、相机标定、空间估计及基于动力学计算的复杂工作流，但这些能力尚不能稳定组合为成功行为。典型失效包括丢失任务相关物体状态、未纠正无效动作、恢复过晚以及将未完成任务误判为完成；不同模型在空间接触、连续平衡和定时交互任务上也呈现明显能力差异。"
purpose = "- 为跨任务、跨机器人形态的具身智能体提供统一且可执行验证的评测环境。\n- 提示训练和系统设计不能只强化单项感知或控制能力，还需提升状态追踪、动作纠错和能力组合的可靠性。\n- 可利用任务结果与执行轨迹联合定位失效环节，为具身智能体的过程评估、故障归因及恢复机制设计提供依据。\n- 模型间互补的能力表现可用于指导模型路由、任务分配或专门化后训练。"
notes = "标题：RobotWorld: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents for Robot Use Across Diverse Tasks and Embodiments\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10409\n摘要：General-purpose agents increasingly write code, use tools, and complete complex digital tasks, raising the question of how far these capabilities carry into the physical world. Its 84 tasks span manipulation, mobile manipulation, locomotion, driving, and aerial control, with explicit interaction budgets and executable success checks. By analysing task outcomes alongside execution traces, we identify both the capabilities that transfer and the gaps that prevent reliable completion. Furthermore, we find that current agents can construct sophisticated perception and control workflows, including image segmenta"

id = "transformer"
title = "线性注意力 Transformer 执行因果结构学习"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究构造固定权重的线性注意力 Transformer，使其前向传播精确执行因果结构学习算法的一次更新，并分析状态保留、收敛步数与误差边界。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10395"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者显式构造了一个固定权重的线性注意力 Transformer，其单个模块可精确复现带无环约束的连续因果发现算法的一次图更新，堆叠模块则可重放完整优化轨迹。模型在更新间携带当前候选因果图与算法乘子，其中保留乘子是精确执行的必要条件，因为不同乘子会产生不同的后续更新。论文给出了固定阶段内预先计算目标精度所需更新次数的条件，并证明舍入误差可在深度增长时保持有界。实验显示，构造模块与参考求解器在浮点精度范围内一致，但它也会继承求解器在合成数据和七类基准网络拓扑上的成功与失败，说明算法执行准确性不等于因果恢复准确性；常规注意力模型则未能稳定学会更新或泛化到更大图。"
purpose = "- 为使用 Transformer 精确模拟迭代优化与因果发现算法提供构造性理论案例。\n- 表明算法执行器必须显式保留影响后续更新的内部状态，不能只传递候选因果图。\n- 提醒评测时区分“忠实执行算法”和“正确恢复因果结构”，前者并不保证后者。\n- 为研究梯度训练能否学得可扩展的线性注意力算法执行器提出了开放问题。"
notes = "标题：Executing Causal Structure Learning with Linear-Attention Transformers\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10395\n摘要：Transformers can execute algorithms on data given in their input. We ask whether they can do the same for causal discovery. We study a standard continuous method that repeatedly updates a candidate causal graph while enforcing acyclicity. We explicitly construct a fixed-weight transformer whose forward pass exactly reproduces one update of this method, so repeated blocks reproduce its optimization trajectory. The transformer carries the current graph and the algorithm's multiplier between updates. We show that retaining the multiplier is essential for exact execution, since different multiplier values can lead to different next updates. We also give conditions under which, within a fixed stage, the number of updates needed to reach a target accuracy can be computed in advance and rounding "


[[items]]
id = "latent-concept-pfn"
title = "无需持续训练的持续学习：Latent Concept PFN"
subtopic = "持续学习"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Latent Concept PFN 通过冻结元训练模型并持续扩展上下文样例记忆，在潜在概念空间中执行贝叶斯推断，实现无需梯度更新的类别与域增量学习。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10379"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出以“持续推断”替代“持续训练”，模型在元训练完成后保持参数冻结，仅通过向上下文证据集加入新样例来适应新类别或新领域。Latent Concept PFN 在跨领域、跨类别共享的潜在概念空间中执行上下文贝叶斯推断，将适应过程表示为后验信念更新，从而降低灾难性遗忘。该方法无需任务身份，可统一处理类别增量学习和域增量学习；概念标注仅在元训练阶段作为潜空间的软锚点。面对噪声、歧义或不完整的概念标注，模型还会结合原始输入证据，发现预定义概念集之外的区分信息，并获得具有竞争力且可解释的持续学习表现。"
purpose = "- 为持续学习提供一种无参数更新路径，可避免反复优化带来的知识覆盖与训练成本。\n- 展示了样例记忆、上下文学习和贝叶斯后验更新结合后替代梯度适应的可能性。\n- 潜在概念空间兼顾跨域迁移与可解释性，可用于构建长期运行且抗遗忘的模型或智能体。\n- 对概念标注采用软约束而非固定瓶颈，有助于提升系统对噪声标签和开放类别的适应能力。"
notes = "标题：Continual Learning without Continual Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10379\n摘要：Continual learning requires models to adapt to new domains and new classes while retaining prior knowledge. Many existing methods rely on continued optimization, using regularization, replay, or parameter expansion to prevent new updates from overwriting previously learned knowledge. Instead, we propose replacing continual training with continual inference: a PFN-based model that is meta-trained, and then frozen, adapting to new classes only by extending an in-context evidence set. Our model, Latent Concept PFN, performs in-context Bayesian inference over a latent concept space that captures semantic structure shared across domains and classes. As each new domain or class arrives, exemplars are added to the memory; adaptation reflects updated posterior beliefs over latent concepts rather t"

id = "oracle"
title = "输入盲控制揭示层程序选择的 Oracle 提升虚高"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现，多项选择评测中层跳过与重复程序的 Oracle 提升可被输入盲扰动和固定字母偏移大量复现，说明共享选项顺序可能造成与输入自适应计算无关的虚假提升空间。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10368"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在 Qwen3-4B-Base 和 Llama-3.1-8B 上测试了 32 种层跳过或重复程序，覆盖 4,413 道多项选择题，并将真实程序与作用于相同位置的输入盲扰动进行比较。共享选项顺序时，输入盲控制产生的 Oracle headroom 达 10.2–19.4 个百分点，甚至超过真实层程序的 9.0 和 10.1 个百分点，固定字母偏移也能产生相近规模的提升。轮换选项后，两类方法的 headroom 均显著下降，但真实程序仍领先控制组 1.4–4.5 个百分点，其统计显著性取决于校正方式和参照设置。结果表明，仅凭答案已知条件下的选择增益，或真实程序相对控制组的排序，都不足以证明增益来自输入特定的层计算机制。"
purpose = "- 为自适应计算和层程序研究提供更严格的安慰剂控制，避免把选项位置偏差误判为输入相关计算收益。\n- Oracle 评测应加入选项随机轮换、输入盲扰动、固定字母偏移及跨提示复测，并报告多重检验校正后的结果。\n- 实用选择器的设计与评估应区分“候选菜单带来的答案扰动”与“程序对当前输入的真实适配收益”。\n- 该研究提示应审计多项选择基准中的共享选项顺序和标签偏差，以提高自适应推理结论的构念效度。"
notes = "标题：Input-Blind Controls Produce Substantial Oracle Headroom for Layer Programs in Multiple-Choice Evaluation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10368\n摘要：Adaptive computation aims to improve language-model inference by tailoring execution to each input. For layer programs, oracle evaluations use known answers to estimate the potential gain from this flexibility, before a practical selector is available. However, a gain from selection does not by itself explain why the chosen programs help. This study examines this distinction using 32 layer-skipping and repetition programs on two models and 4,413 multiple-choice items. The analysis compares their gains over a fixed action selected without the evaluation prompt with those of input-blind perturbations at the same sites, re-evaluating selections on another prompt. With shared option order, the controls give 10.2-11.8 and 15.6-19.4 percentage points of headroom on Qwen3-4B-Base and Llama-3.1-8B"

notes = "标题：AutoAdapt: Automatic Domain Discovery Enables Low-Cost Extensibility\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10349\n摘要：Instruction-tuned models are deployed into environments where domains are heterogeneous and evolve, yet adding new domains or data typically requires costly retraining. The framework automatically discovers latent domains, uses them to train per-domain Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters independently in parallel and performs parameter-free routing. Across 14 domain-specific benchmarks and GPT-4o pairwise judgements, AutoAdapt achieves parity with a LoRA adapter trained on all domains without requiring full-model retraining. We also find evidence of specialisation effect convergence across independent discovery methods. Overall, training ea"

summary = "PatchBench通过同时评估有害邻近提示的修复效果与良性邻近行为的保持程度，衡量大模型越狱安全补丁是否精准及其局部附带损害。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10276"

link = ""
content = "PatchBench从37个公开数据集的27,870条提示出发，在8个开源指令微调模型上筛选并人工验证出400个高置信度、模型特定的越狱失败案例。其 PatchBench-Local 协议为每个有害源提示生成三类局部邻居：保持恶意意图的有害变体、结构匹配的良性提示，以及复用关键有害词语的良性提示。评测分别考察补丁能否修复有害邻居，以及能否保留良性邻居上的正常行为，从而区分选择性修复与广泛的局部抑制。对四种激活引导方法的实验表明，即使 MMLU 等全局能力指标几乎不变，模型仍可能出现严重的局部良性行为退化。"
purpose = "- 为越狱修复提供比攻击成功率、拒答率和全局能力分数更精细的评估框架。\n- 开发安全补丁时应同时测试有害行为泛化修复与相似良性行为保持，避免过度拒答。\n- 激活修补方法的比较应纳入局部行为回归测试，不能仅依据全局基准判断补丁质量。\n- 可将 PatchBench-Local 的邻域构造思路用于模型更新后的定向回归测试与安全审计。"
notes = "标题：PatchBench: Measuring Collateral Damage in Activation Patching\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10276\n摘要：An LLM safety patch can pass a benchmark while still being a poor repair. This risk is especially acute for jailbreak repairs, where the goal is to correct a specific unsafe behaviour without changing unrelated behaviours. A patch may block exact evaluation prompts yet fail on close harmful variants, or suppress harmful behaviour by over-refusing benign prompts that share its wording or structure. Existing protocols primarily test whether models can be broken, while aggregate metrics (attack success, refusal rates, global capability) cannot distinguish selective repairs from broader local suppression. To address this gap, we introduce PatchBench, a benchmark of empirically observed model-specific jailbreak failures inducing actionable harmful answers. Starting from 27,870 prompts from 37 p"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出“稀缺性倒置”概念，指出当 AI 使文献综合、假设生成、仿真与分析趋于充裕后，科学进步的瓶颈将转向物理实验、可信验证、证据溯源及组织决策。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10241"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文设想机器系统在大多数可通过计算机完成的科研工作上比人类更快、更强且成本更低，科学认知由稀缺资源转变为充裕资源。作者将随之发生的瓶颈转移称为“稀缺性倒置”，并从研究选择、物理访问、结果验证和组织决策四个方面展开分析。数学、编程和算法设计可能最先经历这种变化，因为完整的科学循环可以在计算环境内运行。对于国家实验室等机构，其核心作用将转向把丰富的机器推理与受控实验、受保护数据、专家判断和可问责权威结合起来，形成可信科研成果。"
purpose = "- 为自动化科学研究提供新的资源配置视角：重点应从扩充推理能力转向实验能力、验证设施和高质量证据供给。\n- 提醒科研机构建立更严格的验证、溯源和责任机制，避免机器生成的大量假设与分析淹没有限的实验资源。\n- 可用于指导科研工作流设计，通过候选筛选、实验优先级排序和资源分配缓解新的稀缺瓶颈。\n- 为国家实验室及科研治理部门规划 AI 时代的设施建设、数据访问和组织决策机制提供参考。"
notes = "标题：When Scientific Cognition Is No Longer Scarce\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10241\n摘要：AI could change which parts of science impede progress. Consider a world in which machine systems are better, faster, and cheaper than people at most scientific work that can be done through a computer. Our question is what would limit science in that world. Literature synthesis, hypothesis generation, software development, simulation, and analysis could become abundant, while experiments, observations, well-supported conclusions, and accountable institutional au- thority remain scarce. Science would then be constrained by a different set of resources. In this paper, we call this change the scarcity inversion and consider four parts of it: selection, physical access, validation, and organizational choice. This change is arriving first in mathematics and coding/software/algorithm design, wh"

id = "reevo"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究利用 ReEvo 离线演化交叉、变异与局部搜索算子，在无需在线调用 LLM 的情况下提升安全流体天线端口选择性能。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10235"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文面向存在窃听风险的流体天线系统，将安全端口选择建模为进化搜索问题。不同于仅让 LLM 设计交叉或变异算子的 LLM-GA，所提方法通过反思式演化 ReEvo 离线生成专用的交叉、变异和局部搜索算子。生成的算子被嵌入模因搜索框架，执行阶段不再需要在线查询 LLM。在相同进化代数下，该方法获得的安全总速率高于传统遗传算法及先进 LLM-GA。"
purpose = "- 展示 LLM 可从单一遗传算子设计扩展到完整搜索算子组合的自动化设计。\n- 离线演化算子能够消除运行阶段的 LLM 调用，降低时延、成本与外部服务依赖。\n- 为通信系统优化及其他组合优化任务提供“LLM 设计、经典算法执行”的可复用范式。\n- 说明将局部搜索与进化算子联合优化，可能比仅改进交叉或变异操作获得更高性能。"
notes = "标题：Beyond LLM-GA: Secure Fluid Antenna Systems with ReEvo-Designed Memetic Algorithm\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10235\n摘要：Fluid antenna systems (FASs) offer significant spatial flexibility, yet securing them against eavesdropping is critical for practical FAS deployment in military, satellite, and internet-of-things networks. Although large language model (LLM)-assisted genetic algorithms (LLM-GAs) can address this secure FAS port selection problem, whether further algorithmic improvement is possible warrants deeper investigation. To this end, we propose a memetic algorithm based on reflective evolution (ReEvo). Unlike the state-of-the-art LLM-GAs, which design only crossover or mutation operators with an LLM, our algorithm leverages an LLM to evolve dedicated crossover, mutation, and local-search operators offline. These operators are then embedded into a memetic search framework, thereby obviating any onlin"

id = "item-147"
title = "差分隐私合成时序的序列组装策略评估"
subtopic = "数据合成"
topics = [

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究表明，差分隐私合成窗口的重叠率与加权方式会依赖预测模型显著影响 TSTR 效用，单靠连续性或保真度指标不足以选出最佳组装策略。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10222"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "差分隐私时序生成器通常输出固定长度窗口，但下游预测模型需要较长的连续训练序列，因此生成后的窗口组装会改变模型实际接触到的数据。研究系统调整窗口重叠率与加权方案，并从边界连续性、统计及时间保真度和 TSTR 预测效用等方面进行评估。基于 ETTh1、ETTm1、Weather 和 Appliances 数据集及五种预测模型的实验显示，最佳组装配置取决于预测器、重叠率和窗口加权方式的共同作用。增加重叠通常能改善边界连续性，但连续性或单项保真度的提升并不稳定对应更低的预测误差。研究还通过完整的五模型配置网格、TRTR 参照及生成器变异性分析验证了结论的稳健性。"
purpose = "- 提醒合成时序数据使用者将窗口组装视为独立且重要的数据工程超参数，而非固定的后处理步骤。\n- 选择组装配置时应直接依据目标预测模型的 TSTR 表现，不能仅依赖边界连续性或统计保真度指标。\n- 可利用 TRTR 结果作为真实数据训练上界，量化不同组装方案造成的效用损失。\n- 为兼顾隐私保护与预测性能的时序合成数据管线提供模型相关的配置原则。"
notes = "标题：Evaluating Sequence Assembly Strategies for Differentially Private Synthetic Time-Series Forecasting\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10222\n摘要：Differentially private time-series generators commonly produce fixed-length synthetic windows, whereas downstream forecasting models often require long continuous training sequences. How these windows are assembled after generation can therefore alter the effective synthetic data presented to a forecaster, even when the trained generator remains unchanged. We study this post-generation sequence assembly process by systematically varying overlap rates and window-weighting schemes and evaluating the resulting sequences in terms of boundary continuity, statistical and temporal fidelity, and Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) forecasting utility. Across four types of public datasets (ETTh1, ETTm1, Weather, and Appliances) and five forecasting models, the results reveal a clear forecaster-d"

id = "item-148"
title = "通过贝叶斯优化预训练贝叶斯优化算法"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究使用外层贝叶斯优化，在高斯过程生成的样本路径上预先优化内层贝叶斯优化算法的参数，以降低预期累积遗憾。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10186"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文关注贝叶斯优化算法对预设参数较为敏感的问题。研究者根据优化开始时已有的信息拟合高斯过程，并从中抽取样本路径作为模拟的黑箱目标函数。对于每组参数配置，框架在这些样本路径上运行贝叶斯优化算法，以经验估计其预期累积遗憾。由于参数选择本身也是黑箱优化问题，作者使用另一层贝叶斯优化进行求解；实验表明，该方法能够从候选配置中选出表现较强的参数。"
purpose = "- 为昂贵黑箱优化任务提供数据驱动的算法参数预配置方法。\n- 展示了以贝叶斯优化优化自身参数的嵌套元优化范式。\n- 可用于在实际评估预算有限时，借助高斯过程样本路径降低参数试错成本。\n- 启发根据任务初始信息定制优化策略，而非固定使用通用默认参数。"
notes = "标题：Pre-training of Bayesian Optimization Algorithm through Bayesian Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10186\n摘要：Bayesian optimization (BO) is widely used as a standard approach for expensive black-box optimization. However, BO algorithms often involve parameters that must be specified in advance, and their performance can strongly depend on these choices. We propose a framework for optimizing such parameters using sample paths drawn from a Gaussian process (GP) inferred from the information available at the start of BO. We use cumulative regret as the performance metric for a BO algorithm. By running the BO algorithm on the generated sample paths, we obtain an empirical estimate of its expected cumulative regret for a given parameter configuration. Optimizing this estimate allows us to identify parameter configurations that, given the currently available information, are expected to achieve low cumu"

id = "item-149"
title = "超越结果奖励：搜索智能体的检索信用构建与分配"
subtopic = "信用分配"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过为多步检索过程引入中间监督并优化信用分配位置，提升基于可验证奖励强化学习的搜索智能体训练效率与整体性能。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10179"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文研究搜索智能体在回答复杂多跳问题时，如何为中间检索步骤构造奖励并分配信用。传统 RLVR 主要依赖稀疏的最终结果奖励，难以判断多步搜索轨迹中各次检索的实际贡献。作者系统比较多种奖励塑形与信用分配策略，并提出融合中间信号和最终结果奖励的训练框架。在匹配训练条件下的多个基准实验表明，中间信号的类型及其分配位置都会影响训练行为，并可改善搜索智能体的综合表现。"
purpose = "- 为多步搜索智能体缓解稀疏结果奖励带来的信用分配困难。\n- 表明奖励信号的内容与分配位置应作为两个独立的训练设计维度。\n- 可为检索增强智能体、深度研究智能体及其他长轨迹工具调用任务设计过程奖励。\n- 提示后训练评估不应只关注最终正确率，还应分析各检索步骤对结果的边际贡献。"
notes = "标题：Beyond Outcome Rewards: Constructing and Assigning Retrieval Credit for Search Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10179\n摘要：Search agents enable Large Language Models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve and use information for complex multi-hop questions. Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) offers a promising approach for post-training such agents, but its reliance on sparse, outcome-based supervision can make credit assignment difficult and limit learning efficiency. In this paper, we systematically investigate how intermediate supervision can improve reinforcement learning for search agents. We study a range of reward-shaping and credit-assignment strategies that provide learning signals from intermediate retrieval steps. Building on these insights, we develop a training framework that combines intermediate signals with final outcome rewards to improve learning from multi-step search trajectories"


[[items]]
id = "mascope-llm"
title = "MAScope：基于通信拓扑的多智能体 LLM 故障诊断"
subtopic = "多智能体协作风险"

keywords = [

    "通信拓扑",

    "执行轨迹",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "MAScope从无拓扑标签的执行轨迹中恢复多智能体通信结构，并以拓扑为条件识别协调故障，在接近强模型诊断效果的同时显著降低成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10126"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，多智能体 LLM 的相似执行异常可能源于信息传递、使用或验证环节的不同故障，而通信拓扑可提供关键结构线索。MAScope采用两阶段设计：TSE依据消息证据从异构执行轨迹中恢复交互图，TC-Judge结合轨迹、预测拓扑、经验故障先验及拓扑特定模式进行分类。实验发现通信拓扑与故障类型显著相关（χ²=409.9，p=1.2×10⁻⁷⁰）；在851条MAST-clean轨迹上，拓扑上下文将gpt-mini的Macro-F1从0.173提升至最高0.350，使用预测拓扑时达到0.346。对于固定编排结构，拓扑可跨执行复用，1000条轨迹上的预计总成本约为重复使用gpt-5.4诊断的6%。"
purpose = "- 将通信拓扑作为多智能体故障诊断的显式条件，有助于区分表象相近但根因不同的协调失败。\n- 可从执行消息中自动恢复交互图，适用于缺少显式拓扑标签的现有智能体系统。\n- 固定编排场景下可复用一次提取的拓扑，以较弱、低成本模型逼近强模型的诊断效果。\n- 为多智能体系统的轨迹审计、故障归因和运行时监控提供结构感知方法。"
notes = "标题：Know the Shape, Find the Fault: Topology-Conditioned Diagnosis of Multi-Agent LLM Failures\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10126\n摘要：Multi-agent LLM systems coordinate task execution through exchanges of information among agents. When coordination breaks down, similar symptoms in execution traces can reflect different problems in how information is passed, used, or verified. Communication topology captures how agents exchange information and provides structural cues for distinguishing coordination failure modes. Using these cues for diagnosis requires establishing how topology relates to failure patterns and recovering the relevant structure from execution traces that lack explicit topology labels. We analyze the relationship between communication topology and failure patterns and introduce MAScope, a two-stage framework for topology-conditioned diagnosis. Its Trace Structural Extractor TSE recovers communication topolo"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ExperienceIndex将历史推理轨迹转化为面向具体知识载体的持久经验索引，帮助智能体更完整地检索相关材料，并同步提升回答质量与在线效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10091"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "ExperienceIndex面向法院案例、科学文献等共享知识载体上的知识密集型任务，弥补现有智能体记忆偏重用户偏好、事实属性或抽象推理模式的不足。它保存两类互补经验：概括单个载体对历史任务贡献的单载体经验，以及编码推理中所发现结构关系的载体对经验。该方法以轻量级中间件集成到不同搜索框架中，通过经验检索引导智能体找到更完整的相关材料集合。实验显示，其回答质量最高提升11.0分，在线成本最高降低50.5%，且支持跨任务经验迁移以及强模型向弱模型的经验传递。"
purpose = "- 为知识密集型智能体构建可跨任务复用、与具体文档或数据对象绑定的长期记忆。\n- 利用历史推理发现的载体关系改善检索召回完整性，减少重复搜索与在线推理成本。\n- 可将强模型积累的经验提供给弱模型，形成无需直接复制完整轨迹的教师—学生能力迁移机制。\n- 启示记忆系统应同时保存对象级知识与对象间结构，而非仅压缩用户偏好或通用推理模板。"
notes = "标题：ExperienceIndex: Artifact-Grounded Memory\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10091\n摘要：Knowledge-intensive tasks require answering many questions by reasoning about a shared corpus of artifacts (e.g., court cases, or scientific literature). As humans interact with these corpora, they naturally accumulate experiential knowledge about artifacts, enabling them to quickly identify the complete set of relevant artifacts for each new task. However, existing AI agents lack appropriate memory solutions to build or reuse such artifact-grounded experience, leading to lower answer quality and higher online cost. Existing memory solutions extract and reuse information from prior task-solving traces, but they primarily focus on user preferences, factual attributes, or abstract reasoning patterns rather than persistent artifact-specific knowledge. We introduce ExperienceIndex, a novel exp"


[[items]]
id = "hgp"
title = "HGP：基于混合图存储的端侧个性化智能体记忆"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",
    "智能体",
    "端侧部署",
    "状态追踪",
    "知识图谱",
]
research = [
    "MemoryEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "长期记忆",
    "状态追踪",
    "知识图谱",
    "端侧部署",
    "智能体",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "HGP以混合图分别组织情景、语义和程序记忆，并结合状态轨迹与轻量路由分类器，实现准确且适合端侧部署的个性化智能体记忆。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10071"
code = "https://github.com/Ouan6/HGP-"
dataset = "https://github.com/Ouan6/HGP-"
link = ""
content = "HGP针对个性化交互中长期轨迹类型异构、关系复杂且包含隐式约束的问题，避免使用单一向量表示混合不同记忆类型。框架将情景记忆、语义记忆和程序记忆分别构造成图，并通过轻量级自增强分类器完成个性化记忆路由。它还将工作记忆提取为状态轨迹，以刻画当前状态及隐式约束，支持更可靠的决策。轻量分类器减少了对大模型的调用，图存储则支持精确检索和用户画像的增量更新；在PAL-Set方案选择任务上，HGP取得35.58的S-score，比最强基线高近7分。"
purpose = "- 为端侧个性化智能体提供兼顾记忆类型区分、关系建模与低调用成本的长期记忆架构。\n- 图结构可保留记忆实体之间的关系，缓解单向量存储造成的类型混淆和结构信息损失。\n- 将工作记忆表示为状态轨迹，有助于智能体识别交互中的隐式约束并保持决策一致性。\n- 轻量级路由器与增量用户画像机制可用于构建隐私友好、持续适应的本地智能体。"
notes = "标题：HGP:An on-device personalized agent memory via hybrid graph storage\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10071\n摘要：LLM-based agents face challenges in personalized interactive tasks due to heterogeneous, multi-typed, and implicitly constrained long-term traces. Existing memory mechanisms struggle with accurate routing and retrieval, especially on-device where personalization is critical. Most methods use single-vector representations, blurring type distinctions and relational structure. We propose HGP, a hybrid graph memory framework. HGP employs a lightweight self-enhancement classifier for personalized memory routing and constructs episodic, semantic, and procedural memories as graphs. It also extracts working memory as a state trajectory to capture current state and implicit constraints, ensuring reliable decision-making. The classifier reduces large-model calls, enabling on-device deployment, while"


[[items]]
id = "item-153"
title = "理解力审计：降低自动化 AI 研发风险"
subtopic = "评测审计"
topics = [
    "安全审计",
    "人工智能安全治理",
    "人在回路",
    "自动化科学研究",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
]
research = [
    "ResearchEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "评测审计",
    "安全审计",
    "自动化科学研究",
    "人工智能辅助编程",
    "人在回路",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "论文提出“理解力审计”机制，要求负责人向独立审计员证明其理解 AI 生成的研发成果，并将审计结果作为继续开发或使用的前置门槛。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10064"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，前沿 AI 实验室的大量代码已由 AI 自动生成，而人类监督和理解程度可能未能同步提升。作者提出理解力审计，由研发负责人向独立审计员解释具体贡献，并通过分级报告评估其实际理解水平。若负责人未能证明充分理解，相关贡献的开发应暂停直至完成补救，重复失败则触发逐级加重的后果。对领先开源 AI 项目的分析还发现，代码产出增加的同时，单位代码对应的人类审查评论率下降，自动化账号的审查率尤其低。"
purpose = "- 为自动化 AI 研发建立以“人类确实理解”为核心的过程保障机制。\n- 将理解证明设为预先承诺的开发门槛，避免在缺乏有效监督时持续扩大高风险系统。\n- 可通过实验室内嵌的独立审计员、分级报告和失败补救流程落地。\n- 提醒组织除统计 AI 生成代码比例外，还应持续监测人类审查密度与责任人的理解能力。"
notes = "标题：Comprehension Audits to Mitigate Risks from Automated AI Research\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10064\n摘要：AI is already writing a majority of code for frontier AI labs. This creates a safety risk if there is insufficient human oversight. Existing work proposes minimum comprehension thresholds and unaided checks to mitigate this. To our knowledge, however, there is currently no published frontier-AI assurance regime that requires demonstrated evidence that the responsible humans understand what they are building as a precommitted condition for continuing development or usage. We propose comprehension audits, a novel development-process assurance mechanism in which the responsible people explain R&D contributions to auditors to demonstrate understanding. With independent administration and graded reports, they provide a gate: development of a contribution stops based on a failure to demonstrate "


[[items]]
id = "item-154"
title = "工具调用智能体的故障检测与恢复：显性失败与静默失败"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "工具调用",
    "智能体",
    "执行恢复",
    "故障诊断与恢复",
]
research = [
    "HarnessEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "模型可靠性",
    "工具调用",
    "故障诊断与恢复",
    "故障注入",
    "执行恢复",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过可控故障注入评估工具调用智能体，发现智能体主要依赖显式错误通道，往往无法识别格式正常但内容错误的静默故障。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10062"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在函数调用基准的可执行环境中加入故障注入层，针对 24 个多步任务、6 个模型开展 1,920 次试验，考察故障发现、计划调整、任务恢复与重复调用行为。工具返回显式错误时，智能体有 91.3% 的概率将其识别为问题；面对看似合理的错误结果时，该比例仅为 58.8%，说明静默故障更容易穿透智能体的检查机制。推理模型相较对应的指令模型更少发现故障，但更频繁改变计划，最终恢复率并无显著改善；缺失工具会明显降低恢复率，而超时、模式漂移和结果损坏的影响未超出无故障运行的随机波动。故障发生后，智能体有时会连续三次以上调用同一工具，且要求逐项检查结果的简单提示并未提升检测能力，表明其更依赖错误信号而非返回内容本身。"
purpose = "- 为工具调用智能体评测补充故障注入、静默错误检测和恢复能力等真实部署指标，而不只考察正常环境下的任务完成率。\n- 设计工具输出验证器时，应检查内容语义、跨步骤一致性和结果可信度，避免仅依赖状态码或异常信息。\n- 推理模型和简单检查提示不能自动解决静默失败问题，需要引入独立验证、重试上限、替代工具与状态回滚机制。\n- 评估故障影响时应纳入智能体自身的随机运行基线，以免把正常的轨迹波动误判为故障造成的性能下降。"
notes = "标题：Loud Failures, Quiet Failures: Fault Detection and Recovery in Tool-Using Language Model Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10062\n摘要：Tool-using agents are usually scored on whether they finish a task while the tools work. Deployments are less forgiving: services time out, endpoints disappear, parameter names change, and results come back well formed but wrong. Prior work has shown that language models over-trust tool outputs that fail silently; we ask how that over-trust plays out across the stages of failure handling in multi-turn agents. Wrapping the executable environments of an established function-calling benchmark in a fault-injection layer, we inject one of four typed faults at a controlled point in the trajectory and record whether the agent notices, changes plan, recovers the task, or repeats itself. Six models from three families, half of them reasoning variants, ran 1,920 trials over 24 multi-step tasks. Agen"


[[items]]
id = "agenttime"
title = "AgentTime：智能体能否估计并控制自身运行时间"
subtopic = "智能体评测"
topics = [
    "智能体评估",
    "智能体",
    "长程任务",

    "执行反馈",
]

]
keywords = [
    "智能体评测",
    "长程任务执行",
    "时间控制",
    "执行反馈",
    "模型可靠性",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "AgentTime 基准通过 222 项任务评估智能体遵循指定时长、预测运行时间及回顾估计耗时的能力，揭示任务完成能力并不等同于可靠的时间控制能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09944"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "AgentTime 收录来自 18 个来源的 222 项任务，覆盖编程、计算机操作、智能体工作和自动化研究等场景。基准分别测试智能体能否按照约一分钟至数天的指定时长持续工作、事前预测自然运行时间，以及事后估计实际耗时。不同系统的时长遵循能力差异显著：Claude Code 中的 Fable 5.1 典型偏差为目标时长的 2.9 倍，而 Codex 中的 GPT-6 Astra 为 1.2 倍。仅匹配目标时长并不代表持续执行有效工作，部分 Astra 运行在提前完成后明确进入休眠；移除时间信息还会显著降低智能体的事后耗时估计准确性。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体提供独立于任务成功率的运行时控制评估维度。\n- 提醒开发者检查智能体是否真正持续工作，避免以休眠或空转方式表面满足时长要求。\n- 可用于评估智能体的时间感知、执行调度与长期自主运行可靠性。\n- 表明安全部署长时运行智能体时，需要同时验证任务能力和时间控制能力。"
notes = "标题：AgentTime: Can Agents Estimate and Control Their Own Runtime?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09944\n摘要：An essential control of AI agents is their ability to manage runtime. This ability requires a sense of time-awareness, to predict and estimate wall-clock time and to control their own actions. Prior work has focused on time-awareness, but duration-following and control in native agent harnesses remain unexplored. We present AgentTime, a benchmark for testing whether agents can work for a requested duration, predict their runtime, and estimate elapsed time afterward. It comprises 222 tasks from 18 sources spanning coding, computer use, agentic work, and automated research. Duration-following experiments append a single instruction specifying how long to work, with requests ranging from about a minute to multiple days. Accuracy on these instructions varies substantially: Fable 5.1 in Claude "


[[items]]
id = "hvac"
title = "决策模型诊断 HVAC 故障：推理需求、物理表征与偏移鲁棒性"
subtopic = "模型可靠性"
topics = [
    "人工智能应用",
    "故障诊断与恢复",
    "跨域泛化",
    "不确定性估计",
    "模型评估",
]
research = [
    "JevEvolve",
]
keywords = [
    "模型可靠性",
    "故障诊断与恢复",
    "不确定性估计",
    "分布偏移",
    "跨域泛化",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过真实 HVAC 设备的跨季节、跨控制配置和跨建筑测试，界定了决策模型 Jev 的故障诊断能力、所需物理表征及其概率在分布偏移下的可靠性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09937"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究汇集四个公开真实设备数据集中的 128 个故障日，并按照诊断所需推理程度对故障分级。实验比较原始数据、物理特征和 Brick 拓扑三种输入表征，让决策模型 Jev、开放语言模型与监督模型接受九项跨季节、控制配置或建筑的偏移测试。使用物理特征时，Jev 和较大的开放模型能够诊断证据集中于单一特征的故障，但难以处理需要运行上下文的故障。分布偏移下，决策模型保持了准确率与校准水平，而监督模型的宏平均 F1 下降 0.33；不过模型概率仍需校正，故障检测能力也较弱。"
purpose = "- 为 HVAC 故障诊断划定决策模型的适用边界，避免将其用于高度依赖运行上下文的故障。\n- 建议采用“代码计算物理特征、模型排序候选故障、操作员最终核验”的人机协同工作流。\n- 跨建筑部署时应同时评估准确率与概率校准，并对输出概率实施后处理校正。\n- 物理特征工程比直接输入原始数据或仅提供拓扑信息更可能提升可迁移诊断能力。"
notes = "标题：Where Can a Decision Model Diagnose HVAC Faults? Reasoning Demand, Physical Representation, and Robustness Under Shift\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09937\n摘要：Artificial intelligence supports building operations in several forms, each with its own barrier. Expert rules must be tuned for every system, supervised models need labeled data that buildings rarely record, and language models return free text that requires human-in-the-loop checking, since their stated confidence is unreliable. A newer kind of pretrained model, here called a decision model, returns a probability for every allowed answer, so one model could serve many decisions without training. This study answers three open questions for fault diagnosis in heating, ventilation, and air-conditioning systems: which decisions such a model can make, what input it needs, and whether its probabilities hold when conditions change. On 128 fault days from four public datasets of real equipment, "


[[items]]
id = "rollverify-rollout"
title = "RollVerify：兼顾长尾 Rollout 强化学习的效率与精度"
subtopic = "强化学习"
topics = [
    "强化学习",
    "模型训练",
    "系统优化",
    "轨迹验证",
    "成本优化",
]
research = [
    "EvolveLRM",
]
keywords = [
    "强化学习",
    "轨迹验证",
    "模型训练",
    "系统优化",
    "在策略训练",
]
score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "RollVerify 通过度量、验证并修复部分生成轨迹，在保留异步部分 Rollout 效率优势的同时实现接近完全在策略训练的准确率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09914"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "RollVerify 面向长上下文强化学习中 Rollout 长度呈长尾分布、同步训练易产生 GPU 空泡的问题，构建了轻量级部分 Rollout 框架。该方法提出离策略偏移指标 OPS，用于量化部分生成轨迹相对当前策略的偏离程度。框架在样本进入训练前执行序列级与词元级验证，并识别、截断轨迹中的无效后缀，以主动修复陈旧的离策略样本。数学推理、工具辅助数学推理及初步代码生成实验表明，该方法可降低训练成本，同时保持与完全在策略训练相当的准确率。"
purpose = "- 为长尾 Rollout 导致的 GPU 利用率下降提供兼顾吞吐量与模型精度的解决方案。\n- 展示了相比训练阶段被动重加权，在数据进入训练前主动验证和修复离策略轨迹的价值。\n- OPS 可作为部分 Rollout 系统中的轨迹质量控制信号，指导无效后缀截断与样本筛选。\n- 可用于扩展数学推理、工具调用和代码生成等需要大规模长轨迹强化学习的训练任务。"
notes = "标题：RollVerify: Bridging Efficiency and Accuracy in Long-Tail Rollout Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09914\n摘要：Reinforcement learning is crucial for improving large language models' reasoning and generalization. It relies on massive rollouts whose lengths become increasingly long-tailed as context windows grow. In on-policy training, these long-tail rollouts can result in GPU bubbles, reducing system utilization and limiting RL scalability. Asynchronous or partial-rollout methods improve throughput by relaxing synchronization, but inevitably introduce stale off-policy samples (trajectories) that may hurt final accuracy. Existing approaches mainly mitigate this off-policy issue by reweighting off-policy samples during training, yet they can still leave a performance gap compared to fully on-policy training. In this work, rather than passively reweighting samples during training, we propose RollVerif"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "LiveMACEBench以实时金融市场为动态测试环境，通过结果指标与完整决策轨迹诊断相结合，揭示持续型LLM智能体的收益表现与实际机制能力之间存在显著差距。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09872"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "LiveMACEBench利用持续变化的实时金融市场，评估LLM智能体在连续30天运行中的长期行为。研究让五个前沿模型在统一配置下使用工具、持久记忆、规则遵循和多智能体协作机制，并保留完整决策轨迹。评测同时考察实际收益和针对各项机制的过程诊断指标，发现收益相近的智能体可能呈现截然不同的机制使用模式。结果表明，机制是否可用、智能体能否有效使用机制，以及最终任务表现是不可互换的能力度量。"
purpose = "- 为动态环境中的持续型智能体提供兼顾结果与执行过程的评测范式。\n- 提醒研究者不能仅凭金融收益判断智能体能力，应结合完整轨迹定位具体机制瓶颈。\n- 可用于比较工具调用、持久记忆、规则遵循和多智能体协作机制的真实使用效果。\n- 启发将自然演化的真实环境从排行榜转化为智能体能力诊断平台。"
notes = "标题：LiveMACE: Process-Aware Evaluation of LLM Agent Capabilities in Evolving Markets\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09872\n摘要：Evaluating agents by outcomes alone can obscure the capabilities that produce them. This problem is especially pronounced in evolving environments, where outcomes reflect a closed-loop interaction between agent behavior and changing external conditions. We introduce LiveMACEBench, a process-aware benchmark that uses live financial markets as a naturally evolving testbed for persistent LLM agents. Five frontier LLMs operate along continuous trajectories under matched Tool Use, Persistent Memory, Rule Following, and Multi-Agent Collaboration configurations. We evaluate them through both realized outcomes and mechanism-specific diagnostics derived from complete decision traces. Across 30 days of live evaluation, we find a pronounced outcome-capability gap: realized returns often diverge from "

content = "Caddie训练一个在任务执行过程中提供自然语言分析与建议的批评器，无需步骤级标签或参考批评文本，而是直接根据智能体最终是否成功进行优化。训练采用强化学习并冻结基础模型，以便将能力集中在顾问模型的反馈策略上。基于多跳问答训练的Qwen3-4B批评器可提升四种不同规模与架构基础模型的成功率，其中三种模型未参与训练。在MuSiQue上，该批评器使Qwen3-4B的成功率提高超过25个百分点，并在未经额外训练的τ³、DeepDive等域外交互基准上同样取得增益。实验还表明，智能体能够在推理时自主判断何时向批评器求助。"
purpose = "- 展示了仅凭任务级成败信号训练自然语言顾问的可行性，可降低步骤标注和人工批评数据的成本。\n- 批评器对未见过的基础模型与任务领域仍能迁移，可作为不同智能体共享的外部反馈模块。\n- 将基础模型冻结并单独优化顾问，为模块化改进智能体提供了低侵入性的训练路径。\n- 智能体自主决定求助时机的机制，可用于在性能收益与额外推理成本之间进行动态权衡。"
notes = "标题：Training Advisors for LLM Agents from Task Outcomes\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09858\n摘要：Large language model agents tackle multi-step tasks by interleaving reasoning and tool calls with observations from the environment. Prior work has shown that natural-language feedback can help these agents revise their decisions during task execution. We introduce Caddie, a method for training critics to provide natural-language analysis and advice as agents work through a task. Unlike approaches that rely on step-level labels or reference critiques, Caddie learns from whether the agent ultimately succeeds after receiving the critic's feedback. We optimize the critic through reinforcement learning while keeping the base model frozen. Trained on multi-hop question answering with a single base model, our Qwen3-4B critic improves success rates across four base models of different scales and "

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09824"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "论文将多智能体同质化定义为智能体在反复交互中逐渐趋向相似行为，并以多数服从、极端极化和变化惯性三项指标加以量化。实验覆盖代码生成、招聘和科学同行评审，表明同质化会掩盖并放大相关代码错误、延续已移除智能体的偏见，并造成不同研究领域间评价标准不均。研究认为，仅用总体任务性能评估多智能体系统会遗漏这类由交互动力学引发的系统性失效。增加采样随机性或混用不同模型等简单多样化方法未能有效降低风险，说明表面上的智能体多样性并不等于行为与判断的独立性。"
purpose = "- 将同质化纳入多智能体系统的安全评估，避免只关注平均性能或最终任务成功率。\n- 可用多数服从、极端极化和变化惯性指标审计智能体间的意见收敛与错误相关性。\n- 在代码审查、招聘和同行评审等高风险场景中，应监测偏见及错误是否通过交互被持续放大。\n- 设计多智能体协作机制时，需要追求有效的认知与行为多样性，而非仅增加采样随机性或模型种类。"
notes = "标题：Homogenization in Multi-Agent Systems\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09824\n摘要：Multi-agent systems (MAS) leverage interactions between agents to perform complex tasks. Despite their success, we show that these interactions can also lead to homogenization, i.e., agents converging to similar behaviors. Homogenization in MAS can reduce agent diversity and reinforce shared failures. In this paper, we operationalize homogenization using three metrics: conformity to the majority, polarization towards extremes, and growing inertia against changes over subsequent interactions. We evaluate homogenization in MAS for code generation, hiring, and scientific peer review. Across these tasks, we show that homogenization translates to concrete downstream risks: in code generation, it hides and amplifies correlated errors which can create systemic vulnerabilities; in hiring, it allow"

summary = "MIRROR通过参考揭示的在策略自蒸馏，让模型沿自身生成轨迹内化用户偏好，并在提升个性化内容质量的同时减少灾难性遗忘。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09795"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "MIRROR不再直接模仿参考文本中的词元，而是让模型在自身生成轨迹上，对齐无参考状态与参考条件状态下的下一词元分布，从而将个性化目标由措辞复刻转向用户偏好内化。该方法使用“参考揭示的在策略反思”实施自蒸馏，使模型保持自主生成分布的同时吸收参考内容所表达的偏好。扩展组件MIRROR-F进一步选择性监督信息量较高的参考词元，以增强内容生成质量并保留用户特有的表达方式。在三个个性化生成基准、两种模型规模及多类评估中，MIRROR与MIRROR-F取得领先的综合个性化表现，并在三个未见任务上比SFT基线表现出更少的灾难性遗忘。"
purpose = "- 为个性化微调提供一种区别于参考答案模仿的训练范式，更适合学习稳定的用户偏好。\n- 表明在策略分布对齐可兼顾个性化表达与内容质量，减少对参考文本表面措辞的过拟合。\n- MIRROR-F的信息词元选择机制可作为通用插件，用于强化高价值偏好信号。\n- 对需要跨任务保持基础能力的个性化助手具有启示，可降低传统SFT导致的灾难性遗忘。"
notes = "标题：MIRROR: From Imitation to Internalization in LLM Personalization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09795\n摘要：The demand for personalized LLMs is shifting from style imitation toward content quality. We investigate whether self-distillation can bridge this gap in existing fine-tuning paradigm. To address this limitation, we introduce MIRROR(Meta- personalization by Internalizing Reference-Revealed On-policy Reflections), a novel self-distillation framework that shifts LLM personalization from imitation toward preference internalization. First, we replace reference-token imitation with reference-revealed on-policy self-distillation, aligning the model's next-token distributions along its own generation trajectories with those of its reference-conditioned self, thereby internalizing user preferences rather than reproducing reference wording.Second, we introduce MIRROR-F, a focal plug-in that augment"

content = "LatentGRM利用评分标准引导的语义分块、压缩与重构，将逐词生成的文本评估转化为紧凑的连续潜在轨迹，并直接完成成对偏好判断。独立解释器可离线重建评估文本，用于检查压缩轨迹保留的语义和评价信息。在相同训练数据与骨干模型下，4B和8B版本的整体偏好准确率可与显式生成评价文本的SFT评判模型竞争。LatentGRM-8B在四类基准上将评估轨迹压缩8.9—9.2倍，并在vote@5设置下将总评判推理时间缩短6.1—7.0倍；评分准则干预实验还表明，潜在序列保留了依赖具体评价维度的偏好信息。"
purpose = "- 为奖励模型和模型评判器提供无需生成完整文本评语的低成本推理方案。\n- 表明结构化评分标准可作为潜空间压缩的监督信号，在压缩计算轨迹的同时保留多准则判断能力。\n- 独立文本解释器兼顾在线评判效率与离线可审计性，可用于分析潜在评价轨迹中的语义信息。\n- 适合需要多次投票或大规模候选排序的后训练流程，有望显著降低偏好标注、拒绝采样和策略优化成本。"
notes = "标题：Judging in Latent Space: Efficient Generative Reward Modeling via Semantics-Preserving Compression\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09788\n摘要：Reward modeling often requires jointly representing and reasoning over multiple evaluation criteria, yet verbalizing this process token by token can incur substantial inference cost. We introduce LatentGRM, a latent evaluation framework built on semantic chunking, compression, and reconstruction. By using the structure of rubric-guided evaluations to guide compression, LatentGRM learns compact continuous trajectories that support autonomous pairwise judgments without generating textual assessments. A separate interpreter reconstructs evaluation text from these trajectories, providing an offline view of the information retained under compression. Under matched training data and backb"

content = "研究提出 Sequential Isolation Methodology，在隔离执行和系统状态的同时系统性改变并发负载，以降低 LLM 推理测量的跨轮次方差。实验在 NVIDIA A100 80GB 与 vLLM 0.9.1 上测试三个开源模型，覆盖六种上下文长度、八档并发水平，并对每项配置重复测量五次。最终协议将平均变异系数由控制最弱阶段的 15.2% 降至 2.2%，且 144 项配置中有 113 项的 P50 首词延迟变异系数低于 3%。测试还发现并发用户数从 200 增至 500 时出现明显延迟跃迁，并给出了考虑 API 定价敏感性的成本盈亏平衡模型；基础设施即代码和基准脚本可支持实验复现。"
purpose = "- 为 LLM 推理引擎、模型版本和部署配置建立低方差的性能回归测试流程。\n- 提醒评测者隔离执行顺序与系统状态，并以多次重复测量的 TTFT 分布和变异系数判断结果稳定性。\n- 可用于识别并发扩展中的延迟拐点，辅助容量规划、服务等级目标设定及自托管与 API 调用的成本比较。\n- 该协议适合可复现的相对比较，不应被直接用于预测缺乏控制的生产流量表现。"
notes = "标题：Reproducible LLM Inference Benchmarking: A Sequential Isolation Protocol for Regression Testing\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09778\n摘要：Reproducible benchmarking of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is challenging because repeated measurements can vary with execution and system state. We present the Sequential Isolation Methodology, a controlled benchmarking and regression-testing protocol designed to reduce between-run measurement variance while deliberately varying workload concurrency. We evaluate three representative open-source LLMs on an NVIDIA A100 80GB GPU using vLLM 0.9.1 across six context sizes and eight concurrency levels, with five repetitions per configuration. content = "论文提出解耦架构 AO-DA，在同一 INT4 基座模型上通过可热切换的 LoRA 适配器，将逻辑推理“做什么”与角色化表达“怎么说”分成两条推理路径。逻辑路径只接收当前核心输入并生成可验证的结构化 Micro-State，角色路径则结合完整对话历史将其转化为符合设定的回答。在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 与 Gemma-3-4B-it 的 480 次实验中，解耦逻辑路径的提示长度固定且跨污染级别输出逐字节一致，而混合单路径的逻辑得分随污染增加显著下降。专用适配器与结构化格式在 8B 模型上表现出更强鲁棒性，但在 4B 模型上仍会与单路径方案一同崩溃；代价是主题首轮增加一次解码，不过角色适配器可在约 1.7 毫秒内热切换。"
purpose = "- 将任务状态计算与自然语言呈现分离，可从架构层面减少角色指令、历史噪声和误导信息对核心推理的干扰。\n- Micro-State 可作为验证、审计和执行前检查的中间接口，提升端侧智能体的可控性与可靠性。\n- LoRA 热切换允许多个角色复用同一逻辑结果，适合资源受限设备上的个性化智能体部署。\n- 实验显示架构解耦不能完全弥补模型容量不足，落地时仍需评估基座规模、额外解码延迟与鲁棒性收益。"
notes = "标题：Decoupling Logic from Persona: Structural Immunity of Edge LLM Agents to Context Pollution\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09772\n摘要：Small language-model agents on edge devices must hold a persona and reason correctly at once, inside one context window that fills with conversational history and persona instructions. We study what happens to the logical part of such an agent when that history is long, misleading and persona-heavy (persona-logic interference), and present a Decoupling Architecture (AO-DA) that separates logical inference (\"What\") from persona expression (\"How\") into two inference paths on one INT4 base model with hot-swappable LoRA adapters. The logic path receives only the core turn and emits a verifiable structured state (Micro-State); the persona path renders it in character with the full history. We also observe logit-scale saturation when training with synthetic hard negatives and a learnable temperature, and find that fixing the temperature improves downstream performance. Using this geometric analysis we propose SNAP, which generates intra-modal hard negatives that never involve the "

content = "论文通过元分析发现，自动评判已成为自然语言生成中不确定性量化评估的主流做法，但其结果很少与人工判断进行验证。作者在问答实验中比较了4个大语言模型、人工与自动评判结果以及7种常用不确定性量化方法。实验表明，评判器自身的准确率只能粗略预测其错误对不确定性量化评估可靠性的实际影响。不同类别的评判错误与模型置信度存在特定相关模式，导致信息量较高的不确定性量化方法反而更容易被误判结果扭曲。"
purpose = "- 提醒研究者不能仅凭自动评判器的总体准确率判断不确定性量化评估是否可靠。\n- 建议按错误类别分析评判结果与置信度之间的相关性，并使用人工标注对关键结论进行校验。\n- 为选择性预测、拒答机制和幻觉规避系统设计更稳健的评估协议提供依据。\n- 可用于审计以大模型充当评判器的置信度评估流程，识别被聚合指标掩盖的系统性偏差。"
notes = "标题：A Tale of Two Error Categories: Exploring Concealed Trade-Offs in the Errors of Automated Judges in Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantifiers\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09693\n摘要：The wide adoption of LLMs across broad NLG applications heightens the importance of providing users with the means to avert errors and hallucinations. Uncertainty quantification is poised to fill that gap; with low uncertainty (high confidence), as a proxy for correctness, allowing users to be selective (e.g., reject low-confidence, likely incorrect responses). Correlation between confidence and correctness then serves as a useful criterion for evaluation of uncertainty quantifiers (UQs). But in NLG, where diverse responses can be adequate to a prompt, obtaining reliable correctness judgements is not simple, especially without human intervention. Errors in automated judgement are hardly avoidable and known to diminish the reliability of evaluation protocols (Santilli et al., 2025; Ielansky"

summary = "PersonTTS通过复用跨用户的策略搜索经验，为准确率、延迟和推理成本等个性化联合要求高效发现可执行的测试时扩展控制器。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09684"
code = ""

content = "- 论文将个性化测试时扩展定义为控制器发现问题，目标是最大化用户对准确率、延迟与推理成本要求的联合满足率。\n- PersonTTS采用摊销式智能体策略发现框架，通过需求匹配的控制器初始化和源策略蒸馏得到的程序化指导复用历史搜索经验。\n- 框架仍会针对目标用户画像评估每个候选控制器，以避免跨用户经验复用造成策略与实际需求错配。\n- AIME和HMMT实验显示，在相同候选评估预算下，PersonTTS能提升未见用户及留出题目的要求满足率，并降低策略发现所需的时间与成本。"
purpose = "- 将测试时扩展从单一准确率—成本或准确率—延迟优化，拓展为面向多维用户约束的联合策略优化。\n- 为需要按用户服务等级动态分配推理预算的系统提供可执行控制器发现方案。\n- 表明跨用户复用搜索经验可以摊销智能体策略发现开销，同时保留目标场景评估以保障可靠性。"
notes = "标题：From Pareto to Preference: Personalized Test-Time Scaling via Amortized Agentic Policy Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09684\n摘要：Test-time scaling (TTS) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by allocating additional inference computation. Existing approaches to improving TTS efficiency largely optimize accuracy against one resource dimension at a time, advancing either the accuracy--cost or accuracy--latency Pareto frontier. Yet user requirements are multidimensional: users may specify accuracy, latency, and inference-cost requirements jointly, and different requirements can favor different controllers. We formulate Personalized Test-Time Scaling as discovering executable controllers that maximize the joint satisfaction rate of user-specific requirements. To reduce the overhead of repeated policy discovery for new user profiles, we propose PersonTTS, an amortized agentic policy-discovery frame"

summary = "CoCo-Prover通过两级证明图和基于成本的智能体路由，在Lean 4程序验证任务上同时提升证明成功率并降低最多30.9%的成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09681"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "CoCo-Prover将成本高效的程序证明建模为受成本约束的元层决策问题，关注成功率与计算开销之间的帕累托前沿。系统使用声明内部的AND/OR证明超图与声明之间的引理依赖图，形成两级证明图，并以拓扑遍历选择待解决目标。对于每个目标，智能体路由器把不同专家及其配置视为独立计价的有限计算，根据累积证据演化路由规则并决定调用哪些动作。在CLEVER、VERINA、AlgoVeri、NTP4VC和Vero五个Lean 4基准上，该方法均取得最佳求解率，并相较最强基线最多节省30.9%的成本。"
purpose = "- 为包含大量相互依赖证明义务的软件仓库提供成功率与成本联合优化方案。\n- 展示证明图的符号化目标选择与LLM专家智能体路由相结合的系统设计。\n- 可用于提高LLM生成代码的机器可验证性，并控制仓库级验证的推理预算。\n- 提示自动定理证明评测应同时报告求解率、调用成本及成功率—成本前沿，而非只比较通过率。"
notes = "标题：Cost-Efficient Theorem Proving via Agent Orchestration in Program Verification\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09681\n摘要：Program verification establishes software correctness through machine-checkable proofs constructed in theorem provers. It's a guarantee especially valuable for code generated by large language models (LLMs), which is fluent but carries no assurance of correctness. Almost all existing provers, however, pursue pass rates alone at whatever sampling or search budget it takes, and overlook the success-vs-cost frontier; yet real software often carries hundreds of interdependent proof obligations, so what matters at scale is not whether one theorem can be proved, but how many can be proved economically. We introduce CoCo-Prover, which formalizes cost-efficient program proving as metalevel decision-making under cost, grounded on two-level proof graphs: an AND/OR proof hypergraph within each declar"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09667"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "研究对六种推理模型开展行为测试，发现它们在基于标识符做选择时会使用阈值和整除规则，而非真正独立随机抽样。对于 GPT-6 Sol 和 Gemini 3.8 Flash，单智能体测量能够预测共享标识符下的群体相关参与，以及不同标识符共享时间戳位时的选择偏差。改变日期、格式和标识符标签会影响规律是否成立；明确要求独立随机化虽能降低相关性，却无法将其消除。在客户请求抽样审查实验中，仅观察目标选择率会掩盖输入依赖偏差与可预测性，而直接提供随机抽样结果可使四种模型较好遵循随机选择。"
purpose = "- 智能体随机选择评测不应只检查总体命中率，还应检测跨样本偏差、跨智能体相关性和结果可预测性。\n- 审计与资源分配系统应避免让模型直接从共享标识符、时间戳等结构化字段自行产生“随机”决策。\n- 可由外部可信随机数生成器完成抽样，再将明确的随机结果交给智能体执行，以减少模型内生规则造成的系统性偏差。\n- 多智能体安全测试应加入共享输入、格式扰动和标识符变换，以揭示群体层面的相关失效。"
notes = "标题：Shared and structured inputs undermine collective random choice by reasoning AI agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09667\n摘要：Random selection is widely used in resource allocation and auditing, making reliable implementation essential for AI-agent systems. Behavioural tests across six reasoning models uncovered threshold and divisibility rules used in identifier-based choices. For threshold-following GPT-6 Sol and Gemini 3.8 Flash, single-agent measurements prospectively predicted correlated participation under shared identifiers and biased participation under distinct identifiers with common timestamp bits. Changing dates, formats and identifier labels revealed when these predictions held. Explicit instructions to randomize independently reduced but did not eliminate shared-input correlation. To test implications for oversight, we asked four models to select customer requests randomly for human review. GPT-6 So"

id = "sapd"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SAPD 将固定示范转化为与推理步骤对齐的分布式监督，在无需生成 rollout 的情况下实现具备竞争力且高效的离策略自蒸馏。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09665"
code = "https://github.com/Miaow-Lab/SAPD"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SAPD 利用参考解答的已知推进过程，为每个推理状态转移匹配针对性的特权指导，而非将完整解答作为无差别上下文。该方法从固定示范中构造与步骤对齐的分布式监督，无需执行成本较高的在线 rollout。在数学推理基准上，SAPD 平均优于监督微调和标签平滑，并可与在策略强化学习及自蒸馏方法竞争。它还能较好保留域外代码能力，相比在策略基线实现约 2 倍的训练循环加速。"
purpose = "- 为缺乏在线 rollout 预算的推理模型后训练提供高效的离策略替代方案。\n- 表明蒸馏效果不仅取决于示范质量，也取决于特权信息能否与当前推理决策精确对齐。\n- 可用于降低数学推理模型自蒸馏的计算成本，同时缓解对代码等域外能力的破坏。\n- 为设计步骤级分布监督、过程监督和训练与推理对齐机制提供参考。"
notes = "标题：SAPD: Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09665\n摘要：On-policy post-training can improve large language models by learning from their own trajectories, but requires costly rollout generation. We ask whether fixed demonstrations can support competitive off-policy learning through better supervision. Our premise is that their usefulness depends not only on the training trajectories, but also on whether supervision provides informative preferences among continuations and connects this guidance to the reasoning decision being learned. We introduce Step-Aligned Privileged Distillation (SAPD), a rollout-free self-distillation method that turns demonstrations into step-aligned distributional supervision. Its key insight is to use the known progression of a reference solution to associate each reasoning transition with targeted privileged guidance, "

summary = "研究发现 RankMe 等谱秩指标在 LLM 后训练退化时可能反向变化，只有经过独立健康数据校准的双侧多通道监测才能可靠诊断故障类型，但其预警并不早于留出集损失。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09647"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究以 Qwen3-0.6B、四种退化模式和三个随机种子开展受控实验，发现数据重复使留出集损失恶化 75%，却令原始及中心化 RankMe 上升，协方差有效秩也接近健康状态的两倍。该现象源于谱分散而非表征坍缩，因此仅监测秩下降会把最差检查点误判为最健康；不同故障与统计量组合的变化方向也不一致，无法仅靠重新校准修复。论文还区分了归一化奇异值的 RankMe 与归一化特征值的协方差有效秩，并指出巨大激活会使后者在原始中间层状态上接近 1。双侧、多通道顺序监测器可在模型分叉后 10～60 步检测全部三类损伤并区分变化方向，但不会早于留出探针损失发出警报，且健康校准数据不足时会产生误报。"
purpose = "- 不应将“表征秩下降”作为 LLM 后训练退化的单向通用判据，应同时监测秩的异常上升与下降。\n- RankMe 与协方差有效秩具有不同定义和敏感性，设计监控系统时需按具体层、训练阶段和故障类型分别验证。\n- 谱统计更适合用于诊断退化机制，而非替代留出集损失充当更早期的预警信号。\n- 监测器的校准与有效性评估应使用相互独立的数据，并保留未参与校准的健康训练轨迹以测量误报率。"
notes = "标题：When Rank Rises as LLMs Degrade\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09647\n摘要：Post-training adapts language models in non-stationary environments. Practitioners monitor representation health with RankMe and related spectral statistics, often assuming that rank falls when representations degrade. We show that this assumption is unsafe for LLM post-training. In a controlled study of Qwen3-0.6B with four degradation modes and three seeds, data duplication worsens held-out loss by 75% relative to healthy while increasing both original and centred RankMe; the latter changes by 13.5 pooled standard deviations. Covariance effective rank rises to nearly twice its healthy value. This failure is spectral dispersion rather than collapse, so a one-sided monitor rates the worst checkpoint as the healthiest. By contrast, a learning-rate misconfiguration lowers centred RankMe and "

content = "CircuitATLAS构建了覆盖疾病、表型、电生理、神经回路、脑区、细胞类型和分子效应因子的系统神经科学知识图谱，包含383万个节点和766万条边，其中531万条关系由大模型抽取。为减少捷径推理，图谱有意排除疾病与基因或蛋白质之间的直接边，并通过来源信息支撑证据溯源。其智能体工作流从可测量的疾病表型逐层推理至回路、细胞和分子干预，同时评估治疗可行性与临床约束。人在回路实验验证发现ATP1A3可作为调控皮层兴奋性的控制点，随后将该靶点推进至结构引导的小分子研究流程。"

notes = "标题：CircuitATLAS: Agentic reasoning over a systems neuroscience knowledge graph for target discovery in circuitopathies\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09643\n摘要：Drug discovery for neurological disease has traditionally centered on the molecules altered by disease. But the molecules that cause pathology are not necessarily the best points from which to reverse it. Here, we ask which otherwise unaltered molecular control points can be engaged to restore pathological neural circuits toward functional states. We present CircuitATLAS, a provenance-grounded systems-neuroscience knowledge graph and agentic framework for target discovery in circuitopathies. It structures literature-derived relationships across diseases, phenotypes, electrophysiology, circuits, brain regions, cell types and molecular effectors, while deliberately excluding direct disease-gene and disease-protein edges to reduce shortcut reasoning. The graph contains 3.83 million nodes and "

summary = "研究发现，Agentic LLM 的工具调用决策由一个具有因果作用的紧凑内部向量介导，而分析型动词会通过抑制提示脚手架建立的工具调用先验，使模型转向直接回答。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09624"
code = "https://github.com/XijieGo/MI4ToolCalling"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究将复杂的智能体提示压缩为最小对比提示对，仅替换请求中的执行型动词与分析型动词，即可稳定翻转工具调用决策。作者构建了覆盖 Python、Java 和 C++ 的 500 组配对提示，其中 300 组用于机制分析，200 组用于留出评估。机制追踪识别出工具调用向量 μ_Δ，该向量对决策既具有因果必要性也具有充分性，并能泛化至原生多轮 τ²-Bench 轨迹和无动词请求。Transcoder 分解表明，提示脚手架首先建立工具调用先验，分析型动词再通过“无需工具”的特征抑制该先验，而后续注意力头与 MLP 特征负责读取这一状态；该机制在 Qwen、Mistral 和 Granite 系列的七个模型中重复出现。"
purpose = "- 为解释智能体模型何时调用工具提供可干预的内部状态变量，而不只依赖行为层面的相关性分析。\n- 可通过监测或操控 μ_Δ，改善工具调用的校准、路由与故障诊断。\n- 提示词中的细微动词差异可能显著改变工具使用行为，设计智能体指令和工具脚手架时应系统测试此类敏感性。\n- 跨模型家族重复出现的抑制机制，为构建通用的工具调用可解释性与安全控制方法提供依据。"
notes = "标题：How Do Agentic LLMs Decide to Call Tools? A Tool-Call Vector Shaped by Suppression\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09624\n摘要：Tool calling, invoking external tools on demand, is central to agentic LLMs, yet the mechanism that decides whether a model calls a tool or responds directly remains poorly understood. Agentic prompts are long and heavily scaffolded, combining role instructions, tool schemas, format templates, and the user's request across hundreds of tokens, creating a noisy, highly entangled context in which no single controllable variable for mechanistic analysis is obvious. To obtain such a variable, we propose a method that converts complex agentic prompts into minimal contrastive pairs in which a single request verb determines the tool-call decision: replacing an execution-verb (e.g., \\textit{write}) with an analysis-verb (e.g., \\textit{discuss}) reliably flips the decision, suggesting it is mediated"

content = "论文将“首因偏差”定义为早期数据分布暴露对后续学习造成的损害，并对其进行量化。小模型容易将有限学习容量低效地分配给早期分布，而充分过参数化的大模型对此更为稳健。在异构数据顺序进入的预训练场景中，这会削弱小模型对后期代码、数学和推理数据的掌握。作者提出 Exposure Therapy（ET）正则化方法，在十亿参数规模以内提升了模型对后期分布及整体能力的表现。"
purpose = "- 说明大模型的部分优势可能源于对训练顺序副作用的稳健性，而不只是更强的特征学习能力。\n- 提醒预训练数据的呈现顺序会影响能力分配，尤其应关注后期高价值数据是否被充分学习。\n- ET 为小模型缓解首因偏差、缩小其与大模型的能力差距提供了简单的训练改进方案。"
notes = "标题：Sequential Pretraining Favors Large Models\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09611\n摘要：Large neural networks often acquire capabilities that small models fail to learn. Does this stem from large models learning more representative features, or from being more robust to unaccounted-for adverse effects introduced during training? We define and quantify one such adverse effect, primacy bias, as the extent to which exposure to early data distributions impairs later learning. We show that small models can allocate learning capacity inefficiently toward early distributions, whereas sufficiently overparameterized models are robust to this effect. This inefficiency is particularly consequential in pretraining, where foundation models often encounter heterogeneous data distributions sequentially rather than jointly. As a result, small foundation models can struggle to learn distribut"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "COPC 协同校正异步 LLM 强化学习中的策略失配与优势陈旧问题，在保持训练加速的同时提升工具增强数学推理和搜索任务的性能与稳定性。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09597"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "异步强化学习通过解耦轨迹生成与参数优化来加速 LLM 后训练，但优化器会使用由旧策略生成的陈旧轨迹。论文指出，仅在 actor 目标中控制重要性比率无法消除偏差，因为优势估计也会受到行为策略后续轨迹失配的影响，即“优势陈旧”。作者从偏差与方差角度证明策略权重误差和优势估计误差存在不可分离的耦合，并提出 COPC，以 token 级比率掩码结合 TD 残差的双侧截断比率加权。COPC 在工具增强数学推理与搜索任务上超过已有异步基线，在最多 64 步策略陈旧下仍保持稳定，并以很小的额外开销维持相对同步 PPO 约 1.7 倍的单步加速。"
purpose = "- 提示异步 RL 的离策略误差不能只从 actor 的策略比率侧处理，还需同步校正回报与优势估计。\n- 为分析策略陈旧提供双通道偏差—方差框架，可用于设计更稳定的异步后训练算法。\n- COPC 的宽容参数区间和较低额外开销，有利于扩展高吞吐量 LLM 强化学习训练。\n- 搜索任务中的后期稳定性结果表明，优势侧校正可能是防止异步训练崩溃的关键。"
notes = "标题：COPC: Coupled Off-Policy Correction for Asynchronous LLM Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09597\n摘要：Asynchronous RL accelerates large language model post-training by decoupling rollout generation from optimization, but trains on stale trajectories. Existing methods primarily correct token-level policy mismatch through importance-ratio control in the actor objective. We show that this \\emph{policy-side correction} alone is insufficient: advantage estimates also inherit mismatch from behavior-policy continuations, which we term \\emph{advantage staleness}. We derive exact bias and variance decompositions for a general two-channel actor update, revealing nonseparable coupling between policy-weight and advantage-estimation errors: their interaction induces multiplicative bias terms, while squared policy weights amplify advantage uncertainty in gradient variance. This motivates the hypothesis "

notes = "标题：Collaborative Reasoning Distillation via Cross-Feedback and Coherent Curation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09587\n摘要：Reasoning capabilities are critical for advancing Large Language Models, yet current approaches either require massive computational budgets or struggle to effectively distill reasoning to smaller models. Standard distillation methods rely on outcome-based rewards, failing to distinguish between sound reasoning and lucky guesses. We propose Collaborative Reasoning Distillation (CRD), a framework that enhances reasoning in compact models through three innovations: (1) interactive cross-feedback where teachers iteratively critique each other's reasoning, (2) fine-grained step-wise quality assessment capturing logical validity independent of final answers, and (3) coherence-aware step stitching that synthesizes complementary strengths. Students are trained via Reasoning Quality Optimization ("

summary = "研究表明，仅凭结果正确性无法判断智能体日志取证是否可靠，必须保存引用标识符与原始记录之间的显式绑定，才能区分有证据支持的发现与正确猜测。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09581"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究基于 AgentDojo Banking 的已保存执行记录构造了 64 个可机械核验案例，考察两个 LLM 在不同证据可见性及标识符绑定条件下重建来源关系的能力。评测分别衡量完整记录一致性、证据支撑的发现、合理弃答和无依据断言，而非只检查最终答案是否正确。在保留原始标识符但缺少绑定表时，Sonnet 虽能定位所有字面来源，却在 28 个需要缺失绑定关系的案例中有 26 个作出无依据的引用来源断言，其中 22 个仍与完整参考答案一致。加入显式绑定后，两个模型的有据重建能力均得到改善，而单纯重命名标识符没有稳定效果；确定性的同数据包比较器则能始终正确解决任务或选择弃答。"
purpose = "- 智能体日志应同时保存工具记录、局部引用标识符及其到原始记录的显式映射，避免证据链断裂。\n- 评测日志取证与轨迹审计系统时，应将事实一致性和证据可支持性拆分为独立指标。\n- 对缺少关键绑定关系的案例，应奖励合理弃答，并将“答案正确但证据不足”计为无依据断言。\n- 在边界明确的审计任务中，可优先采用确定性比较器，并让 LLM 负责解释或处理非结构化证据。"
notes = "标题：Correct Answers, Unsupported Findings: Evidence Binding in Forensic Reconstruction of LLM Agent Logs\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09581\n摘要：Forensic reconstruction of LLM-agent actions requires not only recovering the correct value, but establishing which preserved record supports that finding. Tool logs, generated explanations, and local citation identifiers capture different parts of this evidence, yet a citation identifier does not establish a source unless its binding to a record is preserved. We audit this distinction using 64 mechanically checkable cases from saved AgentDojo Banking executions. Two LLM readers reconstruct source relationships under controlled variations in visible evidence and identifier-to-record bindings. We separately evaluate complete-record agreement, evidence-grounded findings, justified abstention, and unsupported assertions. With original identifiers and no binding table, Sonnet recovered every l"

summary = "研究发现，LLM 通过注意力头与 MLP 神经元抑制原答案并提升同类别候选来处理否定，但抑制不足和答案偏置会导致其在 37%—71% 的情况下仍重复原答案。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09571"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在一个否定理解基准上评估近期的开源与闭源 LLM，发现模型面对否定问题时，有 37%—71% 的案例会重复肯定问题的原答案。机制分析表明，专门化注意力头和 MLP 神经元共同抑制原答案的检索，同时提升答案类别内某个偏好的替代候选。该机制不同于人类利用原答案判断“应排除什么”的处理方式，抑制强度不足或候选偏置过强都会造成否定失败。作者进一步提出一种训练目标，使模型对置信度越高的原预测产生越大的答案偏好转移，并以低于标准微调的通用能力损失减少否定错误。"
purpose = "- 揭示 LLM 否定理解失败并非单纯缺少语言知识，而与内部答案抑制机制及候选偏置直接相关。\n- 可将否定一致性纳入模型可靠性与逻辑推理评测，重点检测模型是否机械重复原答案。\n- 展示机制分析指导训练目标设计的路径，为针对特定能力缺陷开展低副作用干预提供参考。\n- 可借鉴“按原预测置信度调整偏好转移幅度”的方法，改进否定、反事实和排除式问答训练。"
notes = "标题：How Do LLMs Change Predictions Under Negation?\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09571\n摘要：Negation is an essential feature of human language, yet large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable in processing it. We evaluate recent open-source and closed-source LLMs on our negation benchmark and find that, in 37-71% of cases, they repeat the same answer under negation (e.g., \"Madrid\" for \"What is not the capital of Spain?\"). To understand and address this brittleness, we mechanistically examine how models operate under negation. Our main finding is that specialized attention heads and MLP neurons jointly implement negation by (1) suppressing retrieval of the original answer (e.g., \"Madrid\") while (2) promoting a favored candidate within the answer category (e.g., \"Paris\"). This contrasts with accounts of human negation processing, in which information about the original answer he"

score = 2

summary = "World Potential Model 利用预训练模型已有的世界知识判断长程智能体的实际任务进展，并将其转化为步骤级监督信号以改进策略优化。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09560"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文提出 World Potential Model（WPM），将预训练模型用作目标条件化评估器，识别智能体上下文中相对于任务目标的实际进展。实验表明，无需针对特定任务微调评估器，现成预训练模型便能在 ALFWorld 和 ScienceWorld 中显著优于随机水平地恢复进展结构。研究进一步利用任务里程碑校准进展判断，得到标量世界势能，并以相邻时刻的势能差提供过程敏感的步骤级信用分配。在匹配条件下，WPM 引导的策略优化在所有评测配置中均优于仅依赖最终结果奖励的 GRPO。"
purpose = "- 为终局奖励稀疏的长程智能体任务提供可复用的中间过程监督。\n- 展示预训练世界知识可直接承担进展评估功能，减少为每项任务单独训练价值函数或过程奖励模型的需求。\n- 可将世界势能的时间差用作步骤级奖励，改善强化学习中的信用分配与停滞识别。\n- 为构建跨任务泛化的进展评估器和长程智能体训练机制提供新思路。"
notes = "标题：World Potential Model: Pretrained World Knowledge as Progress Potentials\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09560\n摘要：Long-horizon language agents often receive supervision only from terminal task outcomes, leaving little signal for distinguishing productive intermediate behavior from stagnation or even regression. Rather than learning a separate value function or process reward model for every task, we ask whether pretrained models can recognize task progress from their existing world knowledge. We formalize this capability with a World Potential Model (WPM), a goal-conditioned evaluator of task-relative realized progress in agent contexts. In ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, off-the-shelf pretrained models substantially outperform chance at recovering realized-progress structure without task-specific evaluator fine-tuning. We further anchor these progress judgments to task-specific milestones to obtain scalar"

score = 2

summary = "研究系统揭示了 LLM 评判器评估实体对齐结果时的标签锚定偏差，并证明隐藏系统决策标签可显著恢复其判别能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09554"
code = "https://github.com/vaibhavalakshmiravideshik/llm-as-a-judge-entity-alignment"

link = ""
content = "研究对三种前沿模型、三个数据集和四套实体对齐系统进行了系统性元评估，并采用扰动偏差诊断与反事实标签翻转测试。结果发现，当系统决策标签对评判器可见时，会产生“锚定偏差”，导致评判器的判别方向反转或能力显著下降。移除标签后，评判器在名称特征明显的数据集上达到 0.93–1.00 的 J-ROC-AUC，在生物医学实体对上也恢复至 0.93–0.95。反事实实验与双人盲评进一步确认了该因果机制，同时发现能力更强的模型反而可能对标签更加敏感。研究还发布了包含 1.5 万对实体的 MeSH–SNOMED CT 生物医学基准及可复现的评判器审计框架。"
purpose = "- 提醒实体对齐等结构化预测任务不要直接向 LLM 评判器暴露待评系统的决策标签。\n- 可将标签隐藏、反事实翻转和扰动测试纳入 LLM-as-a-judge 的标准审计流程。\n- 表明更强的评判模型并不必然更可靠，模型选型时应单独测量标签敏感性与判别稳定性。\n- 新发布的生物医学实体对齐数据和审计框架可用于知识图谱系统的低成本规模化评估。"
notes = "标题：Reliability of LLM Judges for Evaluating Entity Alignment\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09554\n摘要：Entity Alignment (EA) identifies equivalent entities across knowledge graphs and is critical for knowledge base integration and ontology merging. Evaluating EA systems at scale requires expensive expert annotation, making systematic assessment across diverse domains practically infeasible. LLM-as-judge evaluation offers a potentially scalable alternative, yet its reliability for structured prediction tasks like EA remains unstudied. We present the first systematic benchmarking study across three frontier models, three datasets, and four EA systems, using perturbation bias diagnostics, meta-evaluation across all dataset-judge-prompt combinations, and counterfactual label-flip tests. We identify anchor bias, a failure mode in which judges invert discrimination when the system's decision labe"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究揭示小样本校准下思维链验证器可能通过频繁弃权获得形式有效却实用失真的认证，并提出覆盖率更高的固定序列认证方法。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09541"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文基于7个开放模型、5种验证器信号和3.7万条已评分思维链，考察仅有数十至数百个标注问题时的分布无关选择性保证。核心发现是“以弃权维持有效性”：当证书触发概率为 $P_{\\rm fire}$ 时，已签发证书的失败概率最多只能由 $\\delta/P_{\\rm fire}$ 约束，因此极少触发的证书可能在每次实际使用时都出错。实验中标准证书总体校准失败率不超过0.3%，但在其真正触发的样本中失败率最高可达69%；残差流探针虽不可解释，却可获得可读信号2至3倍的覆盖率。作者提出无需单调性假设的 floor-started fixed-sequence certificate，在所有模型—信号组合上均优于 Bonferroni 证书，并将非平凡目标下的覆盖率从0.05提高至0.16。论文还发现，在基准分布漂移及针对验证器的 best-of-$n$ 选择下，已接受轨迹的错误率会超过目标，而总体经验失败频率仍可能因弃权机制保持在 $\\delta$ 以下。"
purpose = "- 提醒部署思维链验证器时不能只报告AUC或总体认证有效性，还应同时披露触发率、条件失败率和覆盖率。\n- 说明小校准预算下，形式化有效保证可能被高弃权率“架空”，需要检查证书是否具有实际使用价值。\n- 可将固定序列认证方法用于改进低预算场景的选择性预测与过程验证，但仍需关注认证覆盖率下限。\n- 面对分布漂移或 best-of-$n$ 对抗性筛选，应重新校准并开展部署后监测，不能依赖静态证书保证。"
notes = "标题：Certified by Abstention: Distribution-Free Guarantees for Chain-of-Thought Verifiers at Small Calibration Budgets\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09541\n摘要：Signals that predict whether a chain-of-thought (CoT) trace is correct are compared by AUC, but deploying one requires a threshold with a guarantee. We ask what distribution-free selective guarantees deliver for CoT verifiers at realistic calibration budgets of tens to a few hundred labelled problems, using seven open models, five verifier signals and 37,000 graded traces. The central observation is validity by abstention: an $(α,δ)$-valid procedure that issues a certificate with probability $P_{\\rm fire}$ bounds the failure probability of an issued certificate only by $δ/P_{\\rm fire}$, so a certificate that rarely fires can be valid and wrong every time it is used. In a simulation with known risk the standard certificate fails in at most 0.3% of calibration draws but in up to 69% of those"

content = "研究提出一种“先校准、后融合”的框架，通过合成序数参考空间对齐原本不可直接比较的子集评分器输出。参考空间由表征潜在概念的有序校准特征构建，不依赖训练样本的经验分布。该方法在三个基准数据集上持续优于未经校准的平均融合，并在主要指标的点估计上超过最佳单一评分器。实验还表明，减少每个特征的校准层级可在大幅降低计算成本的同时逼近高分辨率校准效果。"

notes = "标题：Align Before You Combine: Reference Space Calibration for Supervision Without Ground Truth\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09525\n摘要：We introduce a calibration-first framework that produces supervision scores without access to ground-truth labels or a shared annotation space. Our framework aligns subset-specific scorers using a synthetic ordinal reference space before fusion. This reference space is constructed from ordered calibration features that represent the latent concept, providing a common scale on which otherwise incomparable scorer outputs can be aligned. Because our calibration procedure uses the reference space rather than training samples, it is independent of the training set's empirical distribution. Across three benchmark datasets, our framework consistently outperforms uncalibrated averaging and achieves higher primary-metric point estimates on the evaluation metrics than the best individual scorer. content = "Goldsmith将结构化标注定义视为可训练的文本对象，在同一批专家标注的校准样本上执行候选定义并计算结构化损失。智能体式标注脚手架负责输出模式、格式、检索、修复、评判及人工审核，使定义优化与外围执行机制相互解耦。LLM编辑器针对损失最高的失败案例生成“文本梯度”式修订，并仅在实测损失下降时接受更新。在统一评测协议下，该方法优于直接重写、OPRO、APE和PromptBreeder，并可改善类型化跨度、关系与事件论元等下游标注任务。"

notes = "标题：Goldsmith: Gold-Loss-Guided Definition Optimization with an Agentic Annotation Harness\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09489\n摘要：Many annotation projects begin before experts have a stable guideline or enough labels to train a task-specific model. We present Goldsmith, an agentic pipeline that turns a small gold set---expert-annotated calibration examples representing the intended task boundaries---into a reusable structured annotation definition. Goldsmith treats this definition as a trainable textual object. Candidate definitions are run on the same gold examples and scored with an executable structured loss, while the output schema, formatting, retrieval, repair, judging, and human review remain in an external harness. A large language model (LLM) editor converts the highest-loss failures into textual-gradient revisions, which are accepted only when the measured loss decreases. In prompt-optimization comparisons,"

summary = "Secure-CUA 通过在接触不可信界面内容前提交逐动作事务，并隔离内容查询与目标定位，为计算机使用智能体提供可形式化验证的安全执行机制。"

content = "论文形式化定义了计算机使用智能体在动作决策和 GUI 命令执行两方面的安全要求，并证明逐步满足这些要求可保护完整执行轨迹。Secure-CUA 的核心是逐动作生成显式的“动作事务”，预先限定对不可信内容的查询及查询结果的允许用途。系统遮蔽不可信界面区域，使用隔离的查询模型获取必要信息，再依据遮蔽后的界面定位预期操作目标，从而降低恶意指令和误导性视觉线索的影响。在 400 个 WebArena 任务、3 个前沿模型和 5 个随机种子形成的 6000 条轨迹上，其平均成功率为 53.55%，接近 Vanilla-CUA 的 55.12%，并显著高于 CaMeL-CUA 的 13.17%。"
purpose = "- 为桌面、移动端和浏览器智能体提供兼顾决策安全与命令执行安全的形式化设计范式。\n- 通过动作事务约束不可信内容的信息流，可用于防御提示注入、视觉诱导和界面目标重定向攻击。\n- 实验表明，逐步生成安全约束可在保持较高任务效用的同时实现可信执行，适合用于高风险 GUI 自动化系统。"
notes = "标题：Secure-CUA: Controlling Untrusted Influence in Computer-Use Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09469\n摘要：Computer-use agents (CUAs) perform tasks across applications (such as desktops, mobile apps, and web browsers) by observing graphical interfaces and issuing commands such as clicks and keystrokes. These interfaces combine trusted controls and content with untrusted content needed for legitimate tasks. An adversary controlling this untrusted content can embed instructions or misleading visual cues to change the agent's intended action or redirect its commands to the wrong interface target. We formalize security requirements for both the agent's decisions and their execution through GUI commands. In an ideal execution model, we show that enforcing both requirements at each step protects execution traces. We instantiate this model in Secure-CUA, our system for secure CUA execution. Its key id"

summary = "论文提出“自我推介”机制，让证明方法在排序前说明目标、操作、前提和遗留义务，从而提升形式化证明中的适用方法召回率。"

content = "论文将形式化证明中的方法检索重构为“适用性感知的方法选择”，强调语义相关并不等同于方法可用于当前定理。作者整理了 Putnam 2000—2014 中的 82 种可复用方法，并以 Method Contracts 描述其适用条件、Mathlib 锚点、已检查脚手架及预期证明义务。系统通过一次批量模型调用，让每个候选方法针对当前问题生成包含目标、行动和前提的自荐提案，再降权模糊或缺乏依据的候选。在 Putnam 2015—2025 上，该方法达到 95.0% hit@5，超过最强重排序基线的 84.2%；在 IMO ProofBench 上达到 91.7%，高于基线的 88.3%。"
purpose = "- 将证明方法表示为带前提、动作和证明义务的显式契约，可提高自动定理证明系统的方法选择可解释性。\n- 在候选排序前生成问题特定的适用性提案，有助于弥补词法或向量相似度无法区分“相关”与“可执行”的缺陷。\n- Method Contracts 可作为连接自然语言数学策略与 Mathlib 可检查证明脚手架的中间层。\n- 该机制适合用于构建更可靠的证明搜索器、方法检索器和可审计的数学推理智能体。"
notes = "标题：Let the Library Speak: Self-Advertised Method Selection for Formal Proving\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09401\n摘要：LLM-based formal provers can retrieve relevant lemmas and prior proofs, but relevance alone does not say whether a mathematical method can be used on the current theorem. A method has prerequisites, a target, an intended action, and obligations that its use leaves to prove. Methods that look equally related to a theorem may therefore differ substantially in whether they offer a plausible next step. We formulate this as an applicability-aware method-selection problem and introduce self-advertisement: before candidates are ranked, a model generates a problem-specific proposal for each one, stating what part of the goal it targets, what action it would take, and what conditions that action requires. We organize 82 reusable methods from Putnam 2000-2014 as Method Contracts, which pair applicab"

content = "研究在两个包含 1,940 和 5,000 条记录的私有客户反馈语料上构建六棵分类树，包括生产参考体系及相同智能体脚手架的重复生成结果。所有分类树均通过命名、父子关系和同级区分等通用检查，生成树甚至在产品覆盖度检查中更优。整树分析却发现，生成树至少 97.7% 的叶节点名称只是复述祖先名称，部分结果还将约四分之三的记录同时划入多个顶层类别。论文提出结构判别指标与团队可分区指标，用于判断树形结构是否真正源于数据，以及各分支能否形成可由不同团队负责的清晰反馈分区。"

notes = "标题：From Plausible Hierarchies to Useful Taxonomies: Evaluating Agentic Harnesses on Customer Feedback\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09377\n摘要：Taxonomies are the symbolic representations through which AI systems organize evidence, aggregate patterns, and answer questions over large document collections. We ask a more operational question: when is a generated hierarchy actually useful as a production taxonomy? We build six taxonomies over two proprietary feedback corpora (1,940 and 5,000 records): for each corpus, a production reference and two "

content = "DUDA-Bench 将数据驱动的城市诊断建模为多阶段智能体工作流，覆盖问题识别、区域定位和成因分析等环节。基准包含 86 个原子任务与 22 个工作流任务，基于 12 个城市、5 类城市问题的多模态数据构建。对 7 个骨干模型和 5 个智能体系统的评测表明，孤立分析能力与端到端诊断能力之间存在显著差距，且智能体系统带来的收益依赖具体骨干模型。轨迹分析发现，未解决的证据缺口会跨阶段传播，而成功恢复通常依赖利用反馈修正假设与行动。"
purpose = "- 为评估智能体执行真实世界、多阶段分析任务提供分层且过程感知的基准。\n- 提示智能体优化不能只关注单项分析能力，还需加强自适应规划、跨阶段证据整合与结果验证。\n- 可借鉴其原子任务与工作流任务结合的设计，将专家流程转化为可审计、可诊断的端到端评测。"
notes = "标题：DUDA-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Multimodal Data-Driven Urban Diagnosis\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09374\n摘要：Urban diagnosis integrates heterogeneous observations to identify urban problems, localize affected areas, and investigate contributing factors, informing evidence-based urban planning and management. However, its reliance on labor-intensive, case-specific expert workflows limits scalability and reuse, motivating the exploration of agent-based execution. To evaluate this capability, we introduce DUDA-Bench, a hierarchical and interactive benchmark that formalizes data-driven urban diagnosis as a multi-stage agent workflow. It comprises 86 atomic and 22 workflow tasks spanning four analytical stages, grounded in multimodal data from 12 cities covering five urban problem types. Evaluations of seven backbone models and five agent systems reveal a substantial gap between isolated analytical co"

summary = "通过零真实方言语音的伪方言合成与中间标准文本预测，提升语音语言模型在日语、德语和中文方言翻译任务上的鲁棒性。"

content = "研究使用 LLM 生成方言文本，再由标准语言 TTS 模型将其转换为伪方言语音，无需任何真实方言语音资源。训练时加入中间标准文本预测，将其作为语义归一化步骤，以缩小方言语音与下游任务语义之间的差距。实验覆盖日语、德语和中文方言到英语的语音翻译，伪方言增强将日语得分从 25.38 提升至 26.24、德语从 31.57 提升至 32.47。结合中间标准文本预测后，日语得分进一步达到 28.26，中文得分由 11.67 提升至 16.37。"
purpose = "- 为低资源方言建模提供不依赖真实方言语音的可扩展数据增强方案。\n- 可利用现有 LLM 与标准语言 TTS，低成本扩展多语言、多方言语音训练数据。\n- 中间标准文本预测可作为语义归一化接口，改善方言表征向跨语言下游任务的迁移。"
notes = "标题：Dialect-Robust Speech Language Models with Synthetic Pseudo-Dialect Augmentation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09321\n摘要：Speech Language Model (SLM) performance often degrades on dialects due to data scarcity. Conventional text-to-speech (TTS) augmentation struggles to cover diverse dialects as it requires a certain amount of real dialect speech. We propose synthesizing pseudo-dialect speech by converting LLM-generated dialect text via a standard-language TTS model, requiring zero real dialect speech. Additionally, we introduce intermediate standard-text prediction during training, acting as semantic normalization for downstream tasks. We evaluate dialect understanding via dialect-to-English speech translation across Japanese, German, and Chinese dialects. Compared to synthetic standard speech baselines, pseudo-dialect augmentation improves scores for Japanese (from 25.38 to 26.24) and German (from 31.57 to "


[[items]]
id = "kuration-sdk-virtual2real"
title = "Kuration SDK：以数据策展弥合世界模型的 Virtual2Real 鸿沟"
subtopic = "数据策展"

score = 2
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究发现 FVD、LPIPS、JEDi 等指标无法可靠反映动作条件世界模型的实际可玩性，并提出 Kuration SDK 通过训练前的数据诊断与策展定位 Virtual2Real 鸿沟的根因。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09305"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- 研究在 Counter-Strike 游戏数据上训练和评估扩散世界模型，发现定性可玩性与 FVD、LPIPS、JEDi 等视觉质量指标并不一致，并将这一现象称为 Virtual2Real 鸿沟。\n- 作者主张，在可靠且通用的世界模型基准尚未成熟时，应在训练开始前分析原始游戏数据的多种诊断属性，以数据策展提供更稳健的质量信号。\n- 论文提出多种策展策略及面向物理人工智能数据的通用工具包 Kuration SDK，可用于检查动作、状态和场景等数据特征。\n- 在具体案例中，该工具定位了两个数据分布和常用指标近似、但实际交互表现显著不同的世界模型之间的根因，有助于构建样本效率更高的训练集。"
purpose = "- 提醒世界模型开发者不要仅凭 FVD、LPIPS 等视觉相似度指标判断模型是否具备可交互性或物理一致性。\n- 可将训练前的数据诊断和策展纳入世界模型开发流程，提前发现影响动作响应与可玩性的隐性数据问题。\n- 为动作条件世界模型建立覆盖动作语义、状态分布和物理行为的多维数据质量分析体系。\n- 有助于减少低价值训练样本与重复实验，提高训练数据的样本效率并辅助失效根因分析。"
notes = "标题：Kuration SDK: Addressing the Virtual2Real Gap via Data Curation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09305\n摘要：Benchmarks for measuring the quality of action-conditioned world models are still evolving and shifting away from visual similarity-based metrics to action-semantic and physically-grounded metrics. However, for domain and task-agnostic action-conditioned world model training, existing benchmarks provide a limited signal. By training and evaluating diffusion world models on CounterStrike gameplay data, we confirm that qualitative playability does not correspond with metrics such as FVD, LPIPS, and JEDi. We term this the Virtual2Real gap. We posit that, in lieu of reliable benchmarks, curating raw gameplay data and measuring a variety of diagnostic properties provides a more robust signal to bridge the gap, before the training even begins. We present several curation strategies and a general"

summary = "研究通过生成式基于智能体建模模拟评测机构、模型提供商与用户之间的策略互动，揭示公开基准转向私有留出基准对评分有效性和用户满意度的差异化影响。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09296"
code = ""

content = "论文将 AI 评测视为由模型提供商、用户、资助者和监管者共同塑造的动态生态系统，而非孤立的基准设计问题。作者构建了结合规则化市场动态与 LLM 策略参与者的生成式基于智能体建模（GABM）架构，并以推理、编程、知识、安全、沟通和智能体能力六维向量描述基准、用户需求与提供商能力。案例研究表明，私有留出基准通常能缩小基准分数与用户满意度之间的差距，但在评分权重发生特定偏移时也可能扩大差距。研究还依据经典验证与确认框架及 GABM 专用证据标准进行压力测试，将该模拟系统定位为分析评测和政策选择影响的假设生成沙箱。"
purpose = "- 提醒基准设计者将市场激励、信息不对称和参与者策略行为纳入评测有效性分析。\n- 可用于比较公开测试集、私有留出集及不同能力权重配置的系统性后果。\n- 为监管者和评测机构提供政策沙箱，在实际部署前模拟评测规则对模型供给与用户效用的影响。\n- 展示 GABM 在评测机制审计、动态基准设计和多方利益相关者分析中的应用潜力。"
notes = "标题：The AI Evaluation Ecosystem\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09296\n摘要：AI evaluation shapes the decisions of model providers, users, funders, and regulators. We argue that designing valid benchmarks requires contextualizing design choices in the dynamics of this ecosystem of actors. We develop a simulation architecture that combines rule-based market dynamics with LLM-driven strategic actors, building on advances in Generative Agent-Based Modeling (GABM). We model benchmarks, consumer needs, and provider capabilities as vectors over a six-dimensional capability space (reasoning, coding, knowledge, safety, communication, agentic), with structural information partitions across actors. As a case study, we apply this stylized simulation to explore benchmark holdout design. We find that moving from public benchmarks to private holdout benchmarks shrinks the gap be"
